| `encoding` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `mono8`, `mono16`, `rgb8`, `bgr8`, `rgba8`, `bgra8`, `rgb16`, `bgr16`, `rgba16`, `bgra16`, `8UC1`, `16UC1`, `16SC1`, `32SC1`, `32FC1`, `64FC1` Auto preserves native sample depth and tagged color order; explicit RGB/BGR can swap channels. |
| `frame_id` | `""` | Optional header frame override, with no implied physical transform or calibration. |
| `stamp_mode` | `"preserve"` | `preserve`, `zero`, `node_clock` Preserve incoming ROS header (otherwise zero), clear it, or stamp using the ROS node clock. |

## Decode ROS 2 pose / transform (`industrial.ros2_pose_decode`)

Industrial integration / ROS 2

Inputs: `message` (data).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `message_kind` | `"transform_stamped"` | `transform_stamped`, `pose_stamped` TransformStamped includes child_frame_id; PoseStamped needs the configured child frame. |
| `child_frame` | `"object"` | PoseStamped only: explicit object/tool frame whose pose is expressed in header.frame_id. |
| `expected_parent` | `""` | Optional required header.frame_id, checked before inversion. |
| `expected_child` | `""` | Optional required child frame, checked before inversion. |
| `invert` | `false` | Invert the rigid transform and swap source/target frames. |
| `units` | `"m"` | `m`, `mm` ROS translations are meters; optionally convert the app pose to millimeters. |
| `quaternion_policy` | `"require_unit"` | `require_unit`, `normalize` Reject nonunit quaternions or explicitly normalize finite nonzero values. |
| `quaternion_tolerance` | `1e-06` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-12 and 0.01. Allowed quaternion norm error in Require unit mode. |

## Encode ROS 2 pose / transform (`industrial.ros2_pose_encode`)

Industrial integration / ROS 2

Inputs: `pose` (data).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `message_kind` | `"transform_stamped"` | `transform_stamped`, `pose_stamped` TransformStamped includes child_frame_id; PoseStamped needs the configured child frame. |
| `invert` | `false` | Invert the rigid transform and swap source/target frames. |
| `stamp_mode` | `"preserve"` | `preserve`, `zero` Keep a ROS acquisition stamp (otherwise zero) or clear it. Header frame is the output parent. |

## Rectify ROS 2 intensity image (`industrial.ros2_rectify_image`)

Industrial integration / ROS 2

Inputs: `image` (image), `camera_info` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Raw intensity image on the calibrated ROS grid. Physical/axial depth is not an intensity image.

Input `camera_info` — Complete CameraInfo, or decoded/bound CameraInfo camera model. R/P select output geometry.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `camera_info` | `{}` | Fallback complete CameraInfo JSON when the input is unwired. |
| `rectified_frame` | `""` | Empty derives <source>_rectified. Nonidentity R needs a distinct optical frame. |
| `width` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 16384. Both dimensions zero keep the selected binned rectified grid; otherwise resize it. |
| `height` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 16384. Both dimensions must be zero or positive. Output is bounded to 4M pixels/16 MiB. |
| `rectified_grid` | `"camera_info_roi"` | `camera_info_roi`, `full_sensor`, `custom_roi` Select the CameraInfo ROI (only when do_rectify is false), full rectified sensor canvas, or an explicit ROI in full-resolution rectified pixels. |
| `rectified_roi` | `[0, 0, 0, 0]` | Custom grid: [x,y,width,height] in full-resolution rectified coordinates. Offsets are nonnegative integers; dimensions are positive and fit the sensor. |
| `interpolation` | `"linear"` | `nearest`, `linear`, `cubic`, `lanczos4` OpenCV remap sampling; nearest preserves discrete labels. |
| `border` | `"constant"` | `constant`, `replicate`, `reflect`, `reflect_101`, `reflect_edge` Sampling outside source pixels; this does not extend the validity mask. |
| `border_value` | `0` | One to four finite channel values, in the image's existing channel order. |
| `header_mode` | `"require"` | `require`, `zero_if_missing` Require an image header, or explicitly use zero time if absent. Calibration time is never substituted. |
| `scalar_semantics` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `intensity` Auto rejects ambiguous float/16UC1 planes. Intensity explicitly declares brightness or labels; physical DepthArray input is always rejected. |
| `require_matching_ticket` | `false` | Require image and wired calibration to have the same acquisition ticket. |

## Validate registered ROS RGB-D (`industrial.ros2_rgbd`)

Industrial integration / ROS 2

Inputs: `group` (any).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `use_color` | `true` | Require a registered color image; otherwise only depth and calibration are required. |
| `color_label` | `"input_1"` | Color member label in the completed coordinator FlowGroup. |
| `depth_label` | `"input_2"` | Depth member label; use input_1 for a two-input depth/calibration coordinator. |
| `camera_label` | `"input_3"` | Decoded CameraInfo model member; use input_2 in depth-only groups. |
| `calibration_timing` | `"per_acquisition"` | `per_acquisition`, `bound_static` Per acquisition checks the original CameraInfo stamp. Bound static requires an explicit Cache ROS 2 CameraInfo binding to this image ticket and grid. |
| `max_skew_ms` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1000. Maximum header stamp spread. Zero requires exact integer nanosecond equality. |
| `allow_zero_stamps` | `false` | Explicit offline/unknown-time mode. Zero stamps never verify acquisition timing. |
| `uint16_m_per_sample` | `0.001` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-09 and 1000. Explicit meters per uint16 depth sample, e.g. 0.001 for millimeters. |
| `float_depth_units` | `"m"` | `m`, `mm` Declared 32FC1/64FC1 depth units. Output is physical meters. |
| `display_max_m` | `5.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+06. Depth preview upper display bound in meters; does not clip measured values. |

## ROS 2 run identity (`industrial.ros2_run`)

Industrial integration / ROS 2

Inputs: `reset` (boolean, optional).

Input `reset` — A rising true edge advances generation once; holding true does not repeat reset.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `dataset_id` | `"inspection"` | Dataset/job identity shared by related acquisitions. |
| `run_id` | `""` | Optional run label; empty generates a UUID. Runtime session nonce is always fresh after flow reset/reopen. |

## Validate ROS 2 run identity (`industrial.ros2_run_guard`)

Industrial integration / ROS 2

Inputs: `value` (any), `context` (data).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `identity_source` | `"payload"` | `payload`, `envelope` Check full local acquisition payloads or an explicit external JSON run-token field. |
| `run_field` | `"run_id"` | Envelope mode: dotted field containing the current Run identity run_token; no acquisition tag is invented. |

## Lookup ROS 2 transform at acquisition (`industrial.ros2_tf`)

Industrial integration / ROS 2

Inputs: `value` (any), `context` (data, optional).

Input `value` — Ticketed image/data/pose carrying a positive ROS acquisition header stamp.

Input `context` — Optional current run identity: reject stale acquisition payloads before lookup.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `enabled` | `false` | Start a persistent read-only TF listener. Disabled blocks never start ROS. |
| `domain_id` | `71` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 232. DDS domain shared with the transform/clock publishers. |
| `namespace` | `"/opencv_dataflow"` | Namespace for the private TF worker node. |
| `discovery_range` | `"localhost"` | `localhost`, `subnet` Localhost by default; subnet opts in to network discovery. |
| `tf_topic` | `"/tf"` | Dynamic TransformListener topic; standard /tf or a private test topic. |
| `tf_static_topic` | `"/tf_static"` | Static TransformListener topic using standard transient-local TF QoS. |
| `clock_topic` | `"/clock"` | Node clock topic when Use sim time is enabled. |
| `source_frame` | `""` | Empty uses the acquisition header frame. Pose maps this source into Target frame. |
| `target_frame` | `"base_link"` | Explicit destination/parent frame, e.g. base_link. |
| `timeout_seconds` | `0.25` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 30. Maximum wall-clock wait for the exact requested transform; no latest fallback. |
| `startup_timeout_seconds` | `30.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 60. Bounded startup of the separate configured ROS Python worker. |
| `cache_seconds` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 120. TF2 time cache. Start the listener before collecting dynamic acquisitions. |
| `use_sim_time` | `false` | Use the selected ROS clock; observed backward jumps latch until reset/new context. |
| `max_age_seconds` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 3600. Acquisition age against the node clock. Zero explicitly disables age checking for historical queries. |
| `future_tolerance_seconds` | `0.05` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 30. Allowed lead over node time when age checking is enabled. |
| `units` | `"m"` | `m`, `mm` TF translations are meters; optionally convert the app pose to millimeters. |
| `unavailable` | `"error"` | `error`, `wait` Report unavailable exact-time TF as an error or wait without an output event. |

## Load animation frames (`input.animation_file`)

Input / Files

Ticket terminals: optional `ticket` data input and `ticket` data output.
Ticket mode and fixed value are common inspector controls.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `path` | `""` |  |
| `path_base` | `"project"` | `project`, `working_directory` |
| `start_frame` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 32766. |
| `frame_count` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 64. Zero reads the remaining frames, subject to the 64-frame budget. A positive value explicitly selects a bounded subset. |
| `preview_frame` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 63. Zero-based frame within the selected subset shown by Image. |
| `color_format` | `"unchanged"` | `unchanged`, `bgr`, `rgb`, `bgra`, `rgba`, `gray`, `hsv`, `lab` |
| `pixel_depth` | `"unchanged"` | `unchanged`, `uint8`, `uint16`, `float32` |
| `maximum_pixels` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Maximum total selected pixels before native decoding; not per frame. |

## Camera (`input.camera`)

Input / Cameras

Ticket terminals: optional `ticket` data input and `ticket` data output.
Ticket mode and fixed value are common inspector controls.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `device` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 65535. Local capture-device index. Opening happens only on execution. |
| `backend` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `dshow`, `msmf`, `v4l2`, `gstreamer`, `ffmpeg`, `avfoundation` Native capture backend; Auto lets OpenCV choose. Build-specific availability. |
| `width` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 16384. Requested decoded width; zero leaves the backend unchanged. |
| `height` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 16384. Requested decoded height; zero leaves the backend unchanged. |
| `fps` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 100000. Requested backend frame rate; zero leaves it unchanged. Not measured cadence. |
| `buffer_size` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 16384. Requested backend buffer size; zero leaves it unchanged. |
| `fourcc` | `""` | Optional four-character transport/codec request, including meaningful spaces. |
| `auto_exposure_mode` | `"unchanged"` | `unchanged`, `backend_value` Apply a raw backend auto-exposure value, or leave its state unchanged. |
| `auto_exposure_value` | `0.75` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+06 and 1e+06. Backend-specific auto-exposure encoding; no portable 0/1 mapping. |
| `exposure_mode` | `"unchanged"` | `unchanged`, `backend_value` Apply Exposure value in native backend units, or leave it unchanged. |
| `exposure_value` | `-6.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+06 and 1e+06. Backend units can be negative, steps or time; not universally milliseconds. |
| `gain_mode` | `"unchanged"` | `unchanged`, `backend_value` Apply Gain value in native backend units, or leave it unchanged. |
| `gain_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+06 and 1e+06. Native gain request; zero is a real request when its mode is enabled. |
| `focus_mode` | `"unchanged"` | `unchanged`, `auto`, `manual` Auto writes autofocus=1; Manual writes autofocus=0 then Focus value. |
| `focus_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+06 and 1e+06. Manual focus in backend units; zero is permitted. |
| `white_balance_mode` | `"unchanged"` | `unchanged`, `auto`, `manual` Auto writes auto-WB=1; Manual disables it then requests temperature. |
| `white_balance_temperature` | `4500.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+06 and 1e+06. Manual white balance in backend units, usually kelvin. |
| `additional_controls` | `{}` | Optional named raw controls: brightness, contrast, saturation, hue, sharpness, gamma, zoom, backlight, pan, tilt, roll, iris. |
| `negotiation_policy` | `"require_setter"` | `require_setter`, `require_readback`, `report` Require successful setters, matching readback/frame geometry, or report only. |
| `readback` | `"on_open"` | `on_open`, `every_frame` Poll after opening/first frame, or every frame. Geometry is always observed. |
| `absolute_tolerance` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+06. Absolute tolerance for scalar property readback; size/FourCC are exact. |
| `relative_tolerance` | `0.005` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Relative tolerance for scalar readback; not a hardware accuracy claim. |
| `maximum_frame_pixels` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.71089e+07. Decoded frame guard; native read may already allocate its frame. |
| `maximum_frame_bytes` | `134217728` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.07374e+09. Decoded array byte guard; excludes driver/native/graph copies. |

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `url` | `""` | HTTP, HTTPS or RTSP stream. Saved as plain project text; avoid embedded secrets. |
| `source_name` | `"stream"` | Public result label, without the URL or credentials. |
| `backend` | `"ffmpeg"` | `ffmpeg` Explicit FFmpeg in an owned decoder process; no automatic backend fallback. |
| `rtsp_transport` | `"tcp"` | `tcp`, `udp` TCP or UDP for RTSP. Ignored for HTTP/HTTPS. |
| `startup_timeout_ms` | `5000` | Value must be a whole number between 100 and 60000. Worker initialization deadline, separate from stream opening. |
| `open_timeout_ms` | `3000` | Value must be a whole number between 50 and 60000. Per-connection opening deadline, checked by parent and FFmpeg. |
| `read_timeout_ms` | `1000` | Value must be a whole number between 50 and 60000. Per-frame read deadline, checked by parent and FFmpeg. |
| `reconnect_attempts` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 20. Retries after an initial open/read failure in each activation. |
| `reconnect_delay_ms` | `250` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 60000. Cancellable wait between connection attempts. |
| `decoder_threads` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 16. |
| `maximum_frame_pixels` | `16000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Reject larger decoded frames. Native allocations excluded. |
| `maximum_frame_bytes` | `48000000` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 1.92e+08. Shared BGR slot. Native and converted buffers excluded. |
| `color_format` | `"bgr"` | `unchanged`, `bgr`, `rgb`, `bgra`, `rgba`, `gray`, `hsv`, `lab` Convert decoded uint8 BGR; automatic display rotation is disabled. |
| `pixel_depth` | `"uint8"` | `unchanged`, `uint8`, `uint16`, `float32` Output conversion, not recovery of encoded high-bit-depth samples. |

## Paced video file (`input.paced_video_file`)

Input / Video

Ticket terminals: optional `ticket` data input and `ticket` data output.
Ticket mode and fixed value are common inspector controls.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `path` | `""` |  |
| `path_base` | `"working_directory"` | `working_directory`, `project` |
| `backend` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `ffmpeg`, `gstreamer`, `msmf`, `avfoundation`, `opencv_mjpeg` |
| `start_frame` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 2.14748e+09. Zero-based first frame. |
| `end_frame` | `-1` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 2.14748e+09. Exclusive end frame. -1 reads until the decoder reaches end of file. |
| `stride` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. Emit every Nth frame, counting from the start frame. |
| `repeat` | `"stop"` | `stop`, `loop` |
| `frame_ticket` | `true` | Use filename and frame index as the default ticket value. |
| `color_format` | `"bgr"` | `unchanged`, `bgr`, `rgb`, `bgra`, `rgba`, `gray`, `hsv`, `lab` |
| `pixel_depth` | `"uint8"` | `unchanged`, `uint8`, `uint16`, `float32` |
| `speed` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 100. Wall-clock playback factor. Two runs twice as fast; no implicit frame dropping. |
| `timing_policy` | `"decoder_or_fps"` | `decoder_or_fps`, `decoder_only`, `configured_fps` Prefer increasing decoder timestamps, require them, or use the configured frame rate. |
| `fallback_fps` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1000. Configured rate; zero disables this fallback. Configured FPS policy requires a positive rate. |
| `late_policy` | `"rebase"` | `rebase`, `catch_up` Rebase after excessive delay to avoid bursts, or retain deadlines and catch up without dropping frames. |
| `maximum_lateness_ms` | `250.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 60000. Delay allowed before the chosen late policy takes effect. |
| `maximum_gap_ms` | `10000.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 3.6e+06. Reject larger intervals before waiting; measured in media milliseconds, before Speed. |

## Generate random image (`input.random_image`)

Input / Synthetic

Ticket terminals: optional `ticket` data input and `ticket` data output.
Ticket mode and fixed value are common inspector controls.

Inputs: `seed` (number, optional).

Input `seed` — Optional whole-number seed between 0 and 4,294,967,295.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `distribution` | `"uniform"` | `gaussian`, `uniform` |
| `seed` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 4.29497e+09. Repeat the same draw on each activation. A wired Seed takes precedence. |
| `mean` | `127.5` | Value must be a finite number. Gaussian mean in stored sample units. |
| `standard_deviation` | `32.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Gaussian spread in stored sample units. Zero gives a constant. |
| `minimum` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number. Uniform lower bound in stored sample units. |
| `maximum` | `255.0` | Value must be a finite number. Uniform upper bound in stored sample units. |
| `width` | `320` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8192. |
| `height` | `240` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8192. |
| `color_format` | `"gray"` | `gray`, `bgr`, `rgb` |
| `output_depth` | `"uint8"` | `uint8`, `uint16`, `int16`, `float32`, `float64` Integer output rounds and saturates Gaussian samples. Uniform integers are drawn inclusively between the configured bounds. |

## Packed RAW10 / RAW12 file (`input.raw_sensor`)

Input / Files

Ticket terminals: optional `ticket` data input and `ticket` data output.
Ticket mode and fixed value are common inspector controls.

Inputs: `pixel_frame` (data, optional).

Input `pixel_frame` — Optional decoded-pixel spatial frame; byte-grid coordinates are not reused.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `path` | `""` | One headerless/offset packed sensor frame, with explicit byte layout. |
| `path_base` | `"working_directory"` | `working_directory`, `project` |
| `filename_ticket` | `true` | Use the filename as the automatic source ticket. |
| `packing` | `"mipi_raw10"` | `mipi_raw10`, `mipi_raw12` MIPI RAW10: four high bytes plus packed low 2-bit fields; RAW12: two high bytes plus low nibbles. Other vendor/PFNC layouts are not inferred. |
| `width` | `640` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 16384. Decoded pixels per row; RAW10 needs a multiple of four, RAW12 a multiple of two. |
| `height` | `480` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 16384. Decoded image rows. |
| `row_stride` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1.04858e+06. Bytes per stored row, including padding on the final row. Zero uses tightly packed rows. |
| `byte_offset` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1.34218e+08. Bytes to skip before the first row, such as a known file header. |
| `size_policy` | `"exact"` | `exact`, `at_least` Exact rejects extra bytes. At least selects this frame from a buffer/file with trailing data. |
| `alignment` | `"right"` | `right`, `left` Right retains ADC counts (0..1023 or 0..4095). Left shifts into uint16; it does not stretch the maximum to 65535. |
| `bayer_pattern` | `"RGGB"` | `monochrome`, `RGGB`, `BGGR`, `GRBG`, `GBRG` Sensor tile at its original origin. Monochrome disables metadata-driven demosaicing. |
| `column_offset` | `"even"` | `even`, `odd` Parity of the captured first column relative to the sensor tile origin. |
| `row_offset` | `"even"` | `even`, `odd` Parity of the captured first row relative to the sensor tile origin. |

## Read ROS 2 point cloud (`input.ros2_cloud`)

Industrial integration / ROS 2

Ticket terminals: optional `ticket` data input and `ticket` data output.
Ticket mode and fixed value are common inspector controls.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `topic` | `"/opencv_dataflow/camera/points"` | ROS topic. Endpoints must match domain, topic, installed interface and compatible QoS. |
| `namespace` | `"/opencv_dataflow"` | Node namespace; relative topics resolve here. / is allowed. |
| `domain_id` | `71` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 232. Independent DDS domain. Demo default 71; choose the same domain as your peer. |
| `discovery_range` | `"localhost"` | `localhost`, `subnet` Localhost by default; subnet opts in to network discovery. DDS XML may also affect scope. |
| `queue_depth` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. KEEP_LAST DDS depth and maximum buffered received messages; oldest entries are dropped. |
| `queue_bytes` | `33554432` | Value must be a whole number between 1024 and 6.71089e+07. Local subscription buffer budget, including encoded image data; oldest entries are dropped. |
| `reliability` | `"best_effort"` | `reliable`, `best_effort` Best effort suits cameras. Reliable adds DDS delivery acknowledgments, not application acceptance. |
| `durability` | `"volatile"` | `volatile`, `transient_local` Transient local can replay cached messages while a publisher remains alive. |
| `deadline_seconds` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 3600. Requested/offered DDS deadline; zero uses the middleware's infinite default. |
| `lifespan_seconds` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 3600. DDS sample lifespan; zero uses the middleware's infinite default. |
| `timeout_seconds` | `0.25` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 30. Receive wait, or each discovery/acknowledgment wait for a publication. |
| `startup_timeout_seconds` | `30.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 60. Bounded worker startup. ROS runs in its own configured Python environment. |
| `use_sim_time` | `false` | Use /clock for this node's clock. Does not reinterpret incoming header timestamps. |
| `publication_mode` | `"asynchronous"` | `asynchronous`, `synchronous`, `auto` Fast DDS publishing mode. Asynchronous bounds normal publication latency. |
| `consume` | `"latest"` | `fifo`, `latest` FIFO consumes oldest; latest discards superseded buffered samples. |
| `max_age_seconds` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 3600. Reject buffered messages older than this local arrival age; zero disables it. |
| `enabled` | `false` | Explicitly start a subscription. Disabled sources do not create workers or tickets. |
| `ticket_mode` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `header_stamp` Auto acquisition ticket; header frame+stamp string; or an external data field. |
| `ticket_field` | `""` | Dotted path in decoded data for an external string/number ticket; not a ROS timestamp conversion. |
| `units` | `"m"` | `m`, `mm` Declared incoming XYZ units; no scaling is performed when decoding. |
| `invalid_points` | `"drop"` | `drop`, `reject` Drop invalid XYZ/normals or reject the sample. A false dense claim is rejected. |
| `colors` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `ignore` Decode packed RGB/RGBA or scalar r/g/b bytes; alpha stays in the raw message. |
| `normals` | `"normalize"` | `normalize`, `ignore` Normalize complete normal_x/y/z or nx/ny/nz fields, or leave them in the raw message. |
| `empty_frame` | `"ros_cloud"` | Fallback cloud frame only when ROS header.frame_id is empty; no transform is implied. |

## TIFF page sequence (`input.tiff_file`)

Input / Files

Ticket terminals: optional `ticket` data input and `ticket` data output.
Ticket mode and fixed value are common inspector controls.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `path` | `""` | Path to a TIFF file, including a multipage TIFF. |
| `path_base` | `"working_directory"` | `working_directory`, `project` |
| `start_page` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 2.14748e+09. Zero-based first page. |
| `end_page` | `-1` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 2.14748e+09. Exclusive end page. -1 reads until OpenCV cannot decode another page. |
| `stride` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. Emit every Nth page starting at the first page. |
| `repeat` | `"stop"` | `stop`, `loop` |
| `page_ticket` | `true` | Use filename and page index for automatic tickets. |
| `color_format` | `"unchanged"` | `unchanged`, `bgr`, `rgb`, `bgra`, `rgba`, `gray`, `hsv`, `lab` |

## Read TIFF page on demand (`input.tiff_page`)

Input / Files

Ticket terminals: optional `ticket` data input and `ticket` data output.
Ticket mode and fixed value are common inspector controls.

Inputs: `page_index` (integer, optional), `reset` (boolean, optional).

Input `page_index` — Integer page to read now; connected indices must emit. Order is arbitrary.

Input `reset` — True discards the snapshot and LRU before this request, including Pinned mode.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `path` | `""` | Classic/BigTIFF snapshot, up to 1024 pages; no native file handle is retained. |
| `path_base` | `"working_directory"` | `working_directory`, `project` |
| `page_index` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1023. Zero-based page. A wired Page index takes precedence. |
| `page_ticket` | `true` | Automatic filename#page tickets with fresh acquisition identities. |
| `color_format` | `"unchanged"` | `unchanged`, `bgr`, `rgb`, `bgra`, `rgba`, `gray`, `hsv`, `lab` Unchanged preserves native gray/BGR/BGRA order. TIFF RGB decodes as BGR. |
| `pixel_depth` | `"unchanged"` | `unchanged`, `uint8`, `uint16`, `float32` Unchanged preserves uint8/uint16/float32/float64 samples, including HDR. |
| `refresh` | `"on_change"` | `on_change`, `verify_content`, `pinned` On change checks file identity/size/times. Verify content reads/hashes each request. Pinned ignores external file changes until reset/path change. |
| `cache_pages` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 64. Maximum retained raw pages using least-recently-used eviction. 0=off. |
| `cache_bytes` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1.34218e+08. Maximum retained raw sample bytes. Oversize pages bypass cache. 0=off. |
| `maximum_encoded_bytes` | `134217728` | Value must be a whole number between 8 and 1.34218e+08. |
| `maximum_page_pixels` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Checked from the TIFF directory before native pixel decoding. |
| `maximum_decoded_bytes` | `268435456` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 5.36871e+08. Conservative raw/conversion workspace estimate, excluding retained cache, encoded snapshot, native codec internals and runtime copies. Not a hard RSS limit. |

## Video frame at time (`input.video_at_time`)

Input / Video

Ticket terminals: optional `ticket` data input and `ticket` data output.
Ticket mode and fixed value are common inspector controls.

Inputs: `time_ms` (number, optional).

Input `time_ms` — Finite nonnegative milliseconds, independent of playback wall time.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `path` | `""` | Local video file. Each request opens and closes its own decoder. |
| `path_base` | `"working_directory"` | `working_directory`, `project` |
| `backend` | `"ffmpeg"` | `ffmpeg` |
| `time_ms` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+12. Presentation time in milliseconds; a connected Time ms overrides this value. |
| `selection` | `"nearest"` | `nearest`, `at_or_before`, `at_or_after` Bracket the requested time. Nearest ties prefer the earlier frame. |
| `seek_method` | `"native_then_scan"` | `native_then_scan`, `native_only`, `scan_from_start` Try native time positioning, require it, or decode from the beginning. |
| `preroll_ms` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+12. Native seek anchor is max(0, requested time minus preroll). |
| `maximum_error_ms` | `1000.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+12. Maximum selected-time error; zero requires numerical equality. |
| `timestamp_policy` | `"decoder_only"` | `decoder_only`, `decoder_or_fps` Require decoder time or allow labeled native-FPS estimates. |
| `frame_ticket` | `true` | Auto tickets label filename/index/time; every request stays distinct. |
| `open_timeout_ms` | `5000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 60000. Native FFmpeg opening timeout; not a hard execution deadline. |
| `read_timeout_ms` | `5000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 60000. Native FFmpeg read timeout. Stop is cooperative between native calls. |
| `decoder_threads` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 16. |
| `maximum_read_frames` | `10000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1e+06. Read-request budget, including native probe, fallback and EOF. |
| `maximum_frame_pixels` | `16000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. |
| `maximum_working_bytes` | `268435456` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.07374e+09. Estimated candidate/conversion storage; excludes native memory. |
| `color_format` | `"bgr"` | `unchanged`, `bgr`, `rgb`, `bgra`, `rgba`, `gray`, `hsv`, `lab` Convert native uint8 BGR. Automatic orientation is disabled. |
| `pixel_depth` | `"uint8"` | `unchanged`, `uint8`, `uint16`, `float32` Output conversion, not recovery of encoded high-bit-depth samples. |

## Video file (`input.video_file`)

Input / Video

Ticket terminals: optional `ticket` data input and `ticket` data output.
Ticket mode and fixed value are common inspector controls.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `path` | `""` |  |
| `path_base` | `"working_directory"` | `working_directory`, `project` |
| `backend` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `ffmpeg`, `gstreamer`, `msmf`, `avfoundation`, `opencv_mjpeg` |
| `start_frame` | `0` | Zero-based first frame. |
| `end_frame` | `-1` | Exclusive end frame. -1 reads until the decoder reaches end of file. |
| `stride` | `1` | Emit every Nth frame, counting from the start frame. |
| `repeat` | `"stop"` | `stop`, `loop` |
| `frame_ticket` | `true` | Use filename and frame index as the default ticket value. |
| `color_format` | `"bgr"` | `unchanged`, `bgr`, `rgb`, `bgra`, `rgba`, `gray`, `hsv`, `lab` |
| `pixel_depth` | `"uint8"` | `unchanged`, `uint8`, `uint16`, `float32` |

## Affine-invariant image features (`inspect.affine_features`)

Inspection / Features

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Original image passes through unchanged; detection uses an 8-bit gray copy.

Input `mask` — Optional uint8 mask intersected with region, in the input image's frame.

Input `region` — Optional circle, rectangle or polygon in the input image's frame.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `backend` | `"sift"` | `sift`, `orb` SIFT outputs match existing SIFT matchers; ORB outputs match ORB matchers. |
| `view_mode` | `"generated"` | `generated`, `custom` Generate tilt/roll samples or use the explicit custom view list. |
| `min_tilt` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 6. First exponent of tilt_step; zero includes the unwarped image once. |
| `max_tilt` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 6. Last exponent of tilt_step, inclusive. Tilt factors are limited to eight. |
| `tilt_step` | `1.4142135623730951` | Value must be a finite number between 1.01 and 2. Multiplicative tilt factor between generated levels; not an angle. |
| `rotate_step_base` | `72.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 180. Generated roll spacing in degrees divided by each tilt factor. |
| `custom_views` | `[[1.0, 0.0], [2.0, 0.0], [2.0, 90.0]]` | Explicit [tilt_factor, roll_degrees] pairs; 1..64 views, tilt 1..8. |
| `features_per_view` | `400` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2000. Requested detector limit per sampled view, before combining results. |
| `max_features` | `4000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Keep strongest selected results; descriptor rows follow the same order. |
| `sift_octave_layers` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 16. SIFT sublevels per octave; used only with SIFT. |
| `sift_contrast_threshold` | `0.04` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Larger values reject more low-contrast keypoints. |
| `sift_edge_threshold` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 1000. Larger values keep more edge-like keypoints. |
| `sift_sigma` | `1.6` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 100. Initial SIFT Gaussian sigma; used only with SIFT. |
| `sift_descriptor_depth` | `"float32"` | `float32`, `uint8` SIFT descriptor storage; uint8 still uses L1/L2 matching. |
| `sift_precise_upscale` | `false` | Use OpenCV's precise initial upscale to reduce localization bias. |
| `orb_scale_factor` | `1.2` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 4. ORB pyramid scale ratio; used only with ORB. |
| `orb_levels` | `8` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 32. ORB pyramid level count; first level must be smaller. |
| `orb_fast_threshold` | `20` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 255. ORB FAST detector intensity threshold. |
| `orb_edge_threshold` | `31` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1024. ORB image-border exclusion in sampled-view pixels. |
| `orb_first_level` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 31. ORB pyramid level containing the input sampled view. |
| `orb_wta_k` | `"2"` | `2`, `3`, `4` ORB descriptor comparisons; 3/4 require Hamming2 matching. |
| `orb_score_type` | `"harris"` | `harris`, `fast` ORB feature ranking method. |
| `orb_patch_size` | `31` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 3 and 255. Odd ORB descriptor patch width in sampled-view pixels. |

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |

| `descriptor_bits` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 486. MLDB only. Zero selects full length with three channels. |
| `descriptor_channels` | `"3"` | `1`, `2`, `3` Use three channels on the current OpenCV build. |
| `threshold` | `0.001` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-09 and 1. |
| `octaves` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8. |
| `octave_layers` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8. |
| `diffusivity` | `"pm_g2"` | `pm_g1`, `pm_g2`, `weickert`, `charbonnier` |
| `max_features` | `500` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. |

## Approximate contour (`inspect.approximate_contour`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `contour` (data), `image` (image, optional).

Input `image` — Optional aligned background for viewing the simplified contour.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `item_index` | `0` | Value must be a whole number at least 0. Zero-based contour collection item. A blob uses its largest boundary. |
| `closed` | `true` | Connect the last vertex to the first. Disable for an open path. |
| `epsilon_mode` | `"pixels"` | `pixels`, `length_fraction` |
| `epsilon_pixels` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+06. Maximum approximation tolerance when Epsilon Mode is pixels. |
| `length_fraction` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Tolerance as a fraction of the original perimeter or path length. |

## Approximate convex contour (`inspect.approximate_convex_contour`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `contour` (data), `image` (image, optional).

Input `image` — Optional aligned background for viewing the simplified contour.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `item_index` | `0` | Value must be a whole number at least 0. Zero-based contour collection item. A blob uses its largest boundary. |
| `target_sides` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 1024. Exact side count requested. A valid polygon with a different count fails. |
| `convexity` | `"convex_hull"` | `convex_hull`, `require_convex` Start from the convex hull or require a strictly convex input contour. |
| `max_added_area_percent` | `-1.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1 and 10000. Area added beyond the hull as a percentage of source contour area. Use a positive percentage or -1 for no limit. |

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `dictionary` | `"4x4_50"` | `4x4_100`, `4x4_1000`, `4x4_250`, `4x4_50`, `5x5_100`, `5x5_1000`, `5x5_250`, `5x5_50`, `6x6_100`, `6x6_1000`, `6x6_250`, `6x6_50`, `7x7_100`, `7x7_1000`, `7x7_250`, `7x7_50`, `apriltag_16h5`, `apriltag_25h9`, `apriltag_36h10`, `apriltag_36h11`, `aruco_mip_36h12`, `aruco_original` |
| `corner_refinement` | `"subpixel"` | `none`, `subpixel`, `contour`, `apriltag` |
| `adaptive_min` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 255. |
| `adaptive_max` | `23` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 1000. |
| `adaptive_step` | `10` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100. |
| `adaptive_constant` | `7.0` | Value must be a finite number. |
| `min_perimeter_rate` | `0.03` | Value must be a finite number at least 0.0001. |
| `max_perimeter_rate` | `4.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0.0001. |
| `min_border_distance` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1000. |
| `border_bits` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10. |
| `error_correction_rate` | `0.6` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. |
| `required_ids` | `[]` | Emit points in this ID order. Empty emits all IDs in ascending order. |
| `minimum_markers` | `1` | Value must be a whole number at least 1. Minimum number of unique selected IDs needed to pass. |
| `point` | `"center"` | `center`, `corner_0`, `corner_1`, `corner_2`, `corner_3` Use the projected center or a corner in the marker's decoded orientation. |
| `draw_overlay` | `true` |  |

## Monitor camera health (`inspect.camera_health`)

Inspection / Camera monitoring

Inputs: `image` (image), `foreground_mask` (image, optional).

Input `image` — Current image on the trained pixel grid, with matching depth/encoding.

Input `foreground_mask` — Current-image uint8 mask: nonzero means moving/occluded pixels.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `reference_path` | `""` | Project-contained reference/history asset staged by the trainer. |
| `reference_sha256` | `""` | Pinned training bytes. Retrain/stage to change the baseline. |
| `check_position` | `true` | Check the fixed background or trained static fiducials. |
| `check_lighting` | `true` | Check visible corresponding channel means in each lighting region. |
| `check_focus` | `true` | Check sharpness loss in each focus region against the saved reference. |
| `position_method` | `"background"` | `background`, `fiducials` Stable background tracking or trained static-region matching. |
| `position_warning` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10000. Warning at absolute image-center displacement in pixels. Warning < alarm. |
| `position_alarm` | `3.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10000. Alarm at absolute image-center displacement in pixels. Warning < alarm. |
| `rotation_warning` | `0.25` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10000. Warning at absolute rotation in degrees (background or >=2 fiducials). Warning < alarm. |
| `rotation_alarm` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10000. Alarm at absolute rotation in degrees (background or >=2 fiducials). Warning < alarm. |
| `scale_warning` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10000. Warning at absolute scale change in percent (two fiducials or background). Warning < alarm. |
| `scale_alarm` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10000. Alarm at absolute scale change in percent (two fiducials or background). Warning < alarm. |
| `lighting_warning` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10000. Warning at absolute per-channel mean change in percent. Warning < alarm. |
| `lighting_alarm` | `20.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10000. Alarm at absolute per-channel mean change in percent. Warning < alarm. |
| `focus_warning` | `20.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10000. Warning at absolute sharpness loss in percent. Warning < alarm. |
| `focus_alarm` | `40.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10000. Alarm at absolute sharpness loss in percent. Warning < alarm. |
| `auto_foreground` | `true` | Reject coherent changes after rough motion/lighting normalization. |
| `foreground_threshold` | `0.12` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1. Normalized difference on [0,1] after lighting normalization. |
| `foreground_min_area` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1e+06. Minimum coherent changed component area in pixels. |
| `foreground_dilation` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 32. Extra foreground margin in pixels before tracking and scoring. |
| `minimum_region_coverage` | `0.6` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 1. Required visible/stable fraction; missing evidence alarms. |
| `lighting_floor` | `0.02` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1. Minimum normalized mean used as denominator near black. |
| `max_features` | `600` | Value must be a whole number between 16 and 5000. Maximum Shi-Tomasi reference corners. |
| `feature_quality` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 0.0001 and 0.5. Minimum corner quality relative to the strongest corner. |
| `minimum_tracks` | `12` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 5000. Minimum background inlier count after foreground removal. |
| `tracking_window` | `21` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 5 and 101. Odd Lucas-Kanade window width/height in pixels. |
| `pyramid_levels` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 5. Maximum LK pyramid level; zero disables pyramids. |
| `forward_backward_error` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.05 and 10. Largest allowed round-trip feature error in pixels. |
| `ransac_error` | `1.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 10. Inlier reprojection error in pixels for the similarity transform. |
| `minimum_inlier_fraction` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 1. Minimum inlier fraction among eligible final matches. |
| `minimum_track_coverage` | `0.03` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Inlier convex-hull area / trained position selection area. |
| `fiducial_search_margin` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 512. Search distance around each trained region, in pixels. |
| `fiducial_min_score` | `0.85` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 1. Minimum masked normalized correlation for every fiducial. |
| `fiducial_peak_margin` | `0.02` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Required score separation from competing locations. |
| `focus_method` | `"tenengrad"` | `tenengrad`, `laplacian_variance` Training metric. Changes require retraining. |
| `focus_aperture` | `"3"` | `1`, `3`, `5`, `7` Derivative kernel; keep fixed between training and inspection. |
| `normalize_focus` | `true` | Divide sharpness by region variance to reduce gain sensitivity. |
| `float_min` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+12 and 1e+12. Lower bound for floating samples; changes require retraining. |
| `float_max` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+12 and 1e+12. Upper bound for floating samples, usually one or 255; retrain to change. |
| `stability_threshold` | `0.08` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Largest stable-pixel history range on [0,1]; retrain to change. |

| `expected_count` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 2.14748e+09. |

| `region_mode` | `"center"` | `center`, `clip` Center keeps whole objects; clip measures only their selected pixels. |

## Compare exposure-adjusted images (`inspect.differential_exposure`)

Inspection / Reference inspection

Inputs: `image` (image), `reference` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `fit_mask` (image, optional), `fit_region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Candidate intensity image; owns output tickets, geometry and unchanged alpha.

Input `reference` — Aligned reference with matching dimensions, encoding and depth.

Input `mask` — Inspection selection; intersects Region. Residuals are zero outside it.

Input `region` — Optional inspection region on the candidate grid.

Input `fit_mask` — Stable fitting selection, independent of inspection; intersects Fit region.

Input `fit_region` — Optional fitting region. With no fitting inputs, use the inspection selection.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `scope` | `"local"` | `local`, `global` Local weighted windows, or one model per channel for the whole fit selection. |
| `window` | `63` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 3 and 255. Odd local window. Only actual in-image pixels supply support; no reflection. |
| `model` | `"gain_offset"` | `gain_offset`, `offset_only` Reference = gain * image + offset, or fix gain at one. Alpha is not fitted. |
| `robust_loss` | `"tukey"` | `tukey`, `none` Tukey refits reject residual outliers; None uses ordinary least squares. |
| `iterations` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10. Robust reweight/refit steps after the initial fit; unused for None. |
| `outlier_limit` | `8.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-12 and 1e+12. Tukey cutoff in raw sample units. Residuals at/above it receive zero weight. |
| `minimum_contrast` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+12. Raw standard-deviation floor. Low-contrast windows use offset-only correction. |
| `gain_regularization` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+12. Raw contrast scale of a squared ridge penalty toward gain one; zero disables it. |
| `minimum_gain` | `0.25` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Inclusive lower gain bound; out-of-range models provide no coverage. |
| `maximum_gain` | `4.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Inclusive upper gain bound; must be at least Minimum gain. |
| `minimum_fit_pixels` | `16` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 4.1943e+06. Minimum effective weighted samples in each model/window. |
| `minimum_fit_fraction` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Effective support / actual local window area, or / initial global fit selection. |
| `minimum_valid_fraction` | `0.95` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Inclusive selected fraction with usable models and samples. |
| `defect_threshold` | `15.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+12. Maximum accepted raw-unit absolute residual; any channel exceeding it is a defect. |
| `maximum_defect_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Allowed valid-pixel defect fraction; zero allows none. |
| `saturation` | `"integer_endpoints"` | `integer_endpoints`, `none` Exclude integer minimum/maximum endpoints from fitting and inspection, or disable. Floating data has no assumed saturation level. |
| `reference_alignment` | `"pixel_grid"` | `pixel_grid`, `same_frame` Pixel grid assumes equal-index registration; Same frame needs matching known frames. |

## Watershed seeds from distance peaks (`inspect.distance_markers`)

Inspection / Segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `distance_type` | `"l2"` | `l2`, `l1`, `chessboard` |
| `l2_mask_size` | `"precise"` | `precise`, `3`, `5` |
| `minimum_distance` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Minimum distance from a peak to the background, in pixels. |
| `peak_radius` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 63. Compare each peak with neighbors in a square of this radius. |
| `background_gap` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 255. Number of pixel layers to leave unlabeled outside the foreground. |
| `connectivity` | `"8"` | `4`, `8` Connectivity used to group adjacent peak pixels into one seed. |

## Estimate linear motion blur (`inspect.estimate_motion_blur`)

Image processing / Restoration

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — One spatially uniform linear-motion blur over approximately isotropic texture.

Input `mask` — Only fully selected square tiles contribute to estimation.

Input `region` — Optional region intersected with Mask; no black mask padding in spectra.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `channel` | `"gray"` | `gray`, `R`, `G`, `B` Luminance or a named stored color channel; alpha is excluded. |
| `tile_size` | `128` | `64`, `128`, `256`, `512` Value must be a whole number between 64 and 512. Disjoint square tiles, fixed periodic Hann window; current-image pixels. |
| `minimum_tiles` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 4 and 64. At least this many fully selected, opaque tiles; split fit/validation. |
| `maximum_tiles` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 4 and 64. Evenly subsample this many eligible tiles before alternating groups. |
| `minimum_length` | `4.0` | Value must be a finite number between 2 and 63. First centered line-path length in current pixels. |
| `maximum_length` | `24.0` | Value must be a finite number between 2 and 63. Largest searched length; must be at most one quarter of Tile size. |
| `length_step` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.25 and 16. Length grid spacing; last sampled length does not exceed Maximum length. |
| `angle_start` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -360 and 360. First direction; output angles wrap modulo 180 degrees. |
| `angle_span` | `180.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.25 and 180. Search arc in degrees; 180 covers all undirected line orientations. |
| `angle_step` | `5.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.25 and 180. Grid spacing clockwise from horizontal in image coordinates. |
| `model_floors` | `[0.0001, 0.001, 0.01, 0.1]` | 1-8 distinct positive floor ratios <=1; nuisance values, not SNR. |
| `minimum_frequency` | `0.04` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 0.25. Lower fitted radial frequency, cycles/current pixel. |
| `maximum_frequency` | `0.45` | Value must be a finite number between 0.05 and 0.49. Upper fitted radial frequency, below axial Nyquist. |
| `minimum_contrast` | `0.001` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum mean tile standard deviation divided by working sample scale. |
| `minimum_score` | `0.6` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum explained whitened log-power variance in fit and validation. |
| `minimum_margin` | `0.03` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 2. Fit score gap to a distinct hypothesis; zero disables ambiguity gating. |
| `distinct_length` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.25 and 63. Length difference making an alternative distinct, in current pixels. |
| `distinct_angle` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.25 and 90. Modulo-180 angle difference making an alternative distinct. |
| `boundary_policy` | `"reject"` | `reject`, `allow` Reject a best length/partial-arc endpoint, or explicitly allow it. |
| `maximum_candidates` | `4096` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 16384. Cap total length/angle/floor trials before spectrum work. |
| `maximum_search_work` | `64000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2.56e+08. Cap template pixels plus trial frequency samples before DFT. |
| `maximum_working_bytes` | `536870912` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2.14748e+09. Array estimate, excluding graph copies and native workspaces. |

## Evaluate image features and matches (`inspect.evaluate_features`)

Inspection / Feature evaluation

Inputs: `query_image` (image), `train_image` (image), `query` (data), `train` (data), `transform` (data, optional), `matches` (data, optional), `query_mask` (image, optional), `train_mask` (image, optional), `query_region` (data, optional), `train_region` (data, optional).

Input `query` — ORB/BRISK/AKAZE/SIFT/KAZE feature data for Query image.

Input `train` — Same descriptor family/settings for Train image.

Input `transform` — Independent query-to-train truth. Fitting these matches biases evaluation.

Input `matches` — Indexed paired match record; descriptor distance is used as rank.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `reference_transform` | `[[1, 0, 0], [0, 1, 0], [0, 0, 1]]` | Known query-to-train homography; wired Transform overrides. |
| `reprojection_tolerance` | `3.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1024. Inclusive target-pixel center error; geometry-only correctness. |
| `border_margin` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 4096. Boundary margin in each image's pixels; selections use keypoint centers. |
| `ranking` | `"distance"` | `distance`, `ratio` Automatic NN distance or ratio; external Matches always ranks by distance. |
| `match_threshold` | `64.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+09. Inclusive operating threshold; ratio normally uses 0..1. |
| `minimum_correspondences` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 4096. Minimum one-to-one geometric pairs; no-support never passes. |
| `minimum_repeatability` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Correspondence count divided by smaller eligible feature count. |
| `minimum_precision` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Unique correct pairs / accepted rows. Duplicates reduce precision. |
| `minimum_recall` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Unique correct pairs / geometric correspondences. |
| `maximum_features` | `2000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Per-input feature count cap before descriptor conversion. |
| `maximum_pairs` | `4000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. Full input query×train pair guard, before descriptor/geometry matching. |
| `maximum_geometry_edges` | `100000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 200000. Valid geometry edge cap before one-to-one assignment. |
| `maximum_matches` | `10000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 20000. Bound diagnostic rows and external candidate lists. |
| `maximum_curve_points` | `64` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 256. Maximum sampled thresholds, including operating and highest rank. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `8388608` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Per-image cap before grayscale/preview or numeric evaluation. |
| `preview_width` | `1200` | Value must be a whole number between 128 and 2048. Maximum side-by-side diagnostic width; output has its own grid. |
| `preview_height` | `600` | Value must be a whole number between 128 and 1024. Maximum diagnostic height; images scale together. |
| `maximum_graphic_matches` | `200` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 2000. Draw at most this many accepted candidates, best rank first. |

## Fit circle or arc (`inspect.fit_circle_arc`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `points` (data), `image` (image, optional), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `points` — Ordered [x,y] points or contour/blob collection; no edge detection.

Input `image` — Optional fitting grid and unchanged image; compatible frames map into it.

Input `mask` — Optional uint8 selection of points by nearest pixel. Others do not fit or vote.

Input `region` — Optional region intersected with the mask. Selection does not prove closure.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `method` | `"ransac"` | `ransac`, `least_squares` RANSAC refits radial consensus; least squares uses every eligible unique point. |
| `item_index` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 99999. Current collection position. Ignored for point-list input. |
| `boundary_index` | `-1` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 99999. Blob boundary; -1 chooses largest. Ignored for contours/lists. |
| `path` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `open`, `closed` Auto closes detected contours or a list with a repeated endpoint; otherwise open. |
| `inlier_tolerance` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-09 and 1e+07. Inclusive radial consensus distance in output pixels. RANSAC only. |
| `maximum_trials` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 16 and 4096. RANSAC candidate budget; adaptive stopping uses at least 16 trials. |
| `confidence` | `0.99` | Value must be a finite number between 0.5 and 0.999999. Adaptive RANSAC trial target; not a measurement confidence interval. |
| `random_seed` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 4.29497e+09. Repeatable local seed, 0–4294967295. Global random state is unchanged. |
| `refinement_iterations` | `50` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 200. Geometric iterations per support refit; at most five rounds. |
| `position_tolerance` | `1e-08` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-12 and 0.1. Geometric convergence tolerance in output pixel units. |
| `minimum_inliers` | `6` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 100000. Minimum distinct fitting points. Duplicate coordinates vote once. |
| `minimum_inlier_fraction` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum accepted support divided by eligible point count. |
| `minimum_span_degrees` | `30.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 359.99. Smallest angular envelope, excluding the largest circular gap. |
| `maximum_condition` | `10000.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 1e+12. Largest radial-Jacobian condition number. High means unstable. |
| `maximum_gap_degrees` | `120.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 179.99. Largest consecutive support angle, including closed-path closure. |
| `maximum_backtracking_fraction` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 0.5. Allowed reverse angular travel / total travel, 0 to 0.5. |
| `minimum_radius` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-09 and 1e+07. Smallest accepted radius, in the fitting grid's pixels. |
| `maximum_radius` | `10000000.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-09 and 1e+07. Largest accepted radius, also used to screen RANSAC candidates. |
| `check_rmse` | `true` | Judge fitting-support radial root mean square error. |
| `maximum_rmse` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+07. Inclusive fitting-support RMSE limit in output pixels. |
| `sample_limit` | `2000` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Maximum residual rows; zero skips the table without changing the fit. |

## Measure image focus (`inspect.focus`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `mask` — Optional scoring mask. Derivatives still use the full image neighborhood.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |

## Fourier correlation (`inspect.fourier_correlation`)

Inspection / Registration

Inputs: `image` (image), `reference` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reference_mask` (image, optional).

Input `image` — Monochrome sample image in which to locate the reference displacement.

Input `reference` — Monochrome reference. Linear mode accepts a different image size.

Input `reference_mask` — Optional uint8 selection in the reference image frame.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `boundary` | `"linear"` | `linear`, `circular` Linear zero-pads to avoid wrapping. Circular compares equal-size images. |
| `normalization` | `"energy"` | `energy`, `none` Energy scales each whole signal to unit L2 norm before correlation. |
| `subtract_mean` | `false` | Remove each input's mean over its selected samples before correlation. |

## Image frequency response (`inspect.frequency_response`)

Inspection / Spectral measurements

Inputs: `image` (image), `reference` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reference_mask` (image, optional), `reference_region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Aligned sample capture. Original type/encoding is preserved on Image.

Input `reference` — Aligned reference with known scene detail; no registration is fitted.

Input `mask` — Optional sample-space uint8 selection; shared support weights both signals.

Input `region` — Optional sample-space region, intersected with the other selections.

Input `reference_mask` — Optional uint8 selection in the reference's own coordinates.

Input `reference_region` — Optional reference-space region; both selections must overlap.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `channel` | `"gray"` | `gray`, `R`, `G`, `B`, `A` Gray uses Rec.601 RGB luminance; named channels must exist in both inputs. |
| `unsigned_scale` | `"raw"` | `raw`, `unit_range` Raw uses sample units; Unit range divides unsigned values by depth max. |
| `reference_alignment` | `"pixel_grid"` | `pixel_grid`, `same_frame` Assert pixel alignment, or require identical known spatial grids. |
| `ticket_policy` | `"independent"` | `independent`, `same_ticket` Allow historical reference, or require matching acquisition identities. |
| `window` | `"none"` | `none`, `hann`, `sqrt_hann` Shared none/Hann/square-root Hann aperture; finite windows can bias estimates. |
| `subtract_mean` | `true` | Remove each signal's aperture-weighted mean before the transform. |
| `relative_floor` | `1e-06` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Reject reference amplitudes at/below this fraction of its full peak. |
| `absolute_floor` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+100. Reference floor in sample units after aperture normalization. |
| `phase_relative_floor` | `1e-06` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Phase needs sample amplitude above this fraction of its peak. |
| `minimum_frequency` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 0.707107. Inclusive radial lower frequency in cycles per image pixel. |
| `maximum_frequency` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 0.707107. Inclusive radial upper frequency; corners can extend to sqrt(0.5). |
| `direction` | `"all"` | `all`, `horizontal`, `vertical`, `angle` Antipodal frequency cone; horizontal means varying across image x. |
| `angle_degrees` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -180 and 180. Cone orientation with x right/y down, used by Angle. |
| `angular_tolerance_degrees` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 90. Inclusive half-angle of the antipodal frequency cone. |
| `radial_bins` | `64` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 256. Equal-width radial summaries; unsupported bins remain undefined. |
| `include_dc` | `false` | Include DC at minimum frequency zero; centering can remove it. |
| `minimum_supported_bins` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2.1e+06. Minimum excited bins, counting both real conjugate partners. |
| `minimum_supported_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Excited bins divided by eligible band/direction bins. |
| `minimum_mean_gain` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+100. Inclusive lower bound on unweighted supported mean gain. |
| `maximum_mean_gain` | `1000000.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+100. Inclusive upper bound on supported mean linear gain. |
| `check_phase` | `false` | Require phase and limit its maximum absolute wrapped value from zero. |
| `maximum_phase_degrees` | `180.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. Inclusive phase limit when enabled; shifts affect phase. |
| `maximum_workspace_mib` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 256. Work-buffer estimate; snapshots/previews use additional memory. |

## GrabCut foreground (`inspect.grabcut`)

Inputs: `image` (image), `markers` (image, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |

| `rectangle` | `[10, 10, 100, 100]` | Initial foreground rectangle [x, y, width, height] in image pixels. |
| `iterations` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100. |

## GrabCut seeds from masks (`inspect.grabcut_mask_seeds`)

Inspection / Segmentation

Input `image` — Sets the size, coordinate frame, and ticket of the marker image.

Input `background` — Definite background. Overrides either probable class.

Input `foreground` — Definite foreground. Overrides either probable class.

Input `probable_background` — A background hint that GrabCut may change.

Input `probable_foreground` — A foreground hint that GrabCut may change.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `unmarked` | `"probable_background"` | `probable_background`, `probable_foreground` Initial class for pixels outside all supplied masks. |
| `emit_preview` | `true` | Show definite background in blue and foreground in green, with gray and amber for the probable classes. |

## Gray-level co-occurrence (`inspect.gray_level_cooccurrence`)

Inspection / Texture

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Grayscale or color uint8/uint16, limited to 2.1 million pixels.

Input `mask` — Both pixels of every counted pair must be selected.

Input `region` — Both pixels must lie in the region and in any wired mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `offsets` | `[[1, 0], [0, 1]]` | Up to 16 distinct nonzero [dx, dy] pixel offsets. Right and down are positive. |
| `levels` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 256. Quantization bins. Keep levels and intensity range fixed across captures. |
| `range_mode` | `"dtype"` | `dtype`, `manual` Use the full integer depth or clamp to the manual endpoints before binning. |
| `range_min` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 65535. |
| `range_max` | `255` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 65535. |
| `symmetric` | `true` | Count each pair in both directions. Pair counts stay undoubled. |
| `offset_index` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 15. Zero-based offset for the scalar output and probability matrix. |
| `metric` | `"contrast"` | `contrast`, `dissimilarity`, `homogeneity`, `energy`, `correlation`, `asm`, `mean`, `variance`, `standard_deviation`, `entropy` Property to send to the Value output. Measurements contains all properties. |
| `check_threshold` | `false` |  |
| `threshold_mode` | `"maximum"` | `minimum`, `maximum` |
| `threshold` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number. Inclusive lower or upper limit for the selected value. |

## Find rotated and scaled Hough template (`inspect.guil_template`)

Inspection / Matching

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `template` (image).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `template_anchor` | `[-1, -1]` | Template pose origin. [-1, -1] uses the integer center. |
| `canny_low` | `40.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. |
| `canny_high` | `100.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. |
| `levels` | `90` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 720. Number of gradient orientation bins used for voting. |
| `dp` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 16. Accumulator cell spacing in source pixels. Larger values use a coarser grid. |
| `minimum_votes` | `50` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4e+08. Minimum accepted center vote count. |
| `min_center_distance` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10000. Minimum distance between retained anchors after selection. |
| `minimum_matches` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 64. |
| `maximum_matches` | `25` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. |
| `draw_overlay` | `true` |  |
| `min_angle` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 360. Start of the clockwise image-angle search in degrees. |
| `max_angle` | `360.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 360. Exclusive end of the clockwise angle grid, up to 360 degrees. |
| `angle_step` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.5 and 359. Spacing between angle hypotheses in degrees. |
| `min_scale` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0.05 and 8. Smallest template magnification to search. |
| `max_scale` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.05 and 8. Exclusive end of the magnification grid. |
| `scale_step` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 8. Spacing between magnification hypotheses. |
| `min_angle_votes` | `300` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4e+08. |
| `min_scale_votes` | `50` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4e+08. |
| `max_buffer_size` | `48` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 256. Maximum feature pairs kept per orientation bin. |
| `pair_angle` | `90.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 359. Target angle difference between the two gradients of a feature pair. |
| `angle_epsilon` | `4.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 30. Angular tolerance in degrees for building and matching pairs. |

## Histogram (`inspect.histogram`)

Image processing / Statistics

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |

| `bins` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 4096. |
| `range_mode` | `"automatic"` | `automatic`, `manual` |
| `range_min` | `0.0` |  |
| `range_max` | `1.0` |  |
| `include_alpha` | `false` |  |
| `lower_percentile` | `5.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 100. Lower-tail cutoff. Pair with upper percentile for a middle range. |
| `upper_percentile` | `95.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 100. Cutoff for the upper tail, expressed as a percentile from the bottom. |
| `statistics_channel` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `Gray`, `B`, `G`, `R`, `A`, `H`, `S`, `V`, `L`, `a`, `b` Channel for scalar outputs and plot markers. Auto uses the first. |
| `threshold_statistic` | `"median"` | `lower_threshold`, `upper_threshold`, `mean`, `median` Value sent on the threshold output. |
| `show_statistics` | `true` | Show all channel statistics and mark the selected channel on the plot. |

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `histogram` (data).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |

## Histogram of oriented gradients (`inspect.hog`)

Inspection / Features

Inputs: `image` (image).

Input `image` — One uint8 grayscale, BGR or RGB window. Crop first to measure a region.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `cell_size` | `[8, 8]` | [Width, height] of a cell in pixels. |
| `block_size` | `[16, 16]` | [Width, height] in pixels. Both dimensions must be multiples of cell size. |
| `block_stride` | `[8, 8]` | [X, Y] stride in pixels, in whole cells no larger than the block. |
| `bins` | `9` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 36. Gradient orientation bins per cell. |
| `win_sigma` | `-1.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1 and 4096. Gaussian weighting sigma in pixels. −1 uses OpenCV's automatic value. |
| `l2_hys_threshold` | `0.2` | Value must be a finite number between 0.0001 and 1. Clipping threshold before the second block normalization. |
| `gamma_correction` | `true` | Apply square-root gamma correction before measuring gradients. |
| `signed_gradient` | `false` | Use 360° directions. Off treats opposite directions as equivalent. |

## Hough circles (`inspect.hough_circles`)

Inspection / Features

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — 8-bit monochrome or color image. Color is converted to grayscale.

Input `mask` — Keep detections whose center lies in this mask.

Input `region` — Keep detections whose center lies in this region.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `dp` | `1.2` | Value must be a finite number at least 1. Inverse accumulator resolution. Use 1 for the same resolution as the image. |
| `min_distance` | `20.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Minimum distance between detected centers in image pixels. |
| `edge_threshold` | `100.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Upper Canny threshold. Gradient ALT uses Scharr derivatives. |
| `center_threshold` | `30.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Gradient uses an accumulator cutoff. Gradient ALT uses a perfectness threshold below 1 (e.g. 0.9). |
| `min_radius` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Smallest searched radius in pixels. |
| `max_radius` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 100000. Largest searched radius in pixels. Zero uses the largest image dimension. Use -1 in Gradient mode to locate centers without fitting radii. |
| `method` | `"gradient"` | `gradient`, `gradient_alt` |

## Hough line segments (`inspect.hough_lines`)

Inspection / Features

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `votes` | `50` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. |
| `min_length` | `30.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. |
| `max_gap` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. |
| `rho` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. |
| `theta_degrees` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. |

## Hough lines from points (`inspect.hough_point_lines`)

Inspection / Features

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `points` (data).

Input `image` — Coordinate frame and overlay background for the sparse points.

Input `points` — Finite [x, y] rows in input image coordinates. Image pixels do not vote.

Input `mask` — Keep voting points whose nearest pixel is inside this mask.

Input `region` — Keep voting points whose nearest pixel is inside this region.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `minimum_votes` | `12` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. Fewest point votes required in a Hough bin, inclusive. |
| `maximum_lines` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 512. Maximum native peaks returned in vote order. |
| `minimum_lines` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 512. Fewest finite segments required to pass. Zero permits an empty result. |
| `rho_mode` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `custom` Fit a symmetric distance search to the points, or set the native range. |
| `rho_min` | `-512.0` | Value must be a finite number between -32768 and 32768. Lower signed normal-distance limit in Custom mode, in pixels. |
| `rho_max` | `512.0` | Value must be a finite number between -32768 and 32768. Upper signed normal-distance limit in Custom mode, in pixels. |
| `rho_step` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 32768. Spacing between normal-distance bins, in pixels. |
| `theta_min_degrees` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. First line-normal angle. Zero searches vertical lines. |
| `theta_max_degrees` | `180.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. Upper angle bound for the line normal, in degrees. |
| `theta_step_degrees` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 180. Angle bin spacing. A 90-degree normal describes a horizontal line. |
| `support_distance` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 32768. Distance to the peak line for endpoint support. Zero uses half a bin. |

## Hough polar lines (`inspect.hough_polar_lines`)

Inspection / Features

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — 8-bit monochrome edge map. Zero-valued pixels never vote.

Input `mask` — Only selected edge pixels contribute to the accumulator.

Input `region` — Only edge pixels inside the region contribute to the accumulator.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `vote_mode` | `"binary"` | `binary`, `weighted` Binary gives each nonzero pixel one vote. Weighted uses its intensity. |
| `minimum_votes` | `50` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2.55e+08. Inclusive accumulator minimum. Weighted mode uses intensity sums. |
| `maximum_lines` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 512. Maximum native peaks reported, ordered by accumulator value. |
| `minimum_lines` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 512. Fewest finite segments required to pass. Zero permits an empty result. |
| `rho_step` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 32768. Spacing between normal-distance bins, in pixels. |
| `theta_min_degrees` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. First line-normal angle. Zero searches vertical lines. |
| `theta_max_degrees` | `180.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. Upper angle bound for the line normal, in degrees. |
| `theta_step_degrees` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 180. Angle bin spacing. A 90-degree normal describes a horizontal line. |
| `support_distance` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 32768. Distance to the peak line for endpoint support. Zero uses half a bin. |
