| `levels` | `360` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 720. Number of gradient orientation bins used for voting. |
| `dp` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 16. Accumulator cell spacing in source pixels. Larger values use a coarser grid. |
| `minimum_votes` | `25` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 1.5e+08. Minimum accepted center vote count. |
| `min_center_distance` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10000. Minimum distance between retained anchors after selection. |

## Compare aligned images (correlation) (`inspect.image_correlation`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `image` (image), `reference` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Already aligned candidate. Gray/BGR/RGB/BGRA/RGBA: uint8, uint16, float32/64. HSV/Lab: uint8/float32.

Input `reference` — Equal-size aligned reference. Per-channel mode needs matching compared channels. Mixed depths compare raw sample values.

Input `mask` — Optional uint8 selection in the candidate's coordinate frame; intersects the region.

Input `region` — Optional region in the candidate's pixel grid. Both images use the same selected pixels.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `method` | `"ecc"` | `ecc`, `normalized_correlation` Native computeECC or double-precision normalized correlation on selected pixels. |
| `channels` | `"per_channel"` | `per_channel`, `gray` Compare canonical B/G/R separately, or weighted grayscale. HSV/Lab convert to BGR. |
| `aggregation` | `"minimum"` | `minimum`, `mean` Use the lowest similarity or unweighted channel mean; undefined channels fail. |
| `include_alpha` | `false` | Include alpha in per-channel comparison when both inputs have it; otherwise ignore it. |
| `reference_alignment` | `"pixel_grid"` | `pixel_grid`, `same_frame` Pixel grid compares equal-size pixels by index. Same frame requires matching spatial payloads on both images. |
| `constant_policy` | `"fail"` | `fail`, `match_equal` Fail undefined correlation, or explicitly accept two constant regions whose values match within tolerance. |
| `minimum_stddev` | `1e-12` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+308. A channel at or below this standard deviation is treated as constant, in original sample units. |
| `constant_tolerance` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+308. Maximum per-pixel absolute difference for match_equal, in original sample units; no depth rescaling. |
| `check_threshold` | `true` | Apply the inclusive minimum similarity; undefined comparisons still fail when disabled. |
| `minimum_similarity` | `0.95` | Value must be a finite number between -1 and 1. Inclusive signed correlation limit on [-1,1]. Positive gain/offset changes are not defects to this metric. |
| `emit_residual` | `false` | Output maximum absolute per-channel z-score difference as float32; zero outside the selection. |
| `residual_preview_max` | `3.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+06. Z-score difference at full heat-map intensity; affects only the diagnostic preview. |

## Compare image quality (`inspect.image_quality`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `image_a` (image), `image_b` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `metric` | `"ssim"` | `ssim`, `psnr`, `mse`, `mae`, `rmse`, `max_absolute_error` |
| `mask_alignment` | `"same_frame"` | `same_frame`, `pixel_grid` Pixel grid applies an equal-size mask by index across frame IDs. |
| `check_threshold` | `false` |  |
| `threshold` | `0.95` | Minimum for SSIM/PSNR, maximum for error metrics. |
| `peak_value` | `255.0` | Maximum sample value for PSNR. Default is 255 for uint8 images. |

## Check image samples (`inspect.image_validity`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Grayscale, color or physical depth. Tests the stored sample values.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `check_range` | `false` | Also test the stored sample values against the lower and upper bounds. |
| `minimum` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number. Inclusive lower bound, applied to every stored channel. |
| `maximum` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number. Exclusive upper bound, applied to every stored channel. |
| `max_invalid_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Maximum fraction of selected pixels allowed to be invalid. |

## Intensity-weighted center and orientation (`inspect.intensity_moments`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Gray/color uint8, uint16 or float32/64, up to 4.2 MP and 16,384 per axis.

Input `mask` — Optional uint8 selection in the image's coordinate frame.

Input `region` — Optional circle, rectangle or polygon intersected with the mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `weighting` | `"intensity"` | `intensity`, `uniform_foreground`, `uniform_selection` Intensity uses positive contrast weights. Uniform foreground weights qualifying pixels equally. Uniform selection measures the entire mask/region. |
| `channel` | `"luminance"` | `luminance`, `Gray`, `B`, `G`, `R`, `A`, `H`, `S`, `V`, `L`, `a`, `b` Use floating luminance (alpha ignored) or an explicit stored color channel. |
| `intensity_units` | `"native"` | `native`, `normalized` Native sample units, or integer full scale mapped to [0,1]; floats unchanged. |
| `polarity` | `"bright"` | `bright`, `dark`, `absolute` Bright: value-background; dark: background-value; absolute: their magnitude. |
| `background` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number. Constant baseline in the chosen intensity units; negative contrast is clipped. |
| `minimum_signal` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Keep contrast strictly above this value; surviving weights are not reduced. |
| `minimum_support` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4.1943e+06. Minimum number of qualifying pixels for a valid center. |
| `minimum_mass` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Require total weight strictly above this value; uniform modes use pixel count. |
| `minimum_variance` | `1e-09` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Require major-axis variance above this pixel-squared value for orientation. |
| `minimum_anisotropy` | `0.05` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Inclusive minimum (major-minor)/(major+minor) for an unsigned principal axis. |
| `require_orientation` | `false` | Fail an otherwise valid center when its principal axis is ambiguous. |
| `pose_orientation` | `"principal_axis"` | `principal_axis`, `translation_only` Principal axis requires valid orientation; translation only defines zero angle. |
| `missing_center` | `"fail"` | `fail`, `report` Fail missing/below-limit centers, or emit an informational report without a center. |
| `emit_weights` | `false` | Emit the peak-normalized floating weight image and its scale metadata. |
| `show_geometric` | `true` | Draw a uniform-support center and covariance ellipse for comparison. |
| `ellipse_sigma` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 10. Drawing radius in standard deviations; it is not a fitted part boundary. |

## Judge strip defects (`inspect.judge_strip_defects`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `inspection_report` (data).

Input `inspection_report` — Complete inspection report from Group strip defects. The group's minimum report length does not affect this input.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `length_units` | `"source px"` | `source px`, `strip px`, `mm`, `cm`, `m`, `in` Required units for defect lengths. Match the Group strip defects report. |
| `limits` | `{}` | Per-kind limits, for example {"narrow": {"max_count": 1, "max_length": 12}}. Use narrow, wide, out_of_range, missing or unmeasurable. An unlisted kind is rejected. Set either or both inclusive limits for a listed kind. |

## KAZE keypoints and descriptors (`inspect.kaze`)

Inspection / Features

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `extended` | `false` | Use 128 floating-point values per descriptor instead of 64. |
| `upright` | `false` | Use a fixed descriptor orientation for scenes without rotation. |
| `threshold` | `0.001` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-09 and 1. Minimum detector response for accepting a keypoint. |
| `octaves` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8. Maximum number of scales in the nonlinear scale space. |
| `octave_layers` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8. Number of sublevels within each scale. |
| `diffusivity` | `"pm_g2"` | `pm_g1`, `pm_g2`, `weickert`, `charbonnier` Diffusion model used to build the nonlinear scale space. |
| `max_features` | `500` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Keep up to this many of the strongest selected keypoints. |

## K-means image pixels (`inspect.kmeans_image`)

Inspection / Segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `initial_labels` (image, optional).

Input `initial_labels` — Optional single-channel integer label image for the first attempt.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `clusters` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 32. Must be no greater than the number of samples used in this run. |
| `initialization` | `"kmeans_plus_plus"` | `kmeans_plus_plus`, `random` |
| `attempts` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 20. Returns the fit with the lowest sum of squared distances. |
| `termination` | `"both"` | `both`, `iterations`, `epsilon` |
| `max_iterations` | `50` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 100. Used by the both and iterations modes. |
| `epsilon` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Center movement tolerance in feature units, used by both and epsilon modes. |

## Measure line pair (`inspect.line_pair`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `image` (image), `line_a` (data), `line_b` (data), `scale` (data, optional), `valid_mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Grid for geometry, coverage and graphics; pixels are passed through.

Input `line_a` — Line fit from Fit shape, or a lines collection with a selected index.

Input `line_b` — Second fit or collection. Compatible frame transforms are applied.

Input `scale` — Uniform physical scale and origin from Rectify workplane.

Input `valid_mask` — Optional coverage mask; required for physical measurements.

Input `region` — Optional inspection region containing all measured paths.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `mode` | `"corner"` | `corner`, `angle`, `parallel_gap` Measure a corner, angle alone, or transverse gap over common support. |
| `angle_mode` | `"acute"` | `acute`, `obtuse` Acute reports 0..90 degrees; Obtuse reports the 90..180 supplement. |
| `units` | `"px"` | `px`, `mm`, `cm`, `m`, `in` Physical units require a matching Rectify workplane scale and validity mask. |
| `index_a` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 99999. Zero-based entry when Line A receives a lines collection. |
| `index_b` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 99999. Zero-based entry when Line B receives a lines collection. |
| `minimum_intersection_angle` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 90. Reject nearly parallel, poorly conditioned corner fits. |
| `maximum_extension` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+09. Maximum segment extension in selected units; zero disables. |
| `require_vertex_in_image` | `true` | Reject a corner outside the current image. |
| `maximum_parallel_angle` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 45. Maximum acute angle allowed for gap measurement, in degrees. |
| `minimum_overlap` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+09. Minimum shared longitudinal support, in the selected length units. |
| `check_angle` | `false` | Apply target-angle tolerance after geometric qualification. |
| `target_angle` | `90.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. Target for the selected acute or obtuse angle, in degrees. |
| `angle_tolerance` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. Inclusive absolute deviation from the target angle. |
| `check_gap` | `false` | Require the entire measured overlap to meet the gap limits. |
| `minimum_gap` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+09. Inclusive minimum separation in the selected length units. |
| `maximum_gap` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+09. Inclusive maximum separation. Zero disables the upper limit. |

## Find line segments (LSD) (`inspect.line_segments`)

Inspection / Features

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `refinement` | `"standard"` | `none`, `standard`, `advanced` |
| `scale` | `0.8` | Detection image scale in (0,1]. Returned coordinates use the input image. |
| `sigma_scale` | `0.6` |  |
| `quantization` | `2.0` |  |
| `angle_tolerance` | `22.5` |  |
| `log_epsilon` | `0.0` | -log10(NFA) threshold; used only with advanced refinement. |
| `density_threshold` | `0.7` |  |
| `bins` | `1024` |  |

## Local binary patterns (`inspect.local_binary_patterns`)

Inspection / Texture

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Grayscale or color uint8/uint16, limited to 2.1 million pixels.

Input `mask` — Restrict code centers. Neighbor samples may come from outside the mask.

Input `region` — Restrict code centers. Intersected with a wired mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `method` | `"uniform"` | `default`, `ror`, `uniform`, `nri_uniform` Uniform and ror group rotations. Default and nri_uniform retain direction. |
| `neighbors` | `8` | Value must be a whole number between 4 and 24. Samples per circle. Default and ror support up to 12 to bound bin counts. |
| `radius` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.5 and 32. Sampling radius in pixels. The border is excluded from the histogram. |

## Register rotation and scale (log-polar) (`inspect.logpolar_registration`)

Inspection / Registration

Inputs: `image` (image), `reference` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `moving_mask` (image, optional).

Input `image` — Moving gray/RGB(A)/BGR(A) uint8/16/float32/64 image; owns the result ticket.

Input `reference` — Reference of equal pixel dimensions; owns the aligned coordinate grid.

Input `mask` — Reference-grid verification selection; does not crop the Fourier proposal.

Input `region` — Reference-grid scoring region intersected with the mask.

Input `moving_mask` — Optional captured support on the moving grid for spatial verification.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `work_size` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 64 and 1024. Maximum square canvas; isotropic sampling maps back to original pixels. |
| `angular_bins` | `"720"` | `360`, `720`, `1440` Full-circle polar rows; correlation uses the conjugate-symmetric half. |
| `radial_bins` | `"256"` | `128`, `256`, `512` Log-radius columns before the inner-frequency band is removed. |
| `minimum_radius` | `3.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 256. Inner frequency radius in working DFT bins, at least one. |
| `radius_fraction` | `0.45` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 0.49. Outer frequency radius as a fraction of the square working canvas. |
| `spectrum` | `"log"` | `log`, `magnitude` Log-compressed or linear DFT magnitude for the polar proposal. |
| `spatial_window` | `true` | Apply Hann taper before DFT to reduce image-edge effects. |
| `angular_centering` | `true` | Subtract each radius column's angular mean. |
| `radial_window` | `true` | Apply radial Hann taper; angular coordinates remain periodic. |
| `polar_peaks` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6. Additional polar peaks; each also tests its 180-degree alternative. |
| `peak_radius` | `7` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 31. Wrapped nonmaximum suppression radius in polar correlation bins. |
| `minimum_scale` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0.25 and 4. Minimum moving-to-reference scale; must be below Maximum scale. |
| `maximum_scale` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.25 and 4. Maximum moving-to-reference isotropic scale. |
| `minimum_angle` | `-180.0` | Value must be a finite number between -180 and 180. Minimum moving-to-reference angle in OpenCV degrees, within [-180,180]. |
| `maximum_angle` | `180.0` | Value must be a finite number between -180 and 180. Must exceed Minimum angle; no wrapping interval is inferred. |
| `maximum_translation` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 32768. Center displacement in original pixels; zero uses 40% of size. |
| `minimum_polar_response` | `0.05` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum native polar phase response; not a probability. |
| `minimum_score` | `0.8` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum supported-pixel normalized correlation. |
| `minimum_margin` | `0.03` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Required best-minus-runner-up score for distinct eligible poses. |
| `pose_separation` | `3.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 1000. Merge poses within this mapped-corner RMS distance in original pixels. |
| `minimum_overlap` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum supported fraction of the selected reference scoring region. |
| `minimum_pixels` | `64` | Value must be a whole number between 16 and 1.04858e+06. Minimum supported working-grid pixels for a spatial score. |
| `minimum_stddev` | `1e-05` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum working luminance variation after common-channel scaling. |
| `ecc_iterations` | `60` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 500. Affine ECC iterations before similarity projection; zero disables it. |
| `ecc_epsilon` | `1e-06` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 0.1. ECC convergence epsilon; zero uses iteration count only. |
| `ecc_gaussian` | `5` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 1 and 31. Odd Gaussian kernel for native ECC refinement. |
| `maximum_anisotropy` | `1.05` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 2. Maximum singular-value ratio of the ECC affine proposal. |
| `refinement_failure` | `"reject"` | `reject`, `coarse` Reject failed refinement, or allow verified coarse output. |
| `interpolation` | `"linear"` | `nearest`, `linear`, `cubic`, `lanczos4` Final resampling; spatial verification always uses linear sampling. |
| `border` | `"constant"` | `constant`, `replicate`, `reflect_101` Final image extrapolation; the validity mask excludes extrapolated pixels. |
| `border_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number. Final constant border sample value; ignored by other border modes. |

## Mahalanobis distance (`inspect.mahalanobis_distance`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `model` (data), `samples` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `samples` | `[]` | One feature vector per row. The Samples input overrides this table. |
| `ridge` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+26. Add this value to each covariance diagonal entry before inversion. |
| `check_distance` | `false` | Fail when any row exceeds the distance limit. |
| `distance_limit` | `3.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Maximum Mahalanobis distance for an accepted row. |

## Map strip graphics to source (`inspect.map_strip_graphics`)

Image processing / Compositing

Inputs: `image` (image), `map_x` (image), `map_y` (image), `sampling` (data), `graphics` (graphics).

Input `image` — Original image used to unwrap the strip.

Input `map_x` — Source X coordinates from Unwrap path strip.

Input `map_y` — Source Y coordinates from the same strip.

Input `sampling` — Unwrap path strip sampling data for frame validation.

Input `graphics` — Strip result graphics. Maps outlines, labels, text and point markers.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `sample_spacing` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0.25 and 1. Maximum spacing in strip pixels when projecting outline segments. |

## Compare mask boundaries and area (`inspect.mask_boundary`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `reference` (image), `sample` (image), `image` (image, optional), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `reference` — Expected uint8/uint16 monochrome mask; nonzero is foreground by default.

Input `sample` — Observed mask of equal dimensions; owns output coordinates, ticket and payload.

Input `image` — Optional same-grid display image; preserves original pixels, depth and encoding.

Input `mask` — Measurement-center selection, not a crop or a cut in the target boundaries.

Input `region` — Source-grid region intersected with the optional measurement mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `alignment` | `"same_frame"` | `same_frame`, `pixel_grid` Require matching known frames, or compare already aligned pixels by index. |
| `tickets` | `"sample"` | `sample`, `require_match` Sample owns results. Require match also checks a ticketed reference. |
| `foreground` | `"nonzero"` | `nonzero`, `zero` Interpret both monochrome masks using nonzero or zero samples. |
| `connectivity` | `"8"` | `4`, `8` Inner boundary uses cross (4) or square (8) neighborhood erosion. |
| `image_border` | `"background"` | `background`, `ignore` Assume background outside the image, or ignore unknown exterior pixels. |
| `distance_type` | `"euclidean"` | `euclidean`, `manhattan`, `chessboard` Pixel-center Euclidean, Manhattan, or chessboard distance. |
| `l2_mask` | `"precise"` | `precise`, `3`, `5` Precise L2 or approximate 3/5 chamfer mask. Other metrics force size 3. |
| `direction` | `"both"` | `both`, `sample_to_reference`, `reference_to_sample` Select boundary queries; target boundaries always use the whole image. |
| `statistic` | `"maximum"` | `maximum`, `percentile`, `mean`, `rms` Compare this statistic with tolerance; Both combines directed samples. |
| `percentile` | `95.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 100. Percentile of directed/combined pixel-center boundary distances. |
| `tolerance` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Inclusive distance limit in current-image pixels, also used for area bands. |
| `check_boundary` | `true` | Judge the distance statistic and require valid boundary queries. |
| `check_area` | `false` | Also judge missing/extra pixel counts using the chosen area basis. |
| `area_basis` | `"beyond_tolerance"` | `raw`, `beyond_tolerance` Count all area differences, or only those farther than tolerance. |
| `maximum_missing_pixels` | `0` | Value must be a whole number at least 0. Inclusive limit on expected foreground absent from the sample. |
| `maximum_extra_pixels` | `0` | Value must be a whole number at least 0. Inclusive limit on unexpected sample foreground. |
| `empty_policy` | `"fail"` | `fail`, `both_empty_pass` Fail absent selected foreground, or explicitly accept both empty masks. |
| `graphics_points` | `4096` | Value must be a whole number between 64 and 20000. Maximum outline vertices per layer; measurements use all pixels. |

## Mask clearance at reference (`inspect.mask_clearance`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reference` (image).

Input `image` — Measured 8-bit monochrome mask. Nonzero pixels mark foreground.

Input `reference` — 8-bit monochrome mask of expected sample locations, such as a centerline.

Input `mask` — Limit reference sample locations. Clearance uses the full foreground mask.

Input `region` — Limit reference sample locations. Clearance uses the full foreground mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `minimum_clearance` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Minimum Euclidean distance in pixels from a covered sample center to a background pixel center, including background outside the image. |
| `maximum_clearance` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Maximum accepted distance. Zero disables the upper limit. |
| `minimum_coverage` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum fraction of reference samples covered by foreground. At least one reference sample must be covered. |
| `minimum_in_range_fraction` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum fraction of covered samples within clearance limits. Missing samples are checked separately by coverage. |
| `alignment` | `"same_frame"` | `same_frame`, `pixel_grid` Pixel grid compares equal-size images by index across frame IDs. Use it for an independently registered reference. |

## Multiscale ridge response (`inspect.multiscale_ridges`)

Image processing / Filtering

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Grayscale or color uint8, uint16 or float32. Response uses grayscale intensity units. Requires the morphology install extra.

Input `mask` — Zero both outputs outside the mask while retaining full-image neighborhoods.

Input `region` — Zero both outputs outside the region. Intersected with a wired mask.

Selection writes: `image`, `scale`; outside policy: `zero` (preserve requires matching channels).
Neighborhood operations retain full-image context. See [regions](regions.md).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `sigmas` | `[1, 2, 4]` | One to eight distinct Gaussian scales from 1 to 12 pixels. The scale image reports the strongest response's sigma (zero where response is zero). Sigma is a filter setting, not a direct width measurement. |
| `polarity` | `"dark"` | `dark`, `bright`, `both` Detect dark or bright ridges, or keep the stronger of both. |
| `border` | `"reflect"` | `reflect`, `constant`, `nearest`, `mirror`, `wrap` How the filter samples beyond the image edge. |
| `border_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+12 and 1e+12. Original grayscale intensity outside the frame with constant border mode. |

| `angle_degrees` | `0.0` | Tangent degrees: 0 horizontal, 90 vertical; used for angled. |

| `polarity` | `"either"` | `either`, `rising`, `falling` Along normal (tangent-90) mod 180: right for vertical, down for horizontal. |
| `blur_kernel` | `3` |  |
| `blur_sigma` | `1.0` |  |
| `gradient_aperture` | `"3"` | `3`, `5`, `7` |
| `border` | `"reflect"` | `constant`, `replicate`, `reflect`, `reflect_101`, `reflect_edge` |
| `response_threshold` | `10.0` | Pixel gradient threshold in grayscale intensity/pixel. |
| `strength_statistic` | `"mean"` | `mean`, `median`, `peak` |
| `min_strength` | `10.0` |  |
| `max_strength` | `0.0` |  |
| `min_thickness` | `0.0` | Gradient-band width normal to the fitted edge, not bright-stripe width. |
| `max_thickness` | `0.0` |  |
| `min_length` | `20.0` |  |
| `min_continuous_length` | `20.0` | Longest original connected run, independent of gap joining. |
| `min_intensity` | `0.0` | Mean original grayscale intensity over measured edge pixels (0..255). |
| `max_intensity` | `255.0` |  |
| `max_gap` | `0` | Join separated collinear runs; zero disables joining. |
| `gap_normal_distance` | `2.0` |  |
| `min_coverage` | `0.0` |  |
| `min_contrast` | `0.0` |  |
| `max_contrast` | `0.0` |  |
| `contrast_offset` | `2.0` | Sample this far beyond half band width on each side for contrast. |

## Inspect path continuity (`inspect.path_continuity`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `profile` (data).

Input `profile` — Ordered samples and path length from Path intensity profile.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `channel` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `Gray`, `B`, `G`, `R`, `A`, `H`, `S`, `V`, `L`, `a`, `b` Auto uses the first channel in the path profile. |
| `range_mode` | `"at_least"` | `at_least`, `at_most`, `between` Select the inclusive limits that define an acceptable sample. |
| `minimum_value` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number. |
| `maximum_value` | `255.0` | Value must be a finite number. |
| `maximum_gap` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least -1. Maximum contiguous out-of-range length in path pixels. Negative disables. |
| `minimum_sampled_fraction` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum fraction of path samples with values. Unavailable samples break a gap and are checked separately. |

| `hanning_window` | `false` |  |
| `min_response` | `0.0` |  |

## Iterative phase correlation (`inspect.phase_correlation_iterative`)

Inspection / Registration

Inputs: `image_a` (image), `image_b` (image).

Input `image_a` — Reference image with even width and height.

Input `image_b` — Moving image. Its observed shift is measured relative to the reference.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `precision` | `"float64"` | `float32`, `float64` Floating-point working depth. Color inputs are converted to grayscale. |
| `neighborhood_size` | `7` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 3 and 63. Initial odd neighborhood width (OpenCV L2size). |
| `max_iterations` | `10` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1000. Maximum iterations per native refinement attempt. |

## Prune skeleton spurs (`inspect.prune_skeleton`)

Inspection / Segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `max_length` | `5.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 4096. Endpoint-to-junction length in pixels. Zero keeps all branches. |
| `max_passes` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 32. Recompute branches after each pass. Stops early when no spurs qualify. |
| `connectivity` | `"8"` | `4`, `8` Eight includes diagonals that have no equivalent cardinal path. |

| `super_resolution_model` | `""` |  |

## Read QR codes (`inspect.read_qr`)

Inspection / Code reading

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `minimum_reads` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 4095. |
| `draw_overlay` | `true` |  |
| `backend` | `"standard"` | `standard`, `aruco` Standard preserves existing projects; ArUco uses finder-marker candidates. No automatic fallback. |
| `qr_min_module_size` | `4.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 128. ArUco QR: smallest pyramid module size in pixels. |
| `qr_max_rotation_degrees` | `15.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 90. ArUco QR: relative finder-pattern rotation tolerance in degrees; not label angle. |
| `qr_max_module_size_mismatch` | `1.75` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 100. ArUco QR: allowed ratio between finder-pattern module sizes. |
| `qr_max_timing_pattern_mismatch` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.0001 and 100. ArUco QR: timing-module size mismatch tolerance. |
| `qr_max_penalties` | `0.4` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. ArUco QR: permitted fraction of timing-pattern penalty points. |
| `qr_max_colors_mismatch` | `0.2` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. ArUco QR: relative timing-pattern intensity mismatch tolerance. |
| `qr_scale_timing_pattern_score` | `0.9` | Value must be a finite number between 0.0001 and 1. ArUco QR: timing-score scale used to select the finder-pattern hypothesis. |
| `aruco_adaptive_min` | `3` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 3 and 1001. ArUco backend only. Smallest adaptive threshold window, pixels. |
| `aruco_adaptive_max` | `23` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 3 and 1001. ArUco backend only. Largest adaptive threshold window, pixels; at least the minimum. |
| `aruco_adaptive_step` | `10` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1000. ArUco backend only. Threshold window step; at most 128 window scales per invocation. |
| `aruco_adaptive_constant` | `7.0` | Value must be a finite number between -255 and 255. ArUco backend only. Adaptive threshold subtraction constant in uint8 intensity units. |
| `aruco_min_perimeter_rate` | `0.02` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 16. ArUco backend only. Finder perimeter divided by the image's largest dimension. |
| `aruco_max_perimeter_rate` | `4.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 16. ArUco backend only. Largest permitted finder perimeter ratio; at least the minimum. |
| `aruco_polygon_accuracy` | `0.03` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1. ArUco backend only. Polygon approximation error as a fraction of contour perimeter. |
| `aruco_min_corner_distance` | `0.05` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. ArUco backend only. Smallest corner separation relative to finder perimeter. |
| `aruco_min_border_distance` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 16384. ArUco backend only. Finder-corner exclusion distance from image border, pixels. |
| `aruco_min_marker_distance` | `0.125` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. ArUco backend only. Close-candidate grouping distance relative to finder perimeter. |
| `aruco_min_group_distance` | `0.21` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10. ArUco backend only. Candidate group separation measured in module-size units. |
| `aruco_refinement_window` | `5` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 255. ArUco backend only. Corner refinement half-window, pixels. |
| `aruco_relative_refinement_window` | `0.3` | Value must be a finite number between 0.0001 and 10. ArUco backend only. Refinement window relative to estimated module size. |
| `aruco_refinement_iterations` | `30` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1000. ArUco backend only. Maximum corner-refinement iterations. |
| `aruco_refinement_accuracy` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 100. ArUco backend only. Corner-refinement stopping displacement, pixels. |
| `aruco_border_bits` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 16. ArUco backend only. Finder marker border width in modules; QR finders normally require one. |
| `aruco_pixels_per_cell` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. ArUco backend only. Canonical pixels per finder module for identification. |
| `aruco_cell_margin` | `0.13` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 0.49. ArUco backend only. Fraction ignored on each side of a canonical module; below one half. |
| `aruco_max_border_error` | `0.35` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. ArUco backend only. Allowed erroneous border-bit fraction. |
| `aruco_min_otsu_stddev` | `5.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 255. ArUco backend only. Minimum canonical intensity deviation for Otsu thresholding. |
| `aruco_error_correction` | `0.6` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. ArUco backend only. Fraction of the internal finder dictionary's correction capacity. |
| `aruco_valid_bit_threshold` | `0.49` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. ArUco backend only. Native valid-bit identification threshold (OpenCV 5). |
| `aruco_apriltag_decimate` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 16. ArUco backend only. AprilTag candidate extraction only: quad decimation, zero disables. |
| `aruco_apriltag_sigma` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 16. ArUco backend only. AprilTag candidate extraction only: blur sigma. |
| `aruco_apriltag_min_cluster` | `5` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. ArUco backend only. AprilTag candidate extraction only: smallest connected cluster. |
| `aruco_apriltag_max_maxima` | `10` | Value must be a whole number between 4 and 128. ArUco backend only. AprilTag candidate extraction only: maximum corner maxima. |
| `aruco_apriltag_critical_degrees` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 89.9. ArUco backend only. AprilTag candidate extraction only: critical angle, degrees. |
| `aruco_apriltag_max_line_error` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. ArUco backend only. AprilTag candidate extraction only: largest line-fit mean square error. |
| `aruco_apriltag_min_contrast` | `5` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 255. ArUco backend only. AprilTag candidate extraction only: minimum white/black contrast. |
| `aruco_min_canonical_side` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. ArUco backend only. ArUco3 only: smallest canonical finder side, pixels. |
| `aruco_min_length_ratio` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. ArUco backend only. ArUco3 only: smallest finder length relative to original image size. |
| `aruco_corner_refinement` | `"none"` | `none`, `subpixel`, `contour`, `apriltag` ArUco finder refinement; AprilTag selects its candidate extractor. ArUco3 requires subpixel. |
| `aruco_detect_inverted` | `false` | ArUco backend only: inverted finder markers; no guarantee of inverted QR decoding. |
| `aruco_use_aruco3` | `false` | ArUco backend only: native multi-scale candidate extraction; use subpixel refinement. |
| `aruco_apriltag_deglitch` | `false` | AprilTag candidate extraction only: remove isolated threshold artifacts. |
| `maximum_pixels` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 441 and 6.71089e+07. Reject larger images before grayscale/selection/native decoding; not a native workspace or time bound. |
| `mode` | `"multi"` | `multi`, `single`, `curved` Multi reads all decoded symbols; single chooses one. Curved requires standard. Its native quadrilateral is approximate, not the curved surface boundary. |
| `eps_x` | `0.2` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Standard backend only: horizontal finder-pattern scan tolerance. |
| `eps_y` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Standard backend only: vertical finder-pattern scan tolerance. |
| `use_alignment_markers` | `true` | Standard only: refine QR geometry using alignment markers. |

| `maximum_total_pixels` | `33554432` | Value must be a whole number between 441 and 6.71089e+07. Combined image-group budget checked before any native detection. |
| `maximum_payload_bytes` | `65536` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 65536. Total decoded fragment bytes, including rejected duplicates. |

## Read barcodes (ZXing) (`inspect.read_zxing`)

Inspection / Code reading

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `minimum_reads` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 4095. |
| `draw_overlay` | `true` |  |
| `format` | `"DataMatrix"` | `AllReadable`, `AllLinear`, `AllMatrix`, `AllIndustrial`, `AllRetail`, `AllGS1`, `DataMatrix`, `Code128`, `Code39`, `Code93`, `ITF`, `ITF14`, `Codabar`, `EANUPC`, `EAN13`, `EAN8`, `UPCA`, `UPCE`, `DataBar`, `QRCode`, `MicroQRCode`, `RMQRCode`, `PDF417`, `MicroPDF417`, `Aztec`, `MaxiCode` Limit decoding to a specific symbology or a group of formats. |
| `try_rotate` | `true` |  |
| `try_downscale` | `true` | Also search at reduced image resolutions. |
| `try_invert` | `true` |  |
| `text_mode` | `"HRI"` | `HRI`, `Plain`, `ECI`, `Escaped`, `Hex`, `HexECI` Choose how decoded bytes are formatted as text. HRI formats GS1 data. |
| `binarizer` | `"LocalAverage"` | `LocalAverage`, `GlobalHistogram`, `FixedThreshold`, `BoolCast` |
| `is_pure` | `false` | Use only for an aligned image containing one isolated barcode. |
| `ean_add_on` | `"Ignore"` | `Ignore`, `Read`, `Require` Control reading EAN/UPC supplementary codes. |
| `report_decode_errors` | `false` | Emit invalid detections on the errors port. |

## Measure signal/background contrast (`inspect.region_contrast`)

Inspection / Visibility

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `signal_mask` (image, optional), `signal_region` (data, optional), `background_mask` (image, optional), `background_region` (data, optional).

Input `mask` — Optional valid/work mask applied to both selections.

Input `region` — Optional work region applied to both selections.

Input `signal_mask` — Signal mask; intersects Signal region when both are connected.

Input `signal_region` — Region on the image grid. Use an editable Shape region block.

Input `background_mask` — Background mask; intersects Background region in Explicit mode.

Input `background_region` — Region on the image grid, used only with Explicit background mode.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `channel_mode` | `"gray"` | `gray`, `native` Gray uses floating BT.601 intensity without depth conversion. Native reports every stored channel. |
| `channel` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `Gray`, `B`, `G`, `R`, `A`, `H`, `S`, `V`, `L`, `a`, `b` Channel used for Value and decisions. Auto chooses the first reported channel. |
| `include_alpha` | `false` | Native mode may measure alpha as a numeric channel. Alpha never weights selections. |
| `background_mode` | `"explicit"` | `explicit`, `outside_signal` Explicit requires a background selector. Outside signal uses the remaining work selection. |
| `overlap` | `"reject"` | `reject`, `exclude_signal`, `allow` Reject, exclude signal pixels from background, or allow shared samples. |
| `minimum_pixels` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Minimum final pixel counts. Sample deviation also needs at least two. |
| `deviation` | `"population"` | `population`, `sample` |
| `noise_model` | `"quadrature"` | `quadrature`, `background` Quadrature uses hypot(signal_stddev, background_stddev). Background uses background_stddev. |
| `noise_floor` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Minimum denominator in raw sample units. Zero noise without a positive floor leaves CNR undefined. |
| `metric` | `"cnr"` | `cnr`, `absolute_difference`, `weber`, `michelson` Value is the magnitude of the selected metric. Signed differences and contrasts remain in the report. |
| `check_threshold` | `false` | Require minimum Value and requested polarity on the selected channel. |
| `minimum_value` | `3.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. |
| `polarity` | `"either"` | `either`, `bright`, `dark` Bright/dark checks the sign of signal minus background. Equal means satisfy only Either. |

## Review feature matches (`inspect.review_matches`)

Inspection / Registration

Inputs: `query_image` (image), `train_image` (image), `matches` (data), `fit` (data, optional).

Input `query_image` — Image used by the query feature detector, displayed on the left.

Input `train_image` — Image used by the train feature detector, displayed on the right.

Input `matches` — Paired Query and Train points in image pixels.

Input `fit` — Optional geometric fit made from this same ordered match list.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `show` | `"all"` | `all`, `inliers`, `outliers` Filter displayed pairs by the optional geometric fit. |
| `max_pairs` | `160` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1000. Maximum number of pairs to draw, in the incoming match order. |
| `gap` | `16` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 128. Pixels between the two images. |
| `line_width` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8. Width of the lines in pixels. |
| `endpoints` | `true` | Mark both feature locations on each displayed pair. |

## Rotated rectangle overlap (`inspect.rotated_rectangle_overlap`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `rectangle_a` (data, optional), `rectangle_b` (data, optional), `image` (image, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `a_center_x` | `50.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+07 and 1e+07. |
| `a_center_y` | `50.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+07 and 1e+07. |
| `a_width` | `40.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 3e+07. |
| `a_height` | `24.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 3e+07. |
| `a_angle_degrees` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number. Width-axis angle measured clockwise in image coordinates. |
| `b_center_x` | `60.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+07 and 1e+07. |
| `b_center_y` | `50.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+07 and 1e+07. |
| `b_width` | `40.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 3e+07. |
| `b_height` | `24.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 3e+07. |
| `b_angle_degrees` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number. Width-axis angle measured clockwise in image coordinates. |
| `check_iou` | `false` | Judge overlap using intersection area divided by union area. |
| `minimum_iou` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum intersection-over-union ratio for a passing result. |

## Run-linked contours (`inspect.run_contours`)

Inspection / Shapes

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Nonempty uint8 monochrome mask; every nonzero value is foreground.

Input `mask` — Center mode keeps whole polygons; Clip modifies the search mask.

Input `region` — Optional region, intersected with Mask. Coordinate frames must match.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `min_area` | `100.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Filter by the area of native run polygons, not foreground pixel count. |
| `expected_count` | `2` | Value must be a whole number at least 0. |
| `retrieval` | `"list"` | `list` Flat list only. This tool does not supply hole parents or nested topology. |
| `approximation` | `"simple"` | `simple` Native run endpoints; ordering, winding and hole polygons differ from ordinary Simple contours. |
| `offset` | `[0, 0]` | Whole-pixel [x,y] shift; raster outputs stay on the input grid. |
| `region_mode` | `"center"` | `center`, `clip` Center keeps whole objects; clip measures only their selected pixels. |
| `max_runs` | `250000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2e+06. Reject before the native call when the search mask has too many row runs. |
| `max_contours` | `10000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. Reject larger native results before measuring collections; no truncation. |
| `max_points` | `250000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2e+06. Total native vertex budget before filtering or graphics; no sampling. |
| `draw_overlay` | `false` | Optional color raster; drawable graphics are separate. |
| `draw_mask` | `false` | Raster of selected native polygons; may differ from source pixels. |

## Estimate rolling-shutter motion (`inspect.scanline_motion`)

Inspection / Registration

Inputs: `image` (image), `reference` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reference_mask` (image, optional), `reference_region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Current wobbled capture; same shape, depth and intensity encoding as reference.

Input `reference` — Sharp stable scene geometry defining the output/flow index grid.

Input `mask` — Current-grid track-center support, intersected with Region. Also wire to the warper for donor coverage.

Input `region` — Current-grid track-center selection. Does not crop LK patch context.

Input `reference_mask` — Reference fitting selection; exclude moving parts and retain static texture across rows.

Input `reference_region` — Reference-grid region intersected with Reference mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `degree` | `"3"` | `1`, `2`, `3`, `4`, `5` Polynomial degree of horizontal and vertical displacement versus reference row. |
| `max_features` | `800` | Value must be a whole number between 16 and 5000. Maximum Shi-Tomasi corners detected in the reference fitting selection. |
| `feature_quality` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1. Native minimum corner quality relative to the best corner. |
| `feature_spacing` | `5.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 100. Minimum reference corner separation in current pixels. |
| `tracking_window` | `21` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 3 and 61. Odd LK patch width/height. Selections restrict centers, not full patches. |
| `max_pyramid_level` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 5. Highest LK pyramid level; zero uses the original resolution only. |
| `forward_backward_pixels` | `0.75` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 20. Maximum tracking round-trip error in reference pixels. |
| `max_patch_error` | `25.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 255. Maximum native LK mean patch difference in shared 8-bit working units. |
| `minimum_tracks` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 8 and 5000. Required forward/backward-checked paired corners before fitting. |
| `minimum_training_inliers` | `20` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 5000. Required fitting inliers, excluding every-fourth validation tracks. |
| `minimum_inlier_ratio` | `0.7` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Required fraction of training tracks within Inlier pixels. |
| `inlier_pixels` | `0.75` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 20. Huber scale and final Euclidean training residual tolerance in pixels. |
| `fit_iterations` | `10` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 30. Bounded deterministic Huber iterations, followed by one inlier refit. |
| `minimum_validation_tracks` | `6` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 1250. Required withheld tracks within the validation tolerance. |
| `minimum_validation_ratio` | `0.8` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Required accepted fraction of all withheld validation tracks. |
| `validation_pixels` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 20. Maximum withheld reference-to-image prediction error in pixels. |
| `minimum_row_span` | `0.75` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 1. Minimum height fraction spanned by fitting inliers. |
| `maximum_row_gap` | `0.25` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 1. Largest allowed gap between fitting-inlier rows, as a height fraction. |
| `extrapolation_rows` | `8` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 64. Supported rows beyond the first/last fitting inlier; zero forbids extrapolation. |
| `maximum_motion` | `32.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 4096. Reject a fit exceeding this per-axis displacement on supported rows. |
| `maximum_row_step` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 100. Reject excessive change of displacement between neighboring supported rows. |
| `minimum_vertical_jacobian` | `0.25` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1. Minimum 1 + vertical flow step; protects against row folds. |
| `maximum_work` | `64000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 5.12e+08. Preflight max_features * tracking_window squared * (pyramid level + 1) * 30. |

| `clearance` | `8.0` | Extra margin on all sides of the footprint in local pose pixels. |
| `min_score` | `0.8` |  |
| `target_angle` | `0.0` | Desired image pose angle, counterclockwise in degrees. |
| `angle_tolerance` | `180.0` |  |
| `angle_period` | `"360"` | `360`, `180`, `90` Set 180 or 90 for a part with twofold or fourfold rotational symmetry. |
| `ranking` | `"downstream"` | `downstream`, `score` |
| `downstream_direction` | `[0.0, 1.0]` | Image-space vector [dx, dy], with positive Y down. |

## Selective Search proposals (`inspect.selective_search`)

Image processing / Segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Grayscale, BGR or RGB uint8 image. Output boxes use this image's grid.

Input `mask` — Keep proposals whose centers fall within this optional selection.

Input `region` — Keep proposals whose centers fall within this optional selection.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `mode` | `"fast"` | `single`, `fast`, `quality` Native preset. Quality evaluates more color spaces and strategies. |
| `base_k` | `150` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Initial graph segmentation threshold. Single uses only this value. |
| `inc_k` | `150` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Threshold increment between segmentations in Fast or Quality mode. |
| `sigma` | `0.8` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 5. Gaussian smoothing used by the graph segmentations. |
| `working_side` | `320` | Value must be a whole number between 64 and 640. Downscale the longest side to this limit before generating proposals. |
| `max_proposals` | `200` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 5000. Maximum number of retained boxes after the selection filter. |
| `preview_count` | `25` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 250. Number of retained boxes to draw. Zero disables the overlay. |

## Simple blob detector (`inspect.simple_blob_detector`)

Inspection / Features

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `mask` — Accept only blob centers inside the mask. The source is not clipped.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |

| `min_threshold` | `50.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 255. |
| `max_threshold` | `220.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 256. The threshold sweep stops before this value. |
| `threshold_step` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 256. |
| `min_distance` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 16384. Minimum distance in pixels for treating nearby centers as separate blobs. |
| `min_repeatability` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2560. Minimum observations needed to retain a grouped blob. |
| `polarity` | `"dark"` | `dark`, `light`, `any` |
| `filter_area` | `true` |  |
| `min_area` | `25.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 6.4e+07. |
| `max_area` | `5000.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 6.4e+07. Upper limit for contour area in square pixels, exclusive. |
| `filter_circularity` | `false` |  |
| `min_circularity` | `0.8` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 2. |
| `max_circularity` | `1.01` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 2. Upper limit is exclusive. Set above 1 to accept an exact ratio of 1. |
| `filter_inertia` | `true` |  |
| `min_inertia` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 2. |
| `max_inertia` | `1.01` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 2. Upper limit is exclusive. Set above 1 to accept an exact ratio of 1. |
| `filter_convexity` | `true` |  |
| `min_convexity` | `0.95` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 2. |
| `max_convexity` | `1.01` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 2. Upper limit is exclusive. Set above 1 to accept an exact ratio of 1. |
| `minimum_count` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Minimum detected blobs for a pass. Zero disables the count check. |

## Measure slanted-edge sharpness (`inspect.slanted_edge`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Gray/color uint8 or uint16, or float32 on [0,1]. Original output image is preserved.

Input `mask` — Optional edge support mask; intersects the region. Sampling bands need continuous support.

Input `region` — Optional image-space ROI around one straight edge, with both plateaus included.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `orientation` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `vertical`, `horizontal` Select one near-vertical or near-horizontal straight edge in the ROI. |
| `half_width` | `16` | Value must be a whole number between 4 and 128. Perpendicular sampling distance on each side, in current input pixels; include flat plateaus. |
| `oversampling` | `"4"` | `2`, `4`, `8` ESF bins per pixel. A long slanted edge must populate every bin. |
| `fit_method` | `"huber"` | `huber`, `l2` Native fitLine distance metric for the scanline gradient centroids. |
| `minimum_rows` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 8 and 4096. Minimum number of complete, contrasting scanlines along the edge. |
| `minimum_coverage` | `0.8` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 1. Fraction of selected scanlines required to contain a supported edge. |
| `minimum_contrast` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1. Minimum plateau difference after range normalization and gamma, on [0,1]. |
| `minimum_slant` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 15. Absolute angle from vertical/horizontal; avoid axis-aligned edges with no subpixel diversity. |
| `maximum_slant` | `15.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 30. Maximum absolute slant from the chosen orientation, in degrees. |
| `maximum_fit_rms` | `0.35` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 5. Maximum scanline centroid residual, in current input pixels. |
| `plateau_samples` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 32. Samples at each selected scanline end used to find edge contrast/polarity. |
| `maximum_plateau_drift` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1. Allowed end-plateau drift as a fraction of edge contrast. |
| `window` | `"hann"` | `hann`, `hamming`, `none` Taper the line-spread function before the DFT; keep fixed for comparisons. |
| `derivative_correction` | `true` | Compensate the central-difference frequency response through native Nyquist. |
| `gamma` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 5. Raise normalized luminance to this power before measuring. 1 assumes linear light; this is not an sRGB decoder. |
| `reject_clipping` | `true` | Fail when luminance samples at 0 or full scale exceed the configured fraction. |
| `maximum_clipped_fraction` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Maximum fraction at normalized luminance range limits. |
| `sample_pitch` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-09 and 1e+09. Length per current input pixel at the measurement plane. No calibration is inferred. |
| `length_unit` | `"pixel"` | `pixel`, `mm`, `um`, `in` Frequency outputs are cycles per this unit; sample pitch uses the same unit. |
| `check_threshold` | `false` | Apply an inclusive minimum MTF50, expressed in cycles per selected length unit. |
| `minimum_mtf50` | `0.15` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+09. Inclusive sharpness limit. A crossing above Nyquist is reported as a bound, not invented. |

## Inspect strip width (`inspect.strip_width`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `sampling` (data, optional).

Input `image` — 8-bit monochrome foreground mask. Nonzero pixels are foreground.

Input `mask` — Validity mask from the same strip grid. Zero means the sample is unavailable.

Input `sampling` — Optional Unwrap path strip sampling data for source or physical widths. Also connect its validity mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `center_row` | `-1` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 2.1e+06. Row the foreground run must cross. -1 uses the sampling center row or the middle row of an untagged mask. |
| `minimum_width` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Inclusive minimum. Measured as sample count times cross-strip step. |
| `maximum_width` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Inclusive maximum. Zero disables the upper limit. |
| `enforce_limits` | `true` | Turn off to report measurements and defect classifications without assigning a width verdict. |
| `minimum_valid_fraction` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum fraction of columns that can be classified. A clipped run or a masked-out center is unmeasurable. |
| `minimum_coverage` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum fraction of classifiable columns with a measurable foreground run. At least one run must be measurable. |
| `minimum_in_range_fraction` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum fraction of measured widths within limits. |

## Local structure tensor and orientation (`inspect.structure_tensor`)

Inspection / Features

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Gray/color uint8, uint16, float32/64 up to 2.1 MP; passes through unchanged.

Input `mask` — Optional uint8 output-center selection in the same pixel frame.

Input `region` — Optional circle, rectangle or polygon intersected with the mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `block_size` | `9` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 63. Neighborhood width/height for derivative products; odd sizes are centered. |
| `gradient_size` | `"3"` | `1`, `3`, `5`, `7`, `scharr` Sobel derivative aperture, or Scharr. Output tensors use float32. |
| `border` | `"reflect_101"` | `constant`, `replicate`, `reflect_101`, `reflect_edge` Extrapolation at the full image boundary; masks do not create new borders. |
| `orientation_basis` | `"tangent"` | `tangent`, `normal` Tangent follows an edge; normal follows the strongest gradient axis. |
| `min_energy` | `1e-06` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Require eigenvalue sum strictly above this limit. Integers use full-scale luminance; floats retain their supplied intensity units. |
| `min_coherence` | `0.25` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Inclusive minimum (largest-smallest)/(largest+smallest). Flat/equal-eigenvalue pixels have no direction even when this limit is zero. |
| `glyph_step` | `16` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 256. Spacing of direction marks in input pixels; graphics only. |
| `glyph_length` | `12.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 256. Full length of each unsigned direction mark in input pixels. |
| `max_glyphs` | `1000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2000. Bounded, evenly subsampled marks across the selected sampling grid. |

## Filter candidates by visible support (`inspect.visible_support`)

Inspection / Pick selection

Inputs: `image` (image), `template` (image), `matches` (data), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `template_mask` (image, optional), `template_region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Current inspection image; unchanged on output.

Input `template` — Expected template on the candidate transform's original source grid.

Input `matches` — Template-instance list or pattern_matches_v1; original scores are retained.

Input `mask` — Optional observed source-image support, intersected with Region.

Input `region` — Optional source-image support; excluded samples still count in the denominator.

Input `template_mask` — Optional uint8 expected-object selection; excludes template background.

Input `template_region` — Optional template-grid region, intersected with Template mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `transform_mode` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `transform`, `bounds`, `pose` Auto prefers the record's transform, then bounds, then pose. |
| `template_grid_policy` | `"matching_frames"` | `matching_frames`, `assert_pixel_alignment` Match known frames, or assert template pixel alignment. |
| `template_origin` | `[]` | Pose mode: template [x,y] at the pose origin; empty uses pixel center. |
| `comparison` | `"grayscale"` | `grayscale`, `all_channels` Grayscale BGR weights, or agreement in every BGR channel; alpha is ignored. |
| `interpolation` | `"linear"` | `nearest`, `linear` Sample the scene on the template grid; border fill never adds support. |
| `absolute_tolerance` | `20.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Maximum absolute channel difference, in raw image units. |
| `minimum_visible_fraction` | `0.85` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Matching observed samples / all selected template samples. |
| `minimum_observed_fraction` | `0.95` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Fully observed samples / all selected template samples. |
| `minimum_matches` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 64. Minimum accepted candidates for the block to pass; zero permits none. |
| `maximum_candidates` | `64` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. Reject oversized lists; never silently truncate candidates. |
| `maximum_source_pixels` | `8000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Source image pixel limit before comparison allocations. |
| `maximum_template_pixels` | `262144` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.04858e+06. Full template-grid pixel limit, including masked-out pixels. |
| `maximum_sample_count` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.71089e+07. Full template pixels times candidate count, before sampling. |
| `maximum_working_bytes` | `268435456` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2.14748e+09. Estimated arrays; excludes native workspaces and runtime copies. |
| `maximum_graphics_candidates` | `64` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 64. Limit diagnostic outlines/labels; zero omits drawings. |

## Inspect workplane ellipse (`inspect.workplane_ellipse`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `image` (image), `fit` (data), `scale` (data), `valid_mask` (image).

Input `image` — Rectify workplane image used to measure and draw the ellipse.

Input `fit` — Ellipse from Fit line, ellipse or enclosing circle on this same grid.

Input `scale` — Physical scale from Rectify workplane for the current image.

Input `valid_mask` — Rectify workplane validity mask. The footprint needs complete coverage.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `units` | `"mm"` | `mm`, `cm`, `m`, `in` Expected units of the workplane scale and dimensional limits. |
| `minimum_diameter` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Inclusive minimum of the minor diameter. |
| `maximum_diameter` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Inclusive maximum of the major diameter. Zero disables this limit. |
| `maximum_axis_difference` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Maximum major minus minor diameter. Zero disables this limit. |
| `maximum_fit_rmse` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Maximum contour-to-ellipse RMS error. Zero disables this limit. |

## Inspect workplane rectangle (`inspect.workplane_rectangle`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `image` (image), `rectangle` (data), `scale` (data), `valid_mask` (image).

Input `image` — Rectify workplane image used to measure and draw the rectangle.

Input `rectangle` — Rectangle from Minimum-area rotated rectangle on this same grid.

Input `scale` — Physical scale from Rectify workplane for the current image.

Input `valid_mask` — Rectify workplane validity mask. The footprint needs complete coverage.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `units` | `"mm"` | `mm`, `cm`, `m`, `in` Expected units of the workplane scale and dimensional limits. |
| `minimum_length` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Inclusive minimum of the longer side. |
| `maximum_length` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Inclusive maximum of the longer side. Zero disables the upper limit. |
| `minimum_width` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Inclusive minimum of the shorter side. |
| `maximum_width` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Inclusive maximum of the shorter side. Zero disables the upper limit. |
| `check_angle` | `false` |  |
| `target_angle` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -180 and 180. Long-axis angle from workplane +X toward +Y. Equivalent modulo 180°. |
| `angle_tolerance` | `5.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 90. Maximum unsigned difference from the target long-axis angle. |
| `minimum_orientation_aspect` | `1.05` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 1000. Angle is unavailable at or below this length/width ratio. |

## ALIKED learned features (`ml.aliked_features`)

ML / Feature matching

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — uint8/uint16 or normalized float32 gray/color; original pixels are retained.

Input `mask` — Source-grid center filter after native detection; missing events gate work.

Input `region` — Source-grid shape/Mask intersection; CNN still sees surrounding pixels.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `weights_path` | `""` | Local official 128-descriptor ALIKED checkpoint; no runtime download. |
| `weights_sha256` | `""` | Optional 64-character SHA-256; actual model digest is always reported. |
| `device` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `cuda`, `auto` CPU, explicit CUDA, or Auto; unavailable explicitly requested CUDA rejects. |
| `max_features` | `1024` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Native whole-image top-N maximum before source/ROI/alpha filtering. |
| `detection_threshold` | `0.2` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Native positive threshold-mode peak cutoff; unused in top-N mode. |
| `resize_long_edge` | `0` | Value must be zero or a whole number between 32 and 4096. Zero keeps source size; otherwise only downscale the longest edge. |
| `resize_interpolation` | `"area"` | `area`, `linear`, `cubic`, `nearest` OpenCV resize interpolation; cubic overshoot is clipped to [0,1]. |
| `alpha_policy` | `"exclude"` | `exclude`, `ignore` Exclude zero-alpha centers, or ignore alpha; CNN still sees RGB context. |
| `minimum_count` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 4096. Fewest retained features for a pass; zero permits empty capture. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Bound source pixels before allocation; axes are at most 16384. |
| `maximum_network_pixels` | `524288` | Value must be a whole number between 1024 and 2.09715e+06. Bound replicate-padded network pixels (multiples of 32). |
| `graphics_maximum_features` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Cap optional markers without truncating numeric output. |
| `model_name` | `"aliked-n16"` | `aliked-n16`, `aliked-n16rot`, `aliked-n32` 128-descriptor architecture; t16's 64 descriptors do not fit this matcher. |
| `detection_mode` | `"top_k"` | `top_k`, `threshold` Top-N or threshold mode; the latter can fall back to mean score. |
| `nms_radius` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8. Positive native NMS/soft subpixel radius in resized network pixels. |
| `minimum_score` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Inclusive hard score floor after detection, independent of fallback. |

## Describe box appearance (`ml.box_appearance`)

ML / Object tracking

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `detections` (data).

Input `image` — Current uint8 gray/BGR/RGB/alpha image. Primary pixels remain unchanged.

Input `detections` — Current-grid boxes, blobs or contours. Uses axis-aligned bounds; retains records.

Input `mask` — Intersect ROI support with this current-grid mask and region.

Input `region` — Optional selected support within each box, rather than a center-only gate.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `color_model` | `"hsv_hs"` | `hsv_hs`, `gray` HSV hue/saturation joint histogram or grayscale intensity; uint8 input only. |
| `hue_bins` | `16` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 64. HSV hue bins over [0,180); used only by hsv_hs. |
| `saturation_bins` | `8` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 32. HSV saturation bins over [0,256); product with hue bins at most 512. |
| `gray_bins` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 256. Intensity bins over [0,256); used only by gray. |
| `roi_inset` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 0.49. Fraction removed from each box edge before raster sampling; no crop output. |
| `minimum_pixels` | `16` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1e+06. Minimum selected nontransparent pixels for a descriptor. |
| `insufficient` | `"omit"` | `omit`, `error` Omit appearance (retain box), or reject when ROI support is insufficient. |
| `maximum_boxes` | `128` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 128. Reject excess boxes before histogram work; never truncate. |
| `maximum_sample_pixels` | `2000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Reject excess summed raster ROI pixels, including overlapping boxes. |
