## Decode object detections (`ml.decode_detections`)

ML / Object detection

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `tensors` (data), `preprocessing` (data).

Input `image` — Original image whose source coordinates and ticket own the detections.

Input `tensors` — Single-image tensor_v1 outputs from the matching inference activation.

Input `preprocessing` — Matching inference resize/letterbox maps. Known frame/ticket/recipe mismatches reject.

Input `mask` — Optional image-grid selection; only detections whose unclipped center is selected survive.

Input `region` — Optional region intersected with the mask. Does not crop or alter network inference.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `output_name` | `""` | Exact packed tensor name; empty selects the first. Ignored for ssd_heads. |
| `format` | `"xywh_scores"` | `xywh_scores`, `xywh_objectness`, `xyxy_score_class`, `ssd`, `ssd_heads` Explicit row schema. XYWH scores suits decoded YOLOv8-style heads; objectness suits YOLOv5/YOLOX. SSD supports packed rows or explicitly named separate heads. |
| `boxes_output_name` | `"bboxes"` | SSD heads only: exact boxes tensor, [N,4] or [1,N,4]. |
| `classes_output_name` | `"labels"` | SSD heads only: exact explicit class-ID tensor, [N] or [1,N]. |
| `scores_output_name` | `"scores"` | SSD heads only: exact confidence tensor, [N] or [1,N]. |
| `count_output_name` | `""` | SSD heads only: optional [1] or [1,1] integer count; only the first count rows are active. Empty uses all rows. Storage shapes/row budget are checked first. |
| `box_coordinate_order` | `"xyxy"` | `xyxy`, `yxyx` SSD heads only: XYXY or top/left/bottom/right (YXYX). |
| `layout` | `"rows"` | `rows`, `channels_first` Rows [N,C] or channels-first [C,N], optionally with one leading batch of size one. SSD is fixed. |
| `coordinate_units` | `"network_pixels"` | `network_pixels`, `normalized` Box coordinates relative to the network input; normalized values scale by its width/height. |
| `grid_decode` | `"none"` | `none`, `yolox` YOLOX expands XY offsets and exponential sizes using the exact network stride grids. |
| `strides` | `[8, 16, 32]` | Ascending distinct YOLOX head strides, normally [8,16,32]; all must divide network axes. |
| `score_activation` | `"probability"` | `probability`, `sigmoid` Probability validates [0,1]; sigmoid explicitly converts class/objectness logits. |
| `labels` | `[]` | Optional zero-based string table. Score-head formats require one entry per class channel. |
| `accepted_class_ids` | `[]` | Empty keeps all classes. Otherwise keep these adjusted, zero-based IDs. |
| `class_id_offset` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between -1000 and 1000. Adjustment for explicit-ID SSD/XYXY rows, e.g. -1 for one-based classes. |
| `confidence_threshold` | `0.25` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Keep scores strictly above this value. Objectness heads multiply class and objectness. |
| `objectness_threshold` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Objectness-head gate before combined-score filtering; other formats ignore it. |
| `nms_enabled` | `true` | Disable when the model already performs suppression, or to inspect duplicate proposals. |
| `class_agnostic_nms` | `false` | Otherwise suppress separately per class, including boxes with negative coordinates. |
| `nms_iou` | `0.45` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Native hard-NMS overlap threshold, applied to unclipped boxes in the source grid. |
| `nms_eta` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1. Native adaptive IoU factor in (0,1]; one keeps a fixed threshold. |
| `nms_top_k` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Native pre-NMS score cap per class, or globally in class-agnostic mode; zero is unlimited. |
| `border_policy` | `"clip"` | `clip`, `discard`, `keep` Clip partly visible boxes, discard them, or keep their unclipped coordinates. Fully outside boxes drop. |
| `minimum_count` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Fail the inspection below this many retained detections. |
| `maximum_count` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Zero disables the upper count judgment; positive values bound retained detections. |
| `maximum_raw_rows` | `100000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. Reject larger tensors before row processing; tensor_v1 also caps total values. |
| `maximum_candidates` | `10000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Reject excess valid candidates before quadratic NMS; never silently truncate. |
| `maximum_results` | `1000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Reject excess retained results instead of emitting an incomplete collection. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Reject larger source images before selection; collection axes are limited to 16384. |

## Decode instance segmentation (`ml.decode_instances`)

ML / Instance segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `tensors` (data), `preprocessing` (data).

Input `image` — Original image whose source coordinates and ticket own the detections.

Input `tensors` — Matching detection AND mask/prototype heads; row IDs preserve mask identity through NMS.

Input `preprocessing` — Matching inference resize/letterbox maps. Known frame/ticket/recipe mismatches reject.

Input `mask` — Source-grid selection of mask pixels and/or centers according to Selection mode.

Input `region` — Source-grid circle/rectangle/polygon/raster selection; intersected with Mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `output_name` | `""` | Exact packed tensor name; empty selects the first. Ignored for ssd_heads. |
| `format` | `"xywh_scores"` | `xywh_scores`, `xywh_objectness`, `xyxy_score_class`, `ssd`, `ssd_heads` Detection row schema. Prototype mode appends K coefficients after XYWH class scores/objectness. |
| `boxes_output_name` | `"bboxes"` | SSD heads only: exact boxes tensor, [N,4] or [1,N,4]. |
| `classes_output_name` | `"labels"` | SSD heads only: exact explicit class-ID tensor, [N] or [1,N]. |
| `scores_output_name` | `"scores"` | SSD heads only: exact confidence tensor, [N] or [1,N]. |
| `count_output_name` | `""` | SSD heads only: optional [1] or [1,1] integer count; only the first count rows are active. Empty uses all rows. Storage shapes/row budget are checked first. |
| `box_coordinate_order` | `"xyxy"` | `xyxy`, `yxyx` SSD heads only: XYXY or top/left/bottom/right (YXYX). |
| `layout` | `"rows"` | `rows`, `channels_first` Rows [N,C] or channels-first [C,N], optionally with one leading batch of size one. SSD is fixed. |
| `coordinate_units` | `"network_pixels"` | `network_pixels`, `normalized` Box coordinates relative to the network input; normalized values scale by its width/height. |
| `grid_decode` | `"none"` | `none`, `yolox` YOLOX expands XY offsets and exponential sizes using the exact network stride grids. |
| `strides` | `[8, 16, 32]` | Ascending distinct YOLOX head strides, normally [8,16,32]; all must divide network axes. |
| `score_activation` | `"probability"` | `probability`, `sigmoid` Probability validates [0,1]; sigmoid explicitly converts class/objectness logits. |
| `labels` | `[]` | Optional zero-based string table. Score-head formats require one entry per class channel. |
| `accepted_class_ids` | `[]` | Empty keeps all classes. Otherwise keep these adjusted, zero-based IDs. |
| `class_id_offset` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between -1000 and 1000. Adjustment for explicit-ID SSD/XYXY rows, e.g. -1 for one-based classes. |
| `confidence_threshold` | `0.25` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Keep scores strictly above this value. Objectness heads multiply class and objectness. |
| `objectness_threshold` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Objectness-head gate before combined-score filtering; other formats ignore it. |
| `nms_enabled` | `true` | Disable when the model already performs suppression, or to inspect duplicate proposals. |
| `class_agnostic_nms` | `false` | Otherwise suppress separately per class, including boxes with negative coordinates. |
| `nms_iou` | `0.45` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Native hard-NMS overlap threshold, applied to unclipped boxes in the source grid. |
| `nms_eta` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1. Native adaptive IoU factor in (0,1]; one keeps a fixed threshold. |
| `nms_top_k` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Native pre-NMS score cap per class, or globally in class-agnostic mode; zero is unlimited. |
| `border_policy` | `"clip"` | `clip`, `discard`, `keep` Clip partly visible boxes, discard them, or keep their unclipped coordinates. Fully outside boxes drop. |
| `minimum_count` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Fewest instances remaining AFTER mask area filtering required to pass. |
| `maximum_count` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Most instances after mask filtering; zero disables the upper count judgment. |
| `maximum_raw_rows` | `100000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. Reject larger tensors before row processing; tensor_v1 also caps total values. |
| `maximum_candidates` | `10000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Reject excess valid candidates before quadratic NMS; never silently truncate. |
| `maximum_results` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Reject excess retained results instead of emitting an incomplete collection. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Reject larger source images before selection; collection axes are limited to 16384. |
| `mask_mode` | `"roi"` | `roi`, `roi_per_class`, `prototypes` One ROI mask, class-indexed ROI masks, or packed coefficients plus full- field prototypes. |
| `mask_output_name` | `"masks"` | Exact ROI mask/prototype tensor name; distinct from the detection head. |
| `mask_layout` | `"NCHW"` | `NCHW`, `NHWC`, `NHW`, `CHW`, `HWC` ROI: NCHW/NHWC/NHW, N=raw rows. Prototypes: CHW/HWC or batch-one NCHW/NHWC. |
| `mask_class_offset` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between -4096 and 4096. roi_per_class channel = final detection class ID + offset; unused in other modes. |
| `mask_activation` | `"probability"` | `probability`, `sigmoid` Declared mask probabilities, or sigmoid logits; independent of detection scores. |
| `activation_order` | `"before_resampling"` | `before_resampling`, `after_resampling` Activate the raw mask grid before interpolation, or activate sampled logits afterward. |
| `mask_interpolation` | `"linear"` | `nearest`, `linear` Nearest head cells or native floating linear interpolation with replicated head edges. |
| `mask_threshold` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Mask probability >= threshold is foreground, within source/box/selection support. |
| `crop_to_box` | `true` | Prototypes only: restrict masks to original predicted boxes. ROI masks always use their boxes. |
| `selection_mode` | `"pixels"` | `pixels`, `centers`, `centers_and_pixels` Gate mask pixels, detection centers, or both with the optional mask/region. |
| `minimum_mask_pixels` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1.67772e+07. Drop instances below this selected source-pixel area; zero keeps valid empty masks. |
| `maximum_mask_pixels` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1.67772e+07. Drop larger masks; zero disables this area filter. |
| `label_overlap` | `"detection_score"` | `detection_score`, `mask_confidence`, `score_times_mask` Resolve only the summary Labels image. Separate instance masks retain ALL overlapping pixels. |
| `maximum_mask_elements` | `8000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. Reject larger serialized mask/prototype heads before conversion. |
| `maximum_decoded_pixels` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.28e+08. Bound summed source tiles before resampling, rather than allocate N full- size masks. |
| `maximum_prototype_operations` | `128000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 5.12e+08. Bound retained instances x prototype channels x prototype pixels before mixing. |
| `maximum_region_runs` | `200000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1e+06. Total scanline-run budget across all retained instance masks; never truncate masks. |
| `graphics_maximum_contours` | `2000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 20000. Bound optional outline objects; report truncation without changing masks. |
| `graphics_maximum_points` | `100000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1e+06. Bound optional outline vertices; report truncation without changing masks. |

## Decode keypoints and heatmaps (`ml.decode_landmarks`)

ML / Landmarks

Inputs: `image` (image), `tensors` (data), `preprocessing` (data), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Original source image; pixels/encoding/depth remain unchanged.

Input `tensors` — One object's ordered landmark head from this image's inference activation.

Input `preprocessing` — Matching inference resize/letterbox dimensions and inverse maps.

Input `mask` — Optional source-grid eligibility/filter; a wired missing value prevents execution.

Input `region` — Optional circle/rectangle/polygon/raster selection intersected with Mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `output_name` | `""` | Named float32 model head; empty selects the first output. |
| `format` | `"heatmaps"` | `heatmaps`, `xy_score` One ordered peak per heatmap channel, or explicit ordered X/Y/score rows. |
| `layout` | `"NCHW"` | `NCHW`, `NHWC`, `CHW`, `HWC` Heatmap axes, with image batch N=1 when present. |
| `channels` | `[]` | Ordered distinct channel/row IDs; empty uses all. This can exclude background maps. |
| `names` | `[]` | Optional names in configured channel order; one name per selected landmark. |
| `score_activation` | `"probability"` | `probability`, `sigmoid` Declare probabilities, or convert logits with sigmoid before peak/confidence tests. |
| `minimum_confidence` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Accept confidence >= this threshold; heatmaps use the original integer-peak score. |
| `heatmap_mapping` | `"half_pixel"` | `half_pixel`, `align_corners`, `asymmetric` Declared head-to-network alignment. One-cell align-corners axes use the field center. |
| `peak_search` | `"selection"` | `selection`, `entire_head` Search eligible head-cell centers in source/ROI, or find the whole-head peak then filter it. |
| `ambiguity` | `"reject"` | `reject`, `first` Reject equal global maxima, or explicitly select the first in row-major order. |
| `refinement` | `"log_quadratic"` | `none`, `quadratic`, `log_quadratic` Fit a concave quadratic to local scores or log scores; none retains integer peak cells. |
| `refinement_radius` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 3. Square fit window radius in heatmap cells; source ROI boundaries are respected. |
| `refinement_failure` | `"integer"` | `integer`, `reject` Keep integer position or reject when a requested fit cannot be qualified. |
| `minimum_curvature` | `1e-06` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-12 and 100. Minimum negative Hessian curvature magnitude for an accepted fit. |
| `maximum_refinement_offset` | `0.75` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 3. Largest absolute fitted shift on either heatmap axis. |
| `maximum_fit_rmse` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 100. Maximum fit residual in score/log-score units; zero disables this gate. |
| `coordinate_layout` | `"rows"` | `rows`, `channels_first` XY/score rows [K,3] or [3,K], optionally with leading image batch one. |
| `coordinate_units` | `"network_pixels"` | `network_pixels`, `normalized` Direct coordinates in network pixels, or fractions multiplied by network width/height. |
| `coordinate_origin` | `"pixel_centers"` | `pixel_centers`, `pixel_edges` Direct X/Y denote integer-origin pixel centers or continuous pixel edges. |
| `minimum_count` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1024. Fewest accepted landmarks required for a normal pass judgment. |
| `require_all` | `true` | Also fail if any requested landmark is rejected; identities are never renumbered. |
| `points_policy` | `"complete_only"` | `complete_only`, `accepted_only` Default suppresses Points unless all requested landmarks are accepted; otherwise use Indices. |
| `connections` | `[]` | Optional unique [index,index] pairs in configured landmark order for drawable links. |
| `maximum_tensor_elements` | `8000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. Bound serialized values before conversion. |
| `maximum_landmarks` | `1024` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1024. Bound selected landmarks; raw heads allow up to 1024 channels/rows. |
| `maximum_heatmap_pixels` | `1048576` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4.1943e+06. Bound each head plane, including zero-channel heads. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Bound source image pixels; axes are limited to 16384. |
| `graphics_maximum_landmarks` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1024. Limit optional markers/labels/links without truncating numerical results. |

## Decode prompted masks (`ml.decode_prompt_masks`)

ML / Prompted segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `candidates` (any), `qualities` (data), `prompts` (data), `info` (data).

Input `image` — Exact inference source image; pixels/encoding, current frame and ticket are verified.

Input `candidates` — Three float32 network-grid logit planes from EfficientSAM, preserving their child payloads.

Input `qualities` — Bound quality record, not an arbitrary score list.

Input `prompts` — Exact source-grid prompt record used for this inference.

Input `info` — Matching model/request/preprocessing/source identity from inference.

Input `mask` — Optional source-grid output support; full-image inference and raw prompt membership are unchanged.

Input `region` — Optional source-grid output support, intersected with Mask; the box prompt itself never clips outputs.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `selection` | `"best_quality"` | `best_quality`, `index` Highest predicted quality, optionally preferring consistent point membership, or explicit candidate index. |
| `candidate_index` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 2. Zero-based native candidate index; used only by Index selection. |
| `prompt_policy` | `"require_consistent"` | `report_only`, `prefer_consistent`, `require_consistent` Report only; prefer candidates satisfying point labels; or also fail if no selected candidate satisfies them. |
| `minimum_quality` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+06 and 1e+06. Raw predicted IoU-quality floor; a heuristic, not measured IoU or a calibrated probability. |
| `logit_threshold` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+06 and 1e+06. Foreground is logit >= threshold. Default zero matches the pinned native demo. |
| `resampling` | `"mask_nearest"` | `mask_nearest`, `logits_linear` Threshold on the native grid then nearest resize, or linearly resize logits then threshold. |
| `minimum_pixels` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1.67772e+07. Minimum selected output-mask pixels to pass. |
| `maximum_pixels` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1.67772e+07. Maximum selected mask pixels; zero disables the upper limit. |
| `minimum_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum foreground fraction within selected ROI/mask support. |
| `maximum_fraction` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Maximum foreground fraction within selected ROI/mask support. |
| `emit_region` | `true` | Emit an exact bounded scanline-run region; off avoids region encoding work and emits no Region event. |
| `emit_logits` | `false` | Retain the selected source-grid float32 linearly resized logits. Mask-nearest can differ at boundaries. |
| `maximum_region_runs` | `100000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1e+06. Reject region encoding overflow, never silently truncate. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Source-grid allocation limit; maximum axis is 16384. |
| `graphics_maximum_contours` | `2000` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 20000. Bound optional drawn outlines; numerical masks stay complete. |
| `graphics_maximum_points` | `100000` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1e+06. Bound optional outline vertices; zero suppresses outlines. |

## Decode rotated detections (`ml.decode_rotated_detections`)

ML / Object detection

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `tensors` (data), `preprocessing` (data).

Input `image` — Original image whose source coordinates and ticket own the detections.

Input `tensors` — Single-image tensor_v1 outputs from the matching inference activation.

Input `preprocessing` — Matching inference resize/letterbox maps. Known frame/ticket/recipe mismatches reject.

Input `mask` — Optional image-grid selection; only detections whose unclipped center is selected survive.

Input `region` — Optional region intersected with the mask. Does not crop or alter network inference.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `output_name` | `""` | Exact packed tensor name; empty selects the first. Ignored for ssd_heads. |
| `format` | `"xywh_scores_angle"` | `xywh_scores_angle`, `xywha_scores`, `xywha_objectness`, `xywha_score_class` Explicit center XYWH, angle and scores; scores-angle places angle last. |
| `boxes_output_name` | `"bboxes"` | SSD heads only: exact boxes tensor, [N,4] or [1,N,4]. |
| `classes_output_name` | `"labels"` | SSD heads only: exact explicit class-ID tensor, [N] or [1,N]. |
| `scores_output_name` | `"scores"` | SSD heads only: exact confidence tensor, [N] or [1,N]. |
| `count_output_name` | `""` | SSD heads only: optional [1] or [1,1] integer count; only the first count rows are active. Empty uses all rows. Storage shapes/row budget are checked first. |
| `box_coordinate_order` | `"xyxy"` | `xyxy`, `yxyx` SSD heads only: XYXY or top/left/bottom/right (YXYX). |
| `layout` | `"rows"` | `rows`, `channels_first` Rows [N,C] or channels-first [C,N], optionally with one leading batch of size one. SSD is fixed. |
| `coordinate_units` | `"network_pixels"` | `network_pixels`, `normalized` Box coordinates relative to the network input; normalized values scale by its width/height. |
| `score_activation` | `"probability"` | `probability`, `sigmoid` Probability validates [0,1]; sigmoid explicitly converts class/objectness logits. |
| `labels` | `[]` | Optional zero-based string table. Score-head formats require one entry per class channel. |
| `accepted_class_ids` | `[]` | Empty keeps all classes. Otherwise keep these adjusted, zero-based IDs. |
| `class_id_offset` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between -1000 and 1000. Offset explicit-ID rows only; score-head channels use zero-based IDs. |
| `confidence_threshold` | `0.25` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Keep scores strictly above this value. Objectness heads multiply class and objectness. |
| `objectness_threshold` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Objectness-head gate before combined-score filtering; other formats ignore it. |
| `nms_enabled` | `true` | Disable when the model already performs suppression, or to inspect duplicate proposals. |
| `class_agnostic_nms` | `false` | Otherwise suppress separately per class, including boxes with negative coordinates. |
| `nms_iou` | `0.45` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Unclipped network-rectangle IoU; shared affine maps preserve polygon IoU. |
| `nms_eta` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1. Native adaptive IoU factor in (0,1]; one keeps a fixed threshold. |
| `nms_top_k` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Native pre-NMS score cap per class, or globally in class-agnostic mode; zero is unlimited. |
| `border_policy` | `"clip"` | `clip`, `discard`, `keep` Clip polygons, discard crossings or keep them; invisible boxes drop. |
| `minimum_count` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Fail the inspection below this many retained detections. |
| `maximum_count` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Zero disables the upper count judgment; positive values bound retained detections. |
| `maximum_raw_rows` | `100000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. Reject larger tensors before row processing; tensor_v1 also caps total values. |
| `maximum_candidates` | `2000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Reject excess valid candidates before quadratic NMS; never silently truncate. |
| `maximum_results` | `1000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Reject excess retained results instead of emitting an incomplete collection. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Reject larger source images before selection; collection axes are limited to 16384. |
| `angle_unit` | `"degrees"` | `degrees`, `radians` Units of the model's raw angle column, before direction conversion. |
| `angle_direction` | `"image_clockwise"` | `image_clockwise`, `counterclockwise` Positive image-clockwise angles, or reverse counterclockwise angles. |
| `angle_period` | `"180"` | `180`, `360` 180: unoriented width axis; 360: caller asserts a directed model angle. |
| `nms_method` | `"polygon_iou"` | `polygon_iou`, `opencv` Polygon IoU corrects nested-box overlap; OpenCV preserves native behavior. |
| `maximum_pair_comparisons` | `2000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2e+07. Reject quadratic work above the sum of group n*(n-1)/2. |

## Decode semantic segmentation (`ml.decode_segmentation`)

ML / Semantic segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `tensors` (data), `preprocessing` (data).

Input `image` — Original acquisition image, returned with its pixel format and depth.

Input `mask` — Source-grid output selection intersected with Region after inference.

Input `region` — Optional circle/rectangle/polygon/raster selection in the source image frame.

Input `tensors` — One image's dequantized outputs with explicit axes/score meaning.

Input `preprocessing` — Matching inference sizes and inverse resize/padding maps.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `export_segmentation` | `false` | Emit bounded array-backed class probabilities or decoded labels for tile fusion; off avoids retaining class planes. Class filtering/ROI gates apply. |
| `output_name` | `""` | Named tensor_v1 output; empty selects the first output. |
| `format` | `"multiclass"` | `multiclass`, `binary`, `class_ids` Multiclass exclusive scores, one binary foreground score, or decoded class IDs. |
| `layout` | `"NCHW"` | `NCHW`, `NHWC`, `CHW`, `HWC`, `NHW`, `HW` Explicit axes; N must equal one. HW/NHW denote a single-channel plane. |
| `score_activation` | `"softmax"` | `softmax`, `probability`, `sigmoid` Exclusive logits: softmax. Binary logits: sigmoid. Probability validates input. |
| `resampling` | `"labels_nearest"` | `labels_nearest`, `scores_linear` Nearest winner labels, or linear raw scores before activation and decision. |
| `class_count` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 256. Zero infers channels; binary has two classes. IDs require 1..256. |
| `labels` | `[]` | Class names including background; empty uses numeric IDs. |
| `selected_class_ids` | `[1]` | Integer IDs for Mask/area judgment; empty selects all nonignored. |
| `ignored_class_ids` | `[]` | Integer IDs converted to -1 after winner selection; no runner-up reassignment. |
| `minimum_confidence` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Winning confidence >= threshold; lower pixels become -1. IDs require zero. |
| `binary_threshold` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Foreground probability >= threshold selects class 1; otherwise class 0. |
| `minimum_pixels` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1.67772e+07. Fewest selected pixels to pass; zero allows an empty mask. |
| `maximum_pixels` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1.67772e+07. Most selected pixels permitted; zero disables this limit. |
| `minimum_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum selected pixels divided by the full ROI/mask area. |
| `maximum_fraction` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Maximum selected/ROI fraction; ignored/low confidence stays in denominator. |
| `maximum_tensor_elements` | `8000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. Reject larger heads before conversion; inference has its own output limit. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Source-grid pixel budget; maximum axis is 16384 pixels. |
| `maximum_resample_elements` | `32000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Bound channels x source pixels before allocating mapped scores. |
| `graphics_maximum_contours` | `2000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 20000. Limit optional outlines, including holes; numerical masks remain complete. |
| `graphics_maximum_points` | `100000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1e+06. Limit outline vertices; report truncation without changing the mask. |

## DISK learned features (`ml.disk_features`)

ML / Feature matching

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — uint8/uint16 or normalized float32 gray/color; original pixels are retained.

Input `mask` — Source-grid center filter after native top-N; missing wired events gate work.

Input `region` — Source-grid shape intersected with Mask; descriptors see surrounding pixels.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `weights_path` | `""` | Local DISK depth/epipolar checkpoint; no automatic download. |
| `weights_sha256` | `""` | Optional 64-character SHA-256; actual model digest is always reported. |
| `device` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `cuda`, `auto` CPU, explicit CUDA, or Auto; unavailable explicitly requested CUDA rejects. |
| `max_features` | `1024` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Native whole-image top-N maximum before source/ROI/alpha filtering. |
| `nms_window_size` | `5` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 1 and 31. Odd native local-maximum window in resized network pixels. |
| `detection_threshold` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+06 and 1e+06. Native raw score must be strictly greater; not a probability. |
| `pad_if_not_divisible` | `true` | Pad right/bottom to multiples of 16; outputs exclude padding. |
| `resize_long_edge` | `0` | Value must be zero or a whole number between 32 and 4096. Zero keeps source size; otherwise only downscale the longest edge. |
| `resize_interpolation` | `"area"` | `area`, `linear`, `cubic`, `nearest` OpenCV resize interpolation; cubic overshoot is clipped to [0,1]. |
| `alpha_policy` | `"exclude"` | `exclude`, `ignore` Exclude zero-alpha centers, or ignore alpha; CNN still sees RGB context. |
| `minimum_count` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 4096. Fewest retained features for a pass; zero permits empty capture. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Bound source pixels before allocation; axes are at most 16384. |
| `maximum_network_pixels` | `524288` | Value must be a whole number between 1024 and 2.09715e+06. Bound padded network pixels before dense descriptor inference. |
| `graphics_maximum_features` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Cap optional markers without truncating numeric output. |

## DNN ONNX inference (`ml.dnn`)

ML / DNN inference

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `contract_path` | `""` | Project-relative contract.json; uses its bundled, verified ONNX model. |
| `receipt_mode` | `"summary"` | `summary`, `detailed`, `off` Summary: identity/settings/shapes. Detailed: tensor hashes. Off: no receipt. |
| `fallback_policy` | `"error"` | `error`, `cpu_if_unavailable` Error by default; CPU fallback only for an unavailable requested target. |
| `model_path` | `""` | Local ONNX path; Contract path instead uses its bundled model. |
| `width` | `224` |  |
| `height` | `224` |  |
| `color_format` | `"rgb"` | `rgb`, `bgr`, `gray` |
| `resize_mode` | `"stretch"` | `stretch`, `letterbox` |
| `padding_value` | `0.0` |  |
| `scale` | `0.00392156862745098` |  |
| `mean` | `[0.0, 0.0, 0.0]` | Per-channel values in source intensity units, before scaling. |
| `std` | `[1.0, 1.0, 1.0]` | Per-channel divisors after mean subtraction and scaling. |
| `input_name` | `""` |  |
| `output_names` | `[]` | JSON list; empty selects all unconnected output layers. |
| `engine` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `classic`, `new`, `ort` Classic does not retry with New; non-CPU targets require Classic. |
| `backend` | `"opencv"` | `opencv`, `cuda`, `default` |
| `target` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `opencl`, `opencl_fp16`, `cuda`, `cuda_fp16` |
| `fusion` | `true` |  |
| `max_output_elements` | `1000000` | Total tensor values retained per activation (bounded). |

## Batched ONNX image inference (`ml.dnn_batch`)

ML / DNN inference

Inputs: `images` (any), `masks` (any, optional), `regions` (any, optional).

Input `images` — Ordered image Flow group, for example from Batch serial entries or TIFF pages.

Input `masks` — Optional same-order, same-label mask group; known tickets and grids must match.

Input `regions` — Optional same-order, same-label region group on the corresponding image grid.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `contract_path` | `""` | Project-relative contract.json; uses its bundled, verified ONNX model. |
| `receipt_mode` | `"summary"` | `summary`, `detailed`, `off` Summary: identity/settings/shapes. Detailed: tensor hashes. Off: no receipt. |
| `fallback_policy` | `"error"` | `error`, `cpu_if_unavailable` Error by default; CPU fallback only for an unavailable requested target. |
| `model_path` | `""` | Local ONNX path; Contract path instead uses its bundled model. |
| `width` | `224` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Configured mode: network width. Source mode uses each source width (up to 4096). |
| `height` | `224` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Configured mode: network height. Source mode uses source height (up to 4096). |
| `color_format` | `"rgb"` | `rgb`, `bgr`, `gray` Explicit network channel order; this does not rescale source intensity units. |
| `resize_mode` | `"stretch"` | `stretch`, `letterbox` Stretch axes or retain aspect ratio with centered rounded letterbox padding. |
| `padding_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -3.40282e+38 and 3.40282e+38. Letterbox intensity before normalization. |
| `scale` | `0.00392156862745098` | Value must be a finite number between -3.40282e+38 and 3.40282e+38. Normalization is (pixel - mean) * scale / std, in chosen channel order. |
| `mean` | `[0.0, 0.0, 0.0]` | One value per network channel, in original intensity units. |
| `std` | `[1.0, 1.0, 1.0]` | One strictly positive divisor per network channel. |
| `input_name` | `"image"` | Exact native model image input name; this block binds one NCHW input. |
| `output_names` | `[]` | JSON list; empty selects all unconnected output layers. |
| `engine` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `classic`, `new`, `ort` Classic does not retry with New; non-CPU targets require Classic. |
| `backend` | `"opencv"` | `opencv`, `cuda`, `default` Native backend; the selected target must be available on this installation. |
| `target` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `opencl`, `opencl_fp16`, `cuda`, `cuda_fp16` Native compute target. Non-CPU execution requires the classic engine. |
| `fusion` | `true` | Native layer fusion; disabling requires the classic engine. |
| `max_output_elements` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. Combined retained outputs for the activation, including padding. |
| `input_shape` | `[-1, 3, -1, -1]` | Declared [N,C,H,W]; -1 permits supported variation, never reshaping. |
| `batch_size` | `8` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. Maximum rows per native call, preserving group order across chunks. |
| `tail_policy` | `"variable"` | `variable`, `pad_repeat`, `reject` Variable submits the real tail; Pad repeat copies its last row and discards padded outputs; Reject requires a complete multiple. Padding can affect batch-coupled models. |
| `spatial_mode` | `"configured"` | `configured`, `source` Configured resizes each image; Source keeps dimensions, requiring one size per group. |
| `maximum_images` | `64` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. Maximum group entries. No internal waiting or collection of images. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Per-source pixel guard; excludes runtime copies. |
| `maximum_source_pixels` | `32000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Combined source pixel budget checked before image conversion. |
| `maximum_tensor_elements` | `4000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. Prepared N*C*H*W budget per native call, including padding. |

## Multi-input ONNX inference (`ml.dnn_multi`)

ML / DNN inference

Inputs: `input_1` (data), `input_2` (data, optional), `input_3` (data, optional), `input_4` (data, optional).

Input `input_1` — Connect prepared images or float32 tensor records/maps.

Input `input_2` — Connect prepared images or float32 tensor records/maps.

Input `input_3` — Connect prepared images or float32 tensor records/maps.

Input `input_4` — Connect prepared images or float32 tensor records/maps.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `contract_path` | `""` | Project-relative contract.json; uses its bundled, verified ONNX model. |
| `receipt_mode` | `"summary"` | `summary`, `detailed`, `off` Summary: identity/settings/shapes. Detailed: tensor hashes. Off: no receipt. |
| `fallback_policy` | `"error"` | `error`, `cpu_if_unavailable` Error by default; CPU fallback only for an unavailable requested target. |
| `model_path` | `""` | Local ONNX path; Contract path instead uses its bundled model. |
| `output_names` | `[]` | JSON list; empty selects all unconnected output layers. |
| `engine` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `classic`, `new`, `ort` Classic does not retry with New; non-CPU targets require Classic. |
| `backend` | `"opencv"` | `opencv`, `cuda`, `default` Shared native inference setting. |
| `target` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `opencl`, `opencl_fp16`, `cuda`, `cuda_fp16` Shared native inference setting. |
| `fusion` | `true` | Shared native inference setting. |
| `max_output_elements` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. Total tensor values retained per activation (bounded). |
| `active_inputs` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4. First N slots are required every activation; no last-value cache or partial inference. |
| `primary_input` | `"input_1"` | `input_1`, `input_2`, `input_3`, `input_4` Owns output ticket, spatial/pyramid context and optional preprocessing. Must be Current. |
| `ticket_policy` | `"same_ticket"` | `same_ticket`, `same_value`, `unchecked` Current inputs: full ticket identity, explicit kind/value pairing, or unchecked. Reference inputs are exempt. |
| `maximum_input_elements` | `2000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2e+06. Bound each selected float32 tensor before conversion/allocation. |
| `maximum_total_elements` | `4000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. Combined input element budget; native network workspace is separate. |
| `input_1_name` | `"left"` | Exact model input name; distinct across all active slots. |
| `input_1_shape` | `[1, 3, -1, -1]` | Expected axes; -1 permits variation if the model supports it. |
| `input_1_tensor` | `""` | Empty for a tensor/one-entry map; otherwise select an output name. |
| `input_1_role` | `"current"` | `current`, `reference` Current checks live tickets; Reference allows cached/constant input. |
| `input_2_name` | `"right"` | Exact model input name; distinct across all active slots. |
| `input_2_shape` | `[1, 3, -1, -1]` | Expected axes; -1 permits variation if the model supports it. |
| `input_2_tensor` | `""` | Empty for a tensor/one-entry map; otherwise select an output name. |
| `input_2_role` | `"current"` | `current`, `reference` Current checks live tickets; Reference allows cached/constant input. |
| `input_3_name` | `"aux1"` | Exact model input name; distinct across all active slots. |
| `input_3_shape` | `[1, 3, -1, -1]` | Expected axes; -1 permits variation if the model supports it. |
| `input_3_tensor` | `""` | Empty for a tensor/one-entry map; otherwise select an output name. |
| `input_3_role` | `"reference"` | `current`, `reference` Current checks live tickets; Reference allows cached/constant input. |
| `input_4_name` | `"aux2"` | Exact model input name; distinct across all active slots. |
| `input_4_shape` | `[1, 3, -1, -1]` | Expected axes; -1 permits variation if the model supports it. |
| `input_4_tensor` | `""` | Empty for a tensor/one-entry map; otherwise select an output name. |
| `input_4_role` | `"reference"` | `current`, `reference` Current checks live tickets; Reference allows cached/constant input. |

## EfficientSAM prompted inference (`ml.efficientsam`)

ML / Prompted segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `prompts` (data).

Input `image` — Original uint8 color/gray source. Full-image inference; no implicit ROI crop.

Input `prompts` — Current segmentation_prompts_v1 record; points/background/box and source context are checked.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `contract_path` | `""` | Project-relative contract.json; uses its bundled, verified ONNX model. |
| `receipt_mode` | `"summary"` | `summary`, `detailed`, `off` Summary: identity/settings/shapes. Detailed: tensor hashes. Off: no receipt. |
| `fallback_policy` | `"error"` | `error`, `cpu_if_unavailable` Error by default; CPU fallback only for an unavailable requested target. |
| `model_path` | `""` | Local ONNX path; Contract path instead uses its bundled model. |
| `output_names` | `["output_masks", "iou_predictions"]` | Exactly two terminal names in masks, quality order, for the fixed EfficientSAM ABI. |
| `engine` | `"new"` | `auto`, `classic`, `new`, `ort` Classic does not retry with New; non-CPU targets require Classic. |
| `backend` | `"opencv"` | `opencv`, `cuda`, `default` Shared native inference control. |
| `target` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `opencl`, `opencl_fp16`, `cuda`, `cuda_fp16` Shared native inference control. |
| `fusion` | `true` | Shared native inference control. |
| `max_output_elements` | `4000000` | Value must be a whole number between 3.14573e+06 and 8e+06. Budget must fit three 1024x1024 logit planes plus three quality values. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Source grid limit before 1024x1024 RGB preprocessing; axis limit 16384. |

## Classify image Gaussian mixture (`ml.em_classify_image`)

ML / Image surface models

Inputs: `model` (data), `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — uint8/uint16 or [0,1] float gray/BGR/RGB/alpha; original pixels are retained.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `min_posterior` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum winning component posterior; novelty can still have posterior 1. |
| `check_log_likelihood` | `true` | Use absolute mixture density as an additional unknown-pixel gate. |
| `min_log_likelihood` | `-12.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+12 and 1e+12. Inclusive log density in saved feature units, not a probability. |
| `accepted_components` | `[]` | Empty accepts all; otherwise use canonical model-specific IDs. |
| `max_unknown_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Inclusive unsupported-class fraction among supported pixels. |
| `minimum_support_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum supported/requested fraction after texture erosion. |
| `chunk_size` | `4096` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 65536. Posterior/density batch bound; result maps remain full size. |
| `max_work` | `128000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1e+09. Bound estimated fitting/prediction work before native EM calls. |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `256.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 4096. Estimated array/workspace allowance; not measured process RSS. |

## Train image Gaussian mixture (`ml.em_train_image`)

ML / Image surface models

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — uint8/uint16 or [0,1] float gray/BGR/RGB/alpha; original pixels are retained.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `feature_space` | `"lab"` | `bgr`, `lab`, `gray`, `gray_texture` Normalized BGR/Lab/gray, or local grayscale mean and standard deviation. |
| `texture_window` | `7` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 3 and 63. Odd square neighborhood; requires fully selected visible support. |
| `components` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 16. Unsupervised components, sorted by fitted means; IDs are model-specific. |
| `covariance_type` | `"diagonal"` | `diagonal`, `spherical`, `generic` Native fit structure; spherical uses equivalent diagonal scoring. |
| `max_iterations` | `100` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1000. Native count-plus-epsilon stopping limit, not a convergence guarantee. |
| `epsilon` | `1e-06` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-12 and 1. Native EM convergence tolerance. |
| `max_samples` | `20000` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 100000. Evenly spaced supported row-major pixels; rare surfaces may be missed. |
| `covariance_floor` | `0.0001` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-08 and 1. Explicit post-fit eigenvalue floor in squared feature units. |
| `initialization` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `manual` Native k-means, or Gaussian responsibilities followed by native trainM. |
| `initial_means` | `[]` | Manual: component x feature table in normalized feature units. |
| `initial_covariances` | `[]` | Manual: optional component x feature x feature covariance array. |
| `initial_weights` | `[]` | Manual: optional positive weights summing to one; empty means uniform. |
| `initial_variance` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-08 and 1. Manual covariance default when Initial covariances is empty. |
| `max_work` | `128000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1e+09. Bound estimated fitting/prediction work before native EM calls. |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `256.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 4096. Estimated array/workspace allowance; not measured process RSS. |

## Evaluate image class predictions (`ml.evaluate_classification`)

ML / Evaluation

Inputs: `records` (any, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `classes` | `[]` | Optional class order; observed extra labels are appended. |
| `min_accuracy` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Correct / all labeled images, including rejected/missing/errors. |
| `min_coverage` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Recognized / all labeled images, including wrong recognized labels. |
| `thresholds` | `[]` | Distinct cutoffs; recognized rows need comparable finite scores. Sweeps can further reject recognized rows, not recover upstream rejected candidates. |
| `score_direction` | `"higher"` | `higher`, `lower` Keep >= cutoff for higher, <= for lower; scores are not probabilities. |
| `records` | `[]` | Per-image id, expected, prediction, status, optional score/score_kind. Status is predicted/rejected/missing/error. A validation report may be wired directly. |
| `expected_cases` | `[]` | Full set: id, class_label, capture_group. Missing IDs remain scored. |

## Evaluate image detections (`ml.evaluate_detections`)

ML / Evaluation

Inputs: `records` (any, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `classes` | `[]` | Optional display order. Observed extra labels stay in the report. |
| `iou_threshold` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1. Class-aware score-greedy matching; continuous source XYWH edges. |
| `score_threshold` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+12 and 1e+12. Keep >= cutoff for precision/recall; AP uses all supplied scores. |
| `iou_thresholds` | `[0.5, 0.75]` | Up to ten extra IoU cutoffs for separate AP/precision/recall reports. |
| `score_thresholds` | `[]` | Up to 32 cutoffs; cannot recover detections removed upstream. |
| `ap_interpolation` | `"all_points"` | `all_points`, `101_points` Envelope area or 101 recall samples. Equal-score points grouped. |
| `min_precision` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. TP/(TP+FP). Clean negative-only sets pass without numeric precision. |
| `min_recall` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Operating TP/ground-truth objects. Missing/error images always fail. |
| `records` | `[]` | Records: id, size, truth, predictions, status. Accepts reports/groups too. |
| `expected_cases` | `[]` | Full expected set with detection_truth; absent IDs count as missing. |

## Fuse tile detections (`ml.fuse_tile_detections`)

ML / Object detection

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `detections` (any).

Input `image` — Reference image owning the output grid/ticket; known spatial root and acquisition ticket are required.

Input `detections` — Flat FlowGroup of tile vision_collection_v1 boxes, e.g. Join parallel branches. Each packet must retain its tile spatial frame and the reference ticket/recipe.

Input `mask` — Optional reference-grid center selection, intersected with region; no image cropping.

Input `region` — Optional reference-grid selection using each unclipped mapped box center.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `confidence_threshold` | `0.25` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Scores and threshold are compared as float32, strictly greater than, matching native NMS. Original tile scores remain in source_detection provenance. |
| `accepted_class_ids` | `[]` | Empty keeps all classes. Otherwise keep these adjusted, zero-based IDs. |
| `nms_enabled` | `true` | Disable when the model already performs suppression, or to inspect duplicate proposals. |
| `class_agnostic_nms` | `false` | Otherwise suppress separately per class, including boxes with negative coordinates. |
| `nms_eta` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1. Native adaptive IoU factor in (0,1]; one keeps a fixed threshold. |
| `nms_top_k` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Native pre-NMS score cap per class, or globally in class-agnostic mode; zero is unlimited. |
| `border_policy` | `"clip"` | `clip`, `discard`, `keep` Clip partly visible boxes, discard them, or keep their unclipped coordinates. Fully outside boxes drop. |
| `minimum_count` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Fail the inspection below this many retained detections. |
| `maximum_count` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100000. Zero disables the upper count judgment; positive values bound retained detections. |
| `maximum_results` | `1000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Reject excess retained results instead of emitting an incomplete collection. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Reject larger source images before selection; collection axes are limited to 16384. |
| `expected_tiles` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 64. Zero accepts any complete supplied group. Positive values require this many distinct tile labels; this does not prove full image coverage. |
| `box_source` | `"bbox"` | `bbox`, `unclipped` Use each local bbox, or explicitly require geometry.unclipped_xyxy from every detection. No partial-object reconstruction or weighted averaging. |
| `nms_iou` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Native hard-NMS overlap threshold, applied to unclipped boxes in the source grid. |
| `edge_policy` | `"keep"` | `keep`, `drop_internal` Keep all boxes, or discard those touching internal tile edges before NMS. Reference outer edges are exempt; sufficient tile overlap remains necessary. |
| `edge_margin` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 16384. Local tile pixels around an internal edge. Only used by drop_internal; must leave a nonempty tile interior. |
| `include_candidate_details` | `false` | Include a bounded per-detection mapping/filter/NMS trace. Off avoids optional report overhead. |
| `maximum_raw_detections` | `10000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 50000. Reject excess total detections across tiles before mapping and suppression. |
| `maximum_candidates` | `4096` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Reject excess score/class/region/border-selected candidates before quadratic native NMS; never silently truncate. |

## Fuse tile segmentation (`ml.fuse_tile_segmentation`)

ML / Semantic segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `tiles` (any).

Input `image` — Reference image; requires a known spatial root and acquisition ticket.

Input `mask` — Optional reference-grid ROI; intersects Region.

Input `region` — Reference-grid rectangle/circle/polygon/raster selection.

Input `tiles` — Flat FlowGroup from Join of matching exported segmentation records. Shared ticket, root, class order and recipe required; crop/scale/flip/right-angle maps.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `selected_class_ids` | `[1]` | Integer IDs for Mask/area judgment; empty selects all nonignored. |
| `ignored_class_ids` | `[]` | Integer IDs converted to -1 after winner selection; no runner-up reassignment. |
| `minimum_confidence` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum winning probability, or vote fraction in label mode. |
| `minimum_pixels` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1.67772e+07. Fewest selected pixels to pass; zero allows an empty mask. |
| `maximum_pixels` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1.67772e+07. Most selected pixels permitted; zero disables this limit. |
| `minimum_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum selected pixels divided by the full ROI/mask area. |
| `maximum_fraction` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Maximum selected/ROI fraction; ignored/low confidence stays in denominator. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Source-grid pixel budget; maximum axis is 16384 pixels. |
| `graphics_maximum_contours` | `2000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 20000. Limit optional outlines, including holes; numerical masks remain complete. |
| `graphics_maximum_points` | `100000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1e+06. Limit outline vertices; report truncation without changing the mask. |
| `export_segmentation` | `false` | Emit bounded array-backed class probabilities or decoded labels for tile fusion; off avoids retaining class planes. Class filtering/ROI gates apply. |
| `mode` | `"probability_mean"` | `probability_mean`, `label_vote` Average exclusive class probabilities with bilinear masked sampling, or vote on nearest decoded labels. Ties choose the lowest class ID. |
| `weighting` | `"uniform"` | `uniform`, `feather` Uniform support or distance-to-tile-edge feathering, in tile pixels. |
| `feather_width` | `16.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.5 and 16384. Distance in tile pixels from its boundary to full feather weight. |
| `expected_tiles` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 64. Zero accepts 1..64 distinct tiles; positive requires exact count. |
| `minimum_coverage` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Required valid tile support / reference ROI pixels before the final class/confidence gate; empty ROI has zero coverage. |
| `maximum_input_elements` | `32000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Bound total tile label and class-plane samples. |
| `maximum_resample_elements` | `8000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 3.2e+07. Bound classes x reference pixels before accumulation. |
| `maximum_mapped_elements` | `64000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2.56e+08. Conservative tiles x classes x reference pixels work limit. |

## Image to DNN tensor (`ml.image_tensor`)

ML / Tensor preparation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Original ticketed image; returned unchanged with source coordinates.

Input `mask` — Excluded source samples become zero before resize/normalization.

Input `region` — Intersect with the mask on the source grid; keep original dimensions.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `width` | `224` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Network image width after resize, pixels. |
| `height` | `224` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Network image height, pixels. |
| `color_format` | `"rgb"` | `rgb`, `bgr`, `gray` Explicit network channel order; this does not rescale source intensity units. |
| `resize_mode` | `"stretch"` | `stretch`, `letterbox` Stretch axes or retain aspect ratio with centered rounded letterbox padding. |
| `padding_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -3.40282e+38 and 3.40282e+38. Letterbox intensity before normalization. |
| `scale` | `0.00392156862745098` | Value must be a finite number between -3.40282e+38 and 3.40282e+38. Normalization is (pixel - mean) * scale / std, in chosen channel order. |
| `mean` | `[0.0, 0.0, 0.0]` | One value per network channel, in original intensity units. |
| `std` | `[1.0, 1.0, 1.0]` | One strictly positive divisor per network channel. |
| `layout` | `"nchw"` | `nchw`, `nhwc` NCHW [1,C,H,W] or NHWC [1,H,W,C]; never guessed from the model. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Source pixel guard before conversion; graph copies are separate. |
| `maximum_elements` | `2000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2e+06. Prepared tensor budget checked before resizing/allocation. |

## K-means samples (`ml.kmeans`)

ML / Clustering

Inputs: `samples` (data, optional), `initial_labels` (data, optional).

Input `initial_labels` — Optional list of zero-based labels for the first attempt.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `clusters` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 32. Must be no greater than the number of samples used in this run. |
| `initialization` | `"kmeans_plus_plus"` | `kmeans_plus_plus`, `random` |
| `attempts` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 20. Returns the fit with the lowest sum of squared distances. |
| `termination` | `"both"` | `both`, `iterations`, `epsilon` |
| `max_iterations` | `50` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 100. Used by the both and iterations modes. |
| `epsilon` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Center movement tolerance in feature units, used by both and epsilon modes. |
| `samples` | `[]` | One feature vector per row. The samples input overrides this table. |
| `plot_x` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 127. Zero-based feature index for the plot's horizontal axis. |
| `plot_y` | `-1` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 127. -1 chooses the first other feature or a one-dimensional strip. |
| `draw_plot` | `true` |  |

Inputs: `model` (data), `samples` (data).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |

| `max_distance` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+15. Euclidean distance in the saved working feature space; 0 disables the cutoff. Positive limits are inclusive. This is not a probability. |
| `max_unknown_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Allowed fraction rejected by distance or accepted-cluster filters. Exceeding it is an inspection failure, not a processing exception. |
| `accepted_clusters` | `[]` | Stored zero-based IDs to accept; [] accepts all. IDs do not have semantic names and stay fixed only within the same model revision. |

| `initialization` | `"kmeans_plus_plus"` | `kmeans_plus_plus`, `random` |
| `attempts` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 20. Returns the fit with the lowest sum of squared distances. |
| `termination` | `"both"` | `both`, `iterations`, `epsilon` |

## Predict OpenCV K-nearest classes (`ml.knn_predict`)

ML / Classical classification

Inputs: `model` (data), `samples` (data), `image` (image, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `k` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 64. 0 uses the stored model default; 1..64 overrides it. Must not exceed training rows. |
| `contract_policy` | `"require_match"` | `require_match`, `dimensions_only` Require exact descriptor preprocessing, or explicitly check dimensions only. Stored normalization always applies. |
| `max_distance` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+18. Inclusive Euclidean limit for the selected neighbor-distance statistic. 0 disables distance rejection. Native squared distances are also reported. |
| `distance_policy` | `"winning_class_nearest"` | `winning_class_nearest`, `nearest`, `mean`, `kth` Nearest neighbor of the voted class, nearest of all neighbors, mean Euclidean distance of all K, or Kth/farthest returned neighbor. |
| `min_vote_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum fraction of K neighbors voting for the native candidate. This is vote agreement, not a calibrated probability. |
| `reject_vote_ties` | `true` | Withhold recognized labels when two or more classes tie for most votes. Raw candidate and neighbor diagnostics remain available. |
| `accepted_labels` | `[]` | Optional allowed trained classes for inspection pass/fail; [] accepts all recognized classes. A disallowed class is still a recognized label. |

## Train OpenCV K-nearest classifier (`ml.knn_train`)

ML / Classical classification

Inputs: `samples` (any), `labels` (data, optional).

Input `samples` — Numeric feature rows or a batch of image descriptor vectors. Known preprocessing contracts must agree.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `k` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. Stored default neighbor count; must not exceed the number of training rows. Prediction can explicitly override it. No silent native clamping. |
| `normalization` | `"none"` | `none`, `l2` None or per-row L2; saved rows are already normalized and the same normalization is applied to incoming descriptors. Zero vectors cannot use L2. |
| `labels` | `[]` | One string per row, in training/batch order. Wired Labels overrides this list. Native vote ties prefer the first-appearing encoded class; prediction rejects ties by default. Stored models include the descriptor rows, not just a summary. |

## Pose from ordered landmarks (`ml.landmark_pose`)

ML / Landmarks

Inputs: `image` (image), `landmarks` (data).

Input `image` — Target image; shared-root transforms map the source landmark frame when needed.

Input `landmarks` — Matching image_landmarks_v1 data with stable indices and source context.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `origin_index` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1023. Stable configured landmark index, including rejected/missing entries. |
| `angle_mode` | `"pair"` | `pair`, `fixed` Pair uses origin-to-direction axis; fixed uses counterclockwise angle. |
| `direction_index` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1023. Second accepted landmark defining the local positive x direction. |
| `angle_degrees` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+06 and 1e+06. Fixed counterclockwise degrees, in the source landmark image grid. |
| `minimum_baseline` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Minimum source-pixel separation before a landmark pair defines an axis. |
| `pose_offset_x` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+06 and 1e+06. Local x offset in feature/model pixels; follows rotation and scale. |
| `pose_offset_y` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+06 and 1e+06. Local y offset in feature/model pixels; follows rotation and scale. |
| `pose_offset_angle` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+06 and 1e+06. Local counterclockwise rotation in degrees (OpenCV convention). |

## LightGlue ALIKED feature matcher (`ml.lightglue_aliked`)

ML / Feature matching

Inputs: `image` (image), `query_features` (data), `reference_features` (data).

Input `image` — Current query image for source coordinates, unchanged output and diagnostics.

Input `query_features` — Current-image ALIKED features with 128-value descriptors.

Input `reference_features` — Captured ALIKED reference; may use a different acquisition ticket.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `weights_path` | `""` | Local aliked_lightglue.pth matcher; other descriptor families reject. |
| `device` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `cuda`, `auto` CPU, explicitly requested CUDA, or Auto; no unavailable CUDA fallback. |
| `layers` | `9` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 9. 1..9 pretrained layers; fewer layers trade accuracy for latency. |
| `filter_threshold` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Learned match confidence threshold in [0,1]. |
| `depth_confidence` | `0.95` | Early stopping confidence, or -1 to disable. |
| `width_confidence` | `0.99` | Point pruning confidence, or -1 to disable. |
| `flash` | `true` | Use the upstream attention acceleration when available. |
| `mixed_precision` | `false` | CUDA mixed precision; CPU requires this disabled. |
| `minimum_matches` | `8` | Value must be a whole number between 4 and 4096. Minimum learned correspondences for a passing geometric fit. |
| `minimum_inliers` | `6` | Value must be a whole number between 4 and 4096. Minimum geometric inliers for a passing fit. |
| `minimum_inlier_ratio` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Required inlier fraction among learned matches. |
| `geometry` | `"ransac"` | `ransac`, `lmeds`, `rho` Native robust homography method; one planar registration only. |
| `ransac_threshold` | `3.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.0001 and 1e+06. Geometric reprojection threshold in query source pixels. |
| `ransac_confidence` | `0.995` | Value must be a finite number between 0.0001 and 0.999999. Robust-fit confidence, strictly below one. |
| `ransac_iterations` | `2000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. Maximum native robust-fit iterations. |
| `weights_sha256` | `""` | Optional 64-character SHA-256 for the local matcher checkpoint. |
| `maximum_features` | `1024` | Value must be a whole number between 4 and 4096. Bound each descriptor set before learned attention; no truncation. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Bound current query image pixels before diagnostics. |
