## Create float32 tensor (`ml.make_tensor`)

ML / Tensor preparation

Inputs: `data` (data, optional).

Input `data` — Optional scalar or flat numeric list, replacing the Values parameter.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `shape` | `[1, 3]` | Explicit positive axes. Their product must equal the flat value count. |
| `values` | `[1.0, 1.0, 1.0]` | Fallback scalar/flat numeric list when Data is absent; converted to finite float32. |
| `maximum_elements` | `2000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2e+06. Bound values before array allocation; graph snapshots are separate. |

## Estimate monocular depth (`ml.monocular_depth`)

ML / Depth estimation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Original uint8/uint16 or float32 [0,1]; returned unchanged.

Input `mask` — Write selection. Excluded source pixels remain model context.

Input `region` — Intersects Mask; known coordinate-frame conflicts fail.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `contract_path` | `""` | Project-relative contract.json; uses its bundled, verified ONNX model. |
| `receipt_mode` | `"summary"` | `summary`, `detailed`, `off` Summary: identity/settings/shapes. Detailed: tensor hashes. Off: no receipt. |
| `fallback_policy` | `"error"` | `error`, `cpu_if_unavailable` Error by default; CPU fallback only for an unavailable requested target. |
| `model_path` | `""` | Local ONNX or sealed Contract path; no implicit download or calibration. |
| `input_name` | `"input"` |  |
| `output_names` | `[]` |  |
| `width` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. |
| `height` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. |
| `color_format` | `"rgb"` | `rgb`, `bgr`, `gray` |
| `resize_mode` | `"stretch"` | `stretch`, `letterbox` Rounded letterbox or stretch with explicit half-pixel centers. |
| `input_interpolation` | `"cubic"` | `linear`, `cubic`, `area` |
| `padding_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. |
| `mean` | `[0.0, 0.0, 0.0]` | Per-channel normalized source values, subtracted before Std division. |
| `std` | `[1.0, 1.0, 1.0]` | Positive per-channel divisors; avoid duplicating embedded normalization. |
| `layout` | `"NHW"` | `NHW`, `NCHW`, `NHWC`, `HW`, `CHW`, `HWC` |
| `convention` | `"relative_inverse_depth"` | `relative_inverse_depth`, `relative_depth`, `metric_depth`, `metric_inverse_depth` Declare model meaning. Relative outputs do not emit physical Depth. |
| `head_scale` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+09 and 1e+09. Fixed nonzero affine scale before resampling; never fitted per image. |
| `head_offset` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+09 and 1e+09. Fixed affine offset; display normalization never changes model values. |
| `units` | `"m"` | `m`, `mm` Metric Depth units; metric-inverse model values use their reciprocal. |
| `minimum_model_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+12. Affine values at/below this bound are invalid; zero is reserved. |
| `maximum_model_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+12. Optional inclusive affine upper bound; zero disables. |
| `minimum_depth` | `0.001` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+12. Metric lower bound after inversion; values must also be positive. |
| `maximum_depth` | `100.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-12 and 1e+12. Metric upper bound; outside values become invalid, not clipped. |
| `resampling` | `"linear"` | `nearest`, `linear` Interpolate affine model values before inverse-metric conversion. |
| `minimum_valid_pixels` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4e+06. |
| `minimum_valid_fraction` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Required selected-pixel coverage; not a confidence score. |
| `export_estimate` | `true` | Emit JSON quantity map; disable to avoid per-pixel list overhead. |
| `preview_mode` | `"auto_percentile"` | `auto_percentile`, `fixed` Display-only stretch. Numerical outputs never normalize per image. |
| `display_min` | `0.0` |  |
| `display_max` | `1.0` |  |
| `engine` | `"classic"` | `auto`, `classic`, `new`, `ort` |
| `backend` | `"opencv"` | `opencv`, `cuda`, `default` |
| `target` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `opencl`, `opencl_fp16`, `cuda`, `cuda_fp16` |
| `fusion` | `true` |  |
| `maximum_source_pixels` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4e+06. Source-grid resampling/map bound before model I/O. |
| `maximum_input_elements` | `3000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.6e+07. Configured input tensor bound before allocation. |
| `max_output_elements` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. |

## Learned optical flow (`ml.optical_flow`)

ML / Motion estimation

Inputs: `first` (image), `second` (image), `first_mask` (image, optional), `second_mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `first` — First frame; owns returned image/grid/ticket. Vectors map first to second.

Input `second` — Equal-size second frame. Swap inputs for reverse flow; do not negate vectors.

Input `first_mask` — First-grid write selection; excluded pixels remain model context.

Input `second_mask` — Support at first+flow; all bilinear contributors must be valid.

Input `region` — First-grid selection, intersected with First mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `contract_path` | `""` | Project-relative contract.json; uses its bundled, verified ONNX model. |
| `receipt_mode` | `"summary"` | `summary`, `detailed`, `off` Summary: identity/settings/shapes. Detailed: tensor hashes. Off: no receipt. |
| `fallback_policy` | `"error"` | `error`, `cpu_if_unavailable` Error by default; CPU fallback only for an unavailable requested target. |
| `model_path` | `""` |  |
| `first_name` | `"first"` | First binding, or combined binding with first channels then second. |
| `second_name` | `"second"` | Distinct second binding for Named mode. |
| `output_names` | `[]` |  |
| `input_mode` | `"named"` | `named`, `concatenated` |
| `input_layout` | `"NCHW"` | `NCHW`, `NHWC` |
| `layout` | `"NCHW"` | `NCHW`, `NHWC`, `CHW`, `HWC` |
| `width` | `480` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. |
| `height` | `360` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. |
| `color_format` | `"rgb"` | `rgb`, `bgr`, `gray` |
| `resize_mode` | `"stretch"` | `stretch`, `letterbox` |
| `input_interpolation` | `"linear"` | `linear`, `cubic`, `area` |
| `padding_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. |
| `mean` | `[0.0, 0.0, 0.0]` | Per-frame means in unit source values; subtract before Std division. |
| `std` | `[1.0, 1.0, 1.0]` | Positive channel divisors; RAFT's raw RGB model uses 1/255 on unit RGB. |
| `component_order` | `"xy"` | `xy`, `yx` Native channels encode xy or yx; output is always dx,dy. |
| `head_scale` | `[1.0, 1.0]` | Two fixed nonzero multipliers in decoded xy order, before resampling. |
| `head_offset` | `[0.0, 0.0]` | Two finite offsets in decoded xy order and declared head units. |
| `head_units` | `"network_pixels"` | `network_pixels`, `head_pixels`, `source_pixels` Vector units before per-axis conversion to original first-image pixels. |
| `invalid_value_enabled` | `false` |  |
| `invalid_head_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+12 and 1e+12. Either component equal to this enabled sentinel invalidates both. |
| `maximum_motion` | `4096.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 16384. Per-axis source-pixel bound; reject vectors rather than clipping them. |
| `alignment` | `"pixel_grid"` | `pixel_grid`, `same_frame` Same frame rejects known grid conflicts; Pixel grid declares correspondence. |
| `resampling` | `"linear"` | `linear`, `nearest` |
| `check_correspondence` | `true` | Require first+flow inside second; Second mask always enforces it. |
| `bind_image_content` | `true` | Bind exact images/grids and flow direction for existing consumers. |
| `minimum_valid_pixels` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4e+06. |
| `minimum_valid_fraction` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Selected support coverage, not learned confidence or accuracy. |
| `visual_max_motion` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+06. Magnitude for full display brightness; never clips numerical flow. |
| `arrow_spacing` | `24` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1024. Requested drawing stride, increased automatically to limit 2,048 arrows. |
| `engine` | `"classic"` | `auto`, `classic`, `new`, `ort` |
| `backend` | `"opencv"` | `opencv`, `cuda`, `default` |
| `target` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `opencl`, `opencl_fp16`, `cuda`, `cuda_fp16` |
| `fusion` | `true` |  |
| `maximum_source_pixels` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2.1e+06. |
| `maximum_input_elements` | `3000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.6e+07. Combined two-frame tensor budget before conversion/allocation. |
| `max_output_elements` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. |

## Pack separate detection heads (`ml.pack_detection_heads`)

ML / Instance segmentation

Inputs: `tensors` (data).

Input `tensors` — One inference activation's boxes/scores/classes and optional mask heads.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `boxes_output` | `"boxes"` | Named XYXY [N,4] or [1,N,4] head, in the export's coordinate units. |
| `scores_output` | `"scores"` | Named [N] scores with optional singleton axes; probabilities in [0,1]. |
| `classes_output` | `"labels"` | Named [N] nonnegative integer class IDs, with optional singleton axes. |
| `output_name` | `"packed_boxes"` | New [1,N,6] XYXY/score/class head; retain original heads. |
| `maximum_rows` | `10000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. Reject more boxes before packing. |
| `maximum_total_elements` | `8000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. Bound original heads plus the packed head before copying. |

## Pack SuperPoint feature tensors (`ml.pack_superpoint_features`)

ML / Feature matching

Inputs: `image` (image), `tensors` (data), `preprocessing` (data), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Original source image, retained with its depth, format and current ticket.

Input `tensors` — Explicit exported SuperPoint heads; this block does not run a detector.

Input `preprocessing` — Matching inference resize/letterbox inverse and source/network sizes.

Input `mask` — Optional source-grid selection; a connected missing event prevents work.

Input `region` — Optional source-grid shape intersected with Mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `points_output` | `"keypoints"` | Named float32 [K,2] XY tensor, optionally preceded by image batch one. |
| `descriptors_output` | `"descriptors"` | Named float32 [K,256] or [256,K] tensor, optional image batch one. |
| `scores_output` | `"scores"` | Float32 [K] or [1,K] probabilities; blank explicitly uses score one. |
| `descriptor_layout` | `"rows"` | `rows`, `channels_first` Descriptor rows or channels first; never inferred from dimensions. |
| `coordinate_units` | `"network_pixels"` | `network_pixels`, `normalized` Network pixels or fractions multiplied by full network dimensions. |
| `coordinate_origin` | `"pixel_centers"` | `pixel_centers`, `pixel_edges` Direct XY denote integer-origin centers or continuous pixel edges. |
| `minimum_score` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Keep scores >= this threshold, then source/ROI filtering. |
| `normalize_descriptors` | `true` | L2-normalize nonzero rows; otherwise verify existing unit norms. |
| `minimum_count` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 4096. Fewest retained features required for a normal inspection pass. |
| `maximum_features` | `1024` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Bound raw rows before filtering; never truncate correspondences. |
| `maximum_tensor_elements` | `2000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2e+06. Bound each serialized head before conversion. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Bound source pixels; source axes are limited to 16384. |
| `graphics_maximum_features` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Bound optional markers without changing numeric results. |

## Apply image color palette (`ml.palette_predict`)

ML / Clustering

Inputs: `model` (data), `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `max_distance` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+15. Euclidean distance in the saved working feature space; 0 disables the cutoff. Positive limits are inclusive. This is not a probability. |
| `max_unknown_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Allowed fraction rejected by distance or accepted-cluster filters. Exceeding it is an inspection failure, not a processing exception. |
| `accepted_clusters` | `[]` | Stored zero-based IDs to accept; [] accepts all. IDs do not have semantic names and stay fixed only within the same model revision. |

## Train image color palette (`ml.palette_train`)

ML / Clustering

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — uint8/uint16 or normalized float gray/BGR/RGB/BGRA/RGBA; primary pixels and alpha remain unchanged.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `clusters` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 32. Must be no greater than the number of samples used in this run. |
| `initialization` | `"kmeans_plus_plus"` | `kmeans_plus_plus`, `random` |
| `attempts` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 20. Returns the fit with the lowest sum of squared distances. |
| `termination` | `"both"` | `both`, `iterations`, `epsilon` |
| `max_iterations` | `50` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 100. Used by the both and iterations modes. |
| `epsilon` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Center movement tolerance in feature units, used by both and epsilon modes. |
| `color_space` | `"lab"` | `bgr`, `lab`, `gray` Fixed normalized BGR/gray or Lab (L/100, a/128, b/128). No automatic white balance. Stored and reused on other depths/encodings. |
| `max_samples` | `20000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. Evenly spaced selected pixels in row-major order, not random sampling. Raise for rare colors. Transparent pixels are excluded. |

## Recognize constrained text regions (`ml.recognize_constrained_text`)

ML / Text inspection

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `regions` (data, optional).

Input `image` — One source acquisition; output unchanged with format/depth/ticket/frame.

Input `regions` — Source ocr_regions_v1; input order and IDs stay intact.

Input `mask` — Mask filters centers and fills excluded pixels with Background.

Input `region` — Source selection intersected with Mask; independent of the text quadrilaterals.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `contract_path` | `""` | Project-relative contract.json; uses its bundled, verified ONNX model. |
| `receipt_mode` | `"summary"` | `summary`, `detailed`, `off` Summary: identity/settings/shapes. Detailed: tensor hashes. Off: no receipt. |
| `fallback_policy` | `"error"` | `error`, `cpu_if_unavailable` Error by default; CPU fallback only for an unavailable requested target. |
| `model_path` | `""` | Local ONNX path; Contract path instead uses its bundled model. |
| `width` | `100` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 4096. Configured mode: network width. Source mode uses each source width (up to 4096). |
| `height` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 4096. Configured mode: network height. Source mode uses source height (up to 4096). |
| `color_format` | `"gray"` | `rgb`, `bgr`, `gray` Explicit network channel order; this does not rescale source intensity units. |
| `resize_mode` | `"stretch"` | `stretch`, `letterbox` Stretch axes or retain aspect ratio with centered rounded letterbox padding. |
| `padding_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -3.40282e+38 and 3.40282e+38. Letterbox intensity before normalization. |
| `scale` | `0.00784313725490196` | Value must be a finite number between -3.40282e+38 and 3.40282e+38. Normalization is (pixel - mean) * scale / std, in chosen channel order. |
| `mean` | `[127.5]` | One value per network channel, in original intensity units. |
| `std` | `[1.0]` | One strictly positive divisor per network channel. |
| `input_name` | `"input"` | Exact native model image input name; this block binds one NCHW input. |
| `output_names` | `[]` | JSON list; empty selects all unconnected output layers. |
| `engine` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `classic`, `new`, `ort` Classic does not retry with New; non-CPU targets require Classic. |
| `backend` | `"opencv"` | `opencv`, `cuda`, `default` Native backend; the selected target must be available on this installation. |
| `target` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `opencl`, `opencl_fp16`, `cuda`, `cuda_fp16` Native compute target. Non-CPU execution requires the classic engine. |
| `fusion` | `true` | Native layer fusion; disabling requires the classic engine. |
| `max_output_elements` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. Combined retained outputs for the activation, including padding. |
| `input_shape` | `[-1, 1, 32, 100]` | Declared [N,C,H,W]; -1 permits supported variation, never reshaping. |
| `batch_size` | `8` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. Native crop rows per call; model must support declared batch dimensions. |
| `tail_policy` | `"variable"` | `variable`, `pad_repeat`, `reject` Variable submits the real tail; Pad repeat copies its last row and discards padded outputs; Reject requires a complete multiple. Padding can affect batch-coupled models. |
| `spatial_mode` | `"configured"` | `configured` Configured resizes each image; Source keeps dimensions, requiring one size per group. |
| `maximum_images` | `64` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. Maximum supplied regions, including those skipped by source ROI. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Per-source pixel guard; excludes runtime copies. |
| `maximum_source_pixels` | `32000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Combined source pixel budget checked before image conversion. |
| `maximum_tensor_elements` | `4000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. Prepared N*C*H*W budget per native call, including padding. |
| `ctc_layout` | `"TNC"` | `TNC`, `NTC` Exact native CTC axes: time/batch/classes or batch/time/classes. |
| `score_activation` | `"softmax"` | `softmax`, `probability` Softmax for logits; Probability checks normalized class scores. |
| `blank_index` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 10000. CTC blank class; vocabulary lists all other classes in their native order. |
| `quadrilaterals` | `[]` | Source quads without Regions; empty uses image/selection bounds. |
| `vocabulary` | `[]` | Unique ordered tokens, excluding blank; multi-character tokens are allowed. |
| `vocabulary_path` | `""` | Alternative project-relative UTF-8 token lines; requires its SHA256. |
| `vocabulary_sha256` | `""` | File SHA256; inline tokens are sealed with parameters. |
| `minimum_confidence` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Selected score limit, retaining original class mass. |
| `minimum_reads` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 64. Minimum nonblank reads meeting the confidence limit. |
| `aggregate` | `"all"` | `all`, `minimum_reads` Require all selected rows to pass, or only Minimum reads. |
| `background` | `255.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 65535. Intensity outside selection and at borders, in source units. |
| `maximum_steps` | `1024` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Bound time steps before greedy path decoding. |
| `decoder` | `"CTC-prefix-beam-search"` | `CTC-greedy`, `CTC-prefix-beam-search` Greedy verifies its raw read; prefix beam searches admissible label sequences. |
| `allowed_characters` | `""` | Case-sensitive characters; empty permits all vocabulary tokens. |
| `lexicon` | `[]` | Exact words; no case folding, weighting or spell correction. |
| `lexicon_path` | `""` | Alternative project-relative UTF-8 word lines, requiring exact SHA256. |
| `lexicon_sha256` | `""` | Exact lexicon file bytes; inline words are sealed with settings. |
| `beam_width` | `16` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 256. Prefixes retained per step; approximate search with exact final rescoring. |
| `token_top_k` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 10000. Nonblank classes after alphabet filtering; zero keeps all. Blank stays. |
| `maximum_alternatives` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 16. Alternatives with exact unconditioned label-sequence scores. |
| `confidence_mode` | `"token_normalized"` | `token_normalized`, `sequence_probability` Exact label mass or token-count root; not calibrated correctness. |
| `reject_blank_dominant` | `true` | Reject words with mass at or below the blank sequence. |
| `minimum_greedy_ratio` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum candidate/greedy mass, capped at one; zero disables. |
| `maximum_output_tokens` | `128` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 512. Hard decoded-label length limit; reports omitted extensions. |
| `maximum_candidate_prefixes` | `8192` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 65536. Reject too many temporary prefixes before beam pruning. |
| `maximum_search_expansions` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1e+07. Bound attempted prefix/class transitions per source region. |
| `maximum_alignment_elements` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. Combined T x state work for scoring and alignment. |
| `maximum_lexicon_words` | `4096` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Distinct word limit; the file also has a 1 MB byte guard. |
| `maximum_lexicon_characters` | `65536` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 262144. Combined lexicon characters; bounds the prefix trie. |

## Restore image with ONNX (`ml.restore_image`)

ML / Image restoration

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — uint8/uint16 or float32 [0,1], GRAY/BGR/RGB/BGRA/RGBA; no physical depth.

Input `mask` — Write selection, intersected with Region. Excluded pixels remain context.

Input `region` — Source-grid region; outside remains bit-identical to the input.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `contract_path` | `""` | Project-relative contract.json; uses its bundled, verified ONNX model. |
| `receipt_mode` | `"summary"` | `summary`, `detailed`, `off` Summary: identity/settings/shapes. Detailed: tensor hashes. Off: no receipt. |
| `fallback_policy` | `"error"` | `error`, `cpu_if_unavailable` Error by default; CPU fallback only for an unavailable requested target. |
| `model_path` | `""` | Local same-grid ONNX; Contract path selects a sealed model/settings bundle. |
| `input_name` | `"input"` | Exact native input name; single image, batch one. |
| `output_names` | `[]` | Empty requires one output; otherwise select one exact terminal head. |
| `color_format` | `"gray"` | `gray`, `rgb`, `bgr` Match gray/color source channels. Alpha passes unchanged. |
| `input_layout` | `"NCHW"` | `NCHW`, `NHWC` |
| `output_layout` | `"NCHW"` | `NCHW`, `NHWC` |
| `mean` | `[0.0]` | One or C values in normalized source units, subtracted before dividing by Std. |
| `std` | `[1.0]` | One or C positive divisors. Output restoration reverses this normalization. |
| `output_mode` | `"restored"` | `restored`, `noise_residual` Restored reverses normalization; Noise residual subtracts predicted noise. |
| `blend` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Fraction of restored values to write. Zero skips model I/O/inference. |
| `processing_mode` | `"whole"` | `whole`, `halo_tiles` No resize. Halo tiles trim context and write disjoint cores. |
| `tile_width` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Core width, excluding Halo. Edge cores/context clip to actual image bounds. |
| `tile_height` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Core height. Native models must accept every resulting context shape. |
| `halo` | `20` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 256. Context around cores; cover the model's receptive field for parity. |
| `engine` | `"classic"` | `auto`, `classic`, `new`, `ort` Classic preserves explicit backend/fusion; non-CPU targets require Classic. |
| `backend` | `"opencv"` | `opencv`, `cuda`, `default` |
| `target` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `opencl`, `opencl_fp16`, `cuda`, `cuda_fp16` |
| `fusion` | `true` |  |
| `maximum_source_pixels` | `4000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.6e+07. Source-grid guard before normalization or native inference. |
| `maximum_tiles` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1024. Bound selected native calls; unselected cores are skipped. |
| `maximum_processed_pixels` | `16000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Combined context pixels, including repeated Halo work. |
| `max_output_elements` | `8000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. Retained elements per call; does not bound native workspace. |

## Predict OpenCV random forest classes (`ml.rtrees_predict`)

ML / Classical classification

Inputs: `model` (data), `samples` (data), `image` (image, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `contract_policy` | `"require_match"` | `require_match`, `dimensions_only` Match saved preprocessing, or explicitly check only dimensions. Stored normalization always applies. |
| `min_vote_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Inclusive minimum winning vote fraction; agreement is not a calibrated probability. |
| `min_vote_margin` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Inclusive minimum (winner minus runner-up votes) / actual tree count. |
| `reject_vote_ties` | `true` | Withhold recognized labels on tied maximum votes; candidate and votes remain visible. |
| `accepted_labels` | `[]` | Optional allowed trained classes. Known disallowed classes still emit their labels with an inspection failure. |

Output / ML models

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |

## Train OpenCV random forest classifier (`ml.rtrees_train`)

ML / Classical classification

Inputs: `samples` (any), `labels` (data, optional), `sample_weights` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `max_depth` | `10` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 16. Maximum tree depth (root is zero). Bounds model complexity. |
| `min_sample_count` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2048. Native minimum samples used to split a node. |
| `active_variables` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 65536. Features considered per split; 0 uses the native rounded square root. Positive values must not exceed the descriptor length. |
| `max_trees` | `64` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 256. Hard training tree limit; retained even with optional OOB stopping. |
| `termination` | `"tree_limit"` | `tree_limit`, `tree_limit_or_oob_error` Always keep the tree limit; optionally stop when native OOB error is below the target. |
| `oob_error` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. OOB error target used only by OOB termination; needs a positive value. The weighted native statistic is not held-out accuracy. |
| `calculate_importance` | `false` | Compute native permutation importance and OOB statistics. Adds substantial feature-dependent training cost; the work budget is checked first. |
| `class_priors` | `[]` | Optional positive relative class weights in first-appearance label order. |
| `normalization` | `"none"` | `none`, `l2` None or per-row L2, stored with the model and applied to queries. |
| `labels` | `[]` | One class string per training row; a wired Labels input overrides this list. |
| `sample_weights` | `[]` | [] gives equal weights. Otherwise one positive value per row; the wired Sample weights input overrides this list. |

## Define segmentation prompts (`ml.segmentation_prompts`)

ML / Prompted segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `points` (data, optional), `point_labels` (data, optional), `box` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Current source grid/ticket. Points and box do not crop the image.

Input `points` — Optional current-event override of the matching saved field; no last-value fallback.

Input `point_labels` — Optional current-event override of the matching saved field; no last-value fallback.

Input `box` — Optional current-event override of the matching saved field; no last-value fallback.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `points` | `[]` | Source pixel-center [x,y] pairs; at most six total model slots including box corners. |
| `point_labels` | `[]` | One integer per point: 1 foreground, 0 background. Padding is internal. |
| `box` | `[]` | Optional [x,y,width,height] source pixel-edge rectangle; costs two prompt slots. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Reject larger source grids; maximum axis is 16384. |

## Select instance mask (`ml.select_instance_mask`)

ML / Instance segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `collection` (data).

Input `image` — Original source-grid image, with the matching ticket/frame/recipe.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `selection` | `"index"` | `index`, `id` Choose collection position after sorting/filtering, or original row ID. |
| `index` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 9999. Zero-based position in the current collection. |
| `instance_id` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 99999. Original detection-row ID, preserved through suppression/sorting/filtering. |
| `missing` | `"empty"` | `empty`, `error` Emit a normal failed empty mask/region, or reject without current output. |
| `maximum_region_runs` | `200000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1e+06. Reject larger selected mask encodings before rasterizing. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Bound the full source mask allocation. |

## Split labeled images by capture group (`ml.split_image_groups`)

ML / Evaluation

Inputs: `samples` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `samples` | `[]` | Image records: id, path, class_label, capture_group, optional image_sha256. Groups represent correlated captures. Paths/hash claims cannot cross groups or labels; files are not read here. |
| `test_fraction` | `0.25` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Fraction of whole groups, rounded with at least one if positive. |
| `validation_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Additional validation groups; leave at least one training group. |
| `seed` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between -2.14748e+09 and 2.14748e+09. Deterministic group hash order; independent of input order or global RNG state. |
| `require_training_classes` | `true` | Reject missing training classes. Split is not stratified. |

## Learned stereo disparity (`ml.stereo_disparity`)

ML / Depth estimation

Inputs: `left` (image), `right` (image), `left_mask` (image, optional), `right_mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `rectification` (data, optional).

Input `left` — Rectified left image; owns source grid/ticket and is returned unchanged.

Input `right` — Equal-size rectified right view. Epipolar alignment is caller-supplied.

Input `left_mask` — Write selection on the left grid; excluded pixels remain model context.

Input `right_mask` — Support at x_left - disparity; fractional samples need both neighbors.

Input `region` — Left-grid region, intersected with Left mask.

Input `rectification` — Optional stereo_rectification_v1 for this size, preserved for 3D.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `contract_path` | `""` | Project-relative contract.json; uses its bundled, verified ONNX model. |
| `receipt_mode` | `"summary"` | `summary`, `detailed`, `off` Summary: identity/settings/shapes. Detailed: tensor hashes. Off: no receipt. |
| `fallback_policy` | `"error"` | `error`, `cpu_if_unavailable` Error by default; CPU fallback only for an unavailable requested target. |
| `model_path` | `""` |  |
| `left_name` | `"left"` | Left model binding, or the combined binding for concatenated input. |
| `right_name` | `"right"` | Right binding for Named mode; distinct from Left name. |
| `output_names` | `[]` |  |
| `input_mode` | `"named"` | `named`, `concatenated` Two named tensors, or one tensor with left channels followed by right. |
| `input_layout` | `"NCHW"` | `NCHW`, `NHWC` |
| `layout` | `"NHW"` | `NHW`, `NCHW`, `NHWC`, `HW`, `CHW`, `HWC` |
| `width` | `640` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. |
| `height` | `480` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. |
| `color_format` | `"rgb"` | `rgb`, `bgr`, `gray` |
| `resize_mode` | `"stretch"` | `stretch`, `letterbox` Apply identical stretch or rounded letterbox to both rectified images. |
| `input_interpolation` | `"linear"` | `linear`, `cubic`, `area` |
| `padding_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. |
| `mean` | `[0.0, 0.0, 0.0]` | Per-view channel values in normalized [0,1] source units, before Std division. |
| `std` | `[1.0, 1.0, 1.0]` | Positive per-view channel divisors; use zero mean/unit std for HITNET. |
| `head_units` | `"network_pixels"` | `network_pixels`, `head_pixels`, `source_pixels` Affine head quantity before conversion to original left-image pixels. |
| `head_sign` | `"left_minus_right"` | `left_minus_right`, `right_minus_left` Output always becomes d = x_left - x_right, on the left grid. |
| `head_scale` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+09 and 1e+09. Fixed nonzero affine head multiplier, before spatial interpolation. |
| `head_offset` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+09 and 1e+09. Fixed affine offset in declared head units. |
| `invalid_value_enabled` | `false` |  |
| `invalid_head_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+12 and 1e+12. Exact raw sentinel; invalidate all interpolation contributors. |
| `minimum_disparity` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -16384 and 16384. Inclusive lower bound in source pixels, after unit/sign conversion. |
| `maximum_disparity` | `4096.0` | Value must be a finite number between -16384 and 16384. Inclusive upper bound in source pixels; outside samples become NaN. |
| `resampling` | `"linear"` | `linear`, `nearest` |
| `check_correspondence` | `true` | Reject out-of-source x_right; Right mask always checks it. |
| `minimum_valid_pixels` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4e+06. |
| `minimum_valid_fraction` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Required selected coverage; not confidence or accuracy. |
| `ticket_policy` | `"same_ticket"` | `same_ticket`, `same_value`, `unchecked` Match session+kind+value, kind+value, or unchecked; unticketed allowed. |
| `preview_mode` | `"auto_percentile"` | `auto_percentile`, `fixed` Display-only stretch; never changes numerical disparity. |
| `display_min` | `0.0` |  |
| `display_max` | `64.0` |  |
| `engine` | `"new"` | `auto`, `classic`, `new`, `ort` |
| `backend` | `"opencv"` | `opencv`, `cuda`, `default` |
| `target` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `opencl`, `opencl_fp16`, `cuda`, `cuda_fp16` |
| `fusion` | `true` |  |
| `maximum_source_pixels` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4e+06. |
| `maximum_input_elements` | `3000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.6e+07. Combined two-view tensor bound before conversion/allocation. |
| `max_output_elements` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 8e+06. |

## Learned image super-resolution (`ml.super_resolution`)

ML / Image restoration

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — GRAY/BGR/RGB/BGRA/RGBA. Interpolated alpha; no physical depth.

Input `mask` — Optional source-grid nonzero selection; intersects Region and repeats to output.

Input `region` — Source-grid region. Full source remains model context; outside uses baseline.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `model_path` | `""` | Frozen .pb: relative assets copy on Save As; absolute paths stay linked. |
| `model_sha256` | `""` | Exact digest checked each run; imports a private snapshot on cache miss. |
| `algorithm` | `"fsrcnn"` | `fsrcnn`, `espcn`, `edsr`, `lapsrn` Must match the export. Qualified here: pinned FSRCNN x2 on CPU. |
| `scale` | `2` | `2`, `3`, `4`, `8` Export scale; FSRCNN/ESPCN/EDSR: 2/3/4; LapSRN: 2/4/8. Output size is checked. |
| `depth_mode` | `"uint8_only"` | `uint8_only`, `convert_full_scale` Quantizes uint16/float32 [0,1] to 8 bits, then restores source depth. |
| `baseline_interpolation` | `"cubic"` | `nearest`, `linear`, `cubic`, `lanczos4` Full-source resize; also supplies unselected pixels and alpha. |
| `backend` | `"opencv"` | `opencv`, `cuda`, `default` Unavailable targets fail. Layer placement is not measured. |
| `target` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `opencl`, `opencl_fp16`, `cuda`, `cuda_fp16` CPU baseline; other available devices require their own qualification. |
| `maximum_model_bytes` | `67108864` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2.68435e+08. Bound the exact model snapshot; does not bound native workspace. |
| `maximum_input_pixels` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.6e+07. Full-source inference with unselected context; no ROI speedup. |
| `maximum_output_pixels` | `16000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Bound predicted output size before resize and native inference. |

## Train OpenCV SVM classifier (`ml.svm_train`)

ML / Classical classification

Inputs: `samples` (any), `labels` (data, optional).

Input `samples` — Feature rows, or a batch of vectors. Known feature contracts must agree; HOG supplies its complete window/settings contract.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `kernel` | `"rbf"` | `linear`, `rbf`, `polynomial`, `sigmoid`, `chi_squared`, `intersection` OpenCV C-SVC kernel. Chi-squared requires nonnegative features. |
| `c` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-09 and 1e+09. Misclassification penalty; optionally multiplied by per-class weights. |
| `gamma` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-09 and 1e+09. RBF/polynomial/sigmoid/chi-squared kernel scale; ignored by linear/intersection. |
| `degree` | `3.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 32. Polynomial kernel exponent. |
| `coef0` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+06 and 1e+06. Polynomial/sigmoid constant term. |
| `normalization` | `"none"` | `none`, `l2` Persisted per-row normalization; applied identically at prediction. |
| `max_iterations` | `5000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. Maximum native training iterations. |
| `epsilon` | `1e-06` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-12 and 1. Native solver termination tolerance; used with iteration limit. |
| `class_weights` | `[]` | Weights in first-appearance class order; [] gives equal weights. |
| `labels` | `[]` | One string class per sample row; a wired Labels input takes precedence. |

## Text regions from probability map (`ml.text_regions`)

ML / Text inspection

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `tensors` (data), `preprocessing` (data).

Input `image` — Original image, returned unchanged; match inference ticket and frame.

Input `tensors` — One named exclusive float32 probability map, HW or leading singleton axes.

Input `preprocessing` — Matching positive resize/letterbox geometry from DNN inference.

Input `mask` — Optional source-grid selection; region centers must lie inside it.

Input `region` — Optional source-grid region; intersects Mask, applied by region center.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `output_name` | `""` | Probability-map tensor name. Empty selects the first output. |
| `pixel_threshold` | `0.3` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Strictly greater probability starts an external contour. |
| `minimum_confidence` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum mean probability inside each filled contour, inclusive. |
| `minimum_area` | `8.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1.67772e+07. Minimum contour area in probability-map pixels, inclusive. |
| `padding_ratio` | `1.1` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 4. Rectangle scale only; multiply both detected rectangle dimensions. |
| `maximum_regions` | `64` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 256. Retain highest-confidence regions, then order by center y,x. |
| `draw_overlay` | `true` | Optional raster review; global/per-block graphics settings also apply. |
| `expansion` | `"rectangle_scale"` | `rectangle_scale`, `db_unclip` Scale the rectangle, or add the DB area/perimeter offset. |
| `unclip_ratio` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10. DB only: add 2 x area x ratio / perimeter to both dimensions. |
| `minimum_side` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 4096. Minimum unexpanded rectangle side in probability-map pixels. |
| `border_policy` | `"keep"` | `keep`, `discard` Keep expanded corners outside the image, or discard the whole region. |
| `minimum_count` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 256. Minimum retained regions to pass; zero permits an empty result. |
| `maximum_count` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 256. Maximum retained count; zero disables this limit. |
| `fail_on_truncation` | `true` | Fail if accepted regions are truncated; retain current results. |
| `maximum_candidates` | `2000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 20000. Reject more external contours before scoring. |
| `maximum_tensor_elements` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 4 and 1.67772e+07. Bound map samples before conversion; maximum axis is 4096. |
| `maximum_image_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Source-grid pixel limit; maximum source axis is 16384. |

## Write animation file (`output.animation_file`)

Output / Images

Inputs: `frames` (any), `durations` (data, optional), `still_image` (image, optional).

Input `frames` — FlowGroup of 2..64 matching uint8 images; each retains its own context.

Input `durations` — Optional millisecond list, one entry per frame.

Input `still_image` — APNG poster, separate from timed frames. Use grayscale or BGRA to avoid a native RGB poster defect.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `path` | `"output.png"` |  |
| `path_base` | `"project"` | `project`, `working_directory` |
| `codec` | `"apng"` | `apng`, `gif`, `webp` |
| `duration_mode` | `"payload_or_default"` | `payload_or_default`, `uniform`, `explicit` Use frame payload timing with a default, one uniform duration, or the explicit list. Wired Durations overrides this choice. |
| `default_duration_ms` | `100` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 655350. |
| `durations_ms` | `[]` |  |
| `loop_mode` | `"payload_or_default"` | `payload_or_default`, `override` Use the first frame's loop metadata when available, or override it. |
| `loop_count` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 65535. Zero is infinite; GIF viewers may count repeats differently. |
| `background_mode` | `"payload_or_default"` | `payload_or_default`, `override` Use the first frame's background metadata, or override it. |
| `background_bgra` | `[0, 0, 0, 0]` | Four background bytes. GIF requires zero; APNG alpha must be zero. WebP packing is normalized and independently checked. |
| `maximum_pixels` | `16777216` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Total pixel budget. Readback verifies frame count, timing and loops. |
| `gif_timing` | `"exact"` | `exact`, `floor_10ms` Exact requires 10-ms units; floor_10ms quantizes and reports both lists. |
| `gif_quality` | `"palette_256"` | `fast_no_dither`, `fast_floyd`, `palette_8`, `palette_16`, `palette_32`, `palette_64`, `palette_128`, `palette_256` Native fast modes or palette size. GIF has binary alpha and reduced color. |
| `gif_dither` | `"none"` | `most`, `normal`, `less`, `minimal`, `none` Palette-mode dither; fast modes select their own native dither algorithm. |
| `gif_color_table` | `"global"` | `global`, `local` |
| `gif_transparency_threshold` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 255. Alpha below this becomes transparent; zero disables alpha. |
| `webp_mode` | `"lossless"` | `lossless`, `lossy` Lossless uses animation QUALITY=101. The still-image LOSSLESS_MODE flag is ignored by this backend. |
| `webp_quality` | `90` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100. Lossy WebP only, 1..100. |
| `png_compression` | `-1` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 9. APNG only; -1 keeps the native compression default, otherwise 0..9. |
| `png_strategy` | `"native"` | `native`, `default`, `filtered`, `huffman`, `rle`, `fixed` APNG zlib strategy. Native APNG ignores PNG filter and buffer-size flags. |

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |

| `direction` | `"source_to_target"` | `unspecified`, `source_to_target`, `target_to_source` Declared image-pair direction, not inferred from vector signs. |
| `metadata_mode` | `"if_connected"` | `none`, `if_connected`, `require_images` Optional SHA-bound .flo.json; both image anchors bind exact pixels and grids. |
| `source_label` | `""` |  |
| `target_label` | `""` |  |
| `precision` | `"float32_only"` | `float32_only`, `convert_float64` Native .flo is float32; conversion explicitly permits float64 rounding. |
| `invalid_vectors` | `"reject"` | `reject`, `preserve` Reject by default; Preserve writes nonfinite/unknown samples unchanged. |

## Save image (`output.image`)

Output / Images

Inputs: `image` (image), `metadata` (any, optional).

Input `metadata` — PNG/JPEG ImageMetadata; verified before publishing, existing files preserved.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `path` | `"output.png"` |  |
| `jpeg_quality` | `-1` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 100. -1 preserves the codec default; otherwise 0..100. JPEG only. |
| `jpeg_progressive` | `false` |  |
| `jpeg_optimize` | `false` |  |
| `jpeg_restart_interval` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 65535. |
| `jpeg_luma_quality` | `-1` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 100. |
| `jpeg_chroma_quality` | `-1` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 100. |
| `png_compression` | `-1` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 9. -1 preserves the codec default; otherwise 0..9. PNG only. |
| `png_strategy` | `"native"` | `native`, `default`, `filtered`, `huffman`, `rle`, `fixed` |
| `png_bilevel` | `false` |  |
| `png_filter` | `"native"` | `native`, `none`, `sub`, `up`, `avg`, `paeth`, `fast`, `all` PNG filter or adaptive filter set; native keeps the backend default. |
| `png_zlib_buffer_size` | `0` | Value must be zero or a whole number between 6 and 1.04858e+06. PNG libpng buffer in bytes: zero for native, otherwise 6..1048576. SPNG does not use it. |
| `jpeg2000_mode` | `"lossless"` | `lossless`, `lossy` JP2 lossless uses reversible coding. Lossy mode explicitly enables target-rate compression. |
| `jpeg2000_rate_x1000` | `250` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 999. Lossy JP2 only: target rate times 1000, from 1 to 999. It is not a quality score or a guaranteed byte count. |
| `webp_quality` | `-1` | -1 preserves the codec default; otherwise 1..100. WebP only. |
| `tiff_compression` | `"none"` | `none`, `lzw`, `deflate`, `packbits` TIFF only. Explicit lossless compression avoids the native float32-color LogLuv default. |
| `tiff_predictor` | `"native"` | `native`, `none`, `horizontal`, `floating` TIFF LZW/deflate only. Floating prediction requires floating samples; horizontal requires integer samples. |
| `tiff_rows_per_strip` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1.67772e+07. TIFF strip height; zero keeps the native default. |
| `hdr_compression` | `"rle"` | `rle`, `none` Radiance HDR RLE or no compression. Both use lossy shared-exponent RGBE samples. |
| `exr_storage` | `"float32"` | `float32`, `float16` EXR needs a capable OpenCV build. Float32 retains sample precision; float16 quantizes and requires values within Â±65504. |
| `exr_compression` | `"zip"` | `no`, `rle`, `zips`, `zip`, `piz`, `pxr24`, `b44`, `b44a`, `dwaa`, `dwab` EXR codec. PXR24, B44/B44A and DWA variants can be lossy; ZIP is the default. |
| `exr_dwa_level` | `45` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1000. EXR DWAA/DWAB compression level; unused by other compression modes. |

## Track region by color (`sequence.color_tracker`)

Sequence / Object tracking

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reset` (data, optional), `stream` (data, optional), `seed` (data, optional), `reseed` (data, optional).

Input `image` — uint8 BGR/RGB/BGRA/RGBA or native HSV, up to 2.1M pixels.

Input `seed` — Initial [x,y,w,h] or image_region_v1 shape on this grid; read when teaching.

Input `reseed` — Rising edge teaches from current seed; held true does not repeat.

Input `mask` — Current image work area, intersected with region for teaching and probability.

Input `region` — Current work area; use a separate shape on Seed for the taught target.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `warmup` | `2` | Minimum eligible activations before primary data outputs are emitted. |
| `max_streams` | `4` |  |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `128.0` | Stored array/metadata budget; intermediates and GUI copies use extra memory. |
| `overflow` | `"error"` | `error`, `evict_oldest` |
| `context_change` | `"reset"` | `reset`, `separate` Reset a source on recipe/grid/settings change, or cache separate contexts. |
| `max_gap_ms` | `0.0` | Reset after this ingress gap; zero keeps state across pauses. |
| `method` | `"cam_shift"` | `mean_shift`, `cam_shift` MeanShift keeps window size; CamShift estimates a color-mass ellipse and scale. |
| `initial_bounds` | `[]` | Initial [x,y,w,h]; read before teaching or after reset/reseed. |
| `histogram_channels` | `"hue_saturation"` | `hue`, `hue_saturation` Hue or joint hue/saturation, peak-normalized to 255. |
| `hue_bins` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 180. Hue bin count over [0,180), in native 8-bit HSV units. |
| `saturation_bins` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 256. Saturation bins over [0,256), ignored by hue-only models. |
| `minimum_saturation` | `40` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 255. Discard lower-saturation pixels in teaching and backprojection. |
| `minimum_value` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 255. Inclusive minimum native HSV value; reject dark unreliable hue. |
| `maximum_value` | `255` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 255. Inclusive maximum HSV value; optionally exclude clipped highlights. |
| `minimum_alpha` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 255. For BGRA/RGBA, reject lower stored opacity; ignored without alpha. |
| `minimum_seed_pixels` | `16` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2.1e+06. Minimum eligible pixels in the seed selection before teaching. |
| `termination` | `"count_and_epsilon"` | `count_and_epsilon`, `count`, `epsilon` Native TermCriteria flags; inactive controls retain their values. |
| `max_iterations` | `20` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100. Native count limit when enabled; OpenCV caps MeanShift at 100. |
| `epsilon` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10. Native convergence displacement in pixels when enabled. |
| `minimum_confidence` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Mean backprojection/255; not an accuracy probability. |
| `support_threshold` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 255. Count box pixels meeting this backprojection threshold. |
| `minimum_support_fraction` | `0.05` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum fraction of the box meeting Support threshold. |
| `minimum_area_ratio` | `0.25` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1000. Minimum area relative to the original seed box. |
| `maximum_area_ratio` | `4.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1000. Maximum relative area; CamShift uses equivalent-ellipse area. |
| `maximum_center_jump` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 32766. Maximum center motion from last accepted update; zero disables. |
| `max_misses` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1000. Misses before expiry; reset/reseed is then required to teach again. |
| `learning_rate` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Blend accepted updates; zero freezes. Lost frames never learn. |
| `pose_mode` | `"center_only"` | `center_only`, `principal_axis` Zero-angle center pose, or elongated CamShift principal axis. |
| `minimum_axis_ratio` | `1.1` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 100. Minimum axial pose ratio; orientation has 180° ambiguity. |

## DIS optical flow (`sequence.dis`)

Sequence / Temporal processing

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reset` (data, optional), `stream` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `warmup` | `2` | Minimum eligible activations before primary data outputs are emitted. |
| `max_streams` | `4` |  |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `128.0` | Stored array/metadata budget; intermediates and GUI copies use extra memory. |
| `overflow` | `"error"` | `error`, `evict_oldest` |
| `context_change` | `"reset"` | `reset`, `separate` Reset a source on recipe/grid/settings change, or cache separate contexts. |
| `max_gap_ms` | `1000.0` | Reset after this ingress gap; zero keeps state across pauses. |
| `visual_max_motion` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 1e-06. Pixels mapped to full brightness. Vectors retain pixel units. |
| `consistency_tolerance` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Maximum round-trip error in pixels. Zero disables the check. |
| `preset` | `"fast"` | `ultrafast`, `fast`, `medium` OpenCV preset for the search and refinement defaults. |
| `initial_flow` | `"none"` | `none`, `previous` Previous reuses the preceding unmasked vectors as the initial estimate. |
| `finest_scale` | `-1` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 9. -1 uses the preset, reduced for small images. Zero uses full resolution. |
| `coarsest_scale` | `-1` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 9. -1 selects the coarsest level that fits the input and OpenCV limits. |
| `patch_size` | `0` | Value must be zero or a whole number between 4 and 64. Square matching patch in level pixels. Zero uses the preset. |
| `patch_stride` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 63. Distance between patches. Zero uses the preset. |
| `gradient_iterations` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 1000. Maximum inverse-search iterations. Zero uses the preset. |
| `mean_normalization` | `true` | Normalize patch brightness before comparing image patches. |
| `spatial_propagation` | `true` | Use nearby flow vectors as candidates during patch search. |
| `refinement_iterations` | `-1` | Value must be a whole number between -1 and 1000. -1 uses the preset. Zero disables variational refinement. |
| `refinement_alpha` | `20.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Variational refinement smoothness weight. |
| `refinement_delta` | `5.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+06. Variational refinement brightness-constancy weight. |
| `refinement_gamma` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+06. Variational refinement gradient-constancy weight. |
| `refinement_epsilon` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Variational refinement robust penalty offset. |

## Fill stabilization borders for review (`sequence.fill_stabilization_borders`)

Image processing / Video restoration

Inputs: `frames` (any), `coverage` (any), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `frames` — 2-64 corrected gray/RGB(A)/BGR(A) images, same dimensions and pixel depth.

Input `coverage` — Required ordered group of original measured uint8 masks, one per frame.

Input `mask` — Optional current-grid selection of fill centers; measured pixels never change.

Input `region` — Optional current-grid region intersected with Mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `target_index` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 63. Current capture index; retain its ticket, timing and coordinate frame. |
| `temporal_radius` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 63. Only use neighbors within this many ordered batch positions. |
| `alignment` | `"spatial"` | `spatial`, `pixel_grid` Spatial uses a shared known root; pixel grid asserts registration. |
| `different_segment` | `"skip"` | `skip`, `error` Skip another known root, or reject it. Unknown spatial roots reject. |
| `coverage_tickets` | `"matching_if_known"` | `matching_if_known`, `ignore` Check known per-image/mask acquisition tickets, or explicitly ignore. |
| `interpolation` | `"linear"` | `nearest`, `linear` Resample neighboring measured pixels with full kernel support. |
| `holes` | `"border_only"` | `border_only`, `all` Only border-connected geometric holes, or all geometric holes. |
| `blend` | `"nearest"` | `nearest`, `mean` Nearest capture wins (earlier index breaks ties), or mean accepted samples. |
| `consistency` | `"overlap"` | `overlap`, `off` Require measured overlap, or explicitly disable the photometric check. |
| `maximum_error` | `0.03` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 2. Maximum channel error divided by shared integer/full-batch float scale. |
| `minimum_overlap_pixels` | `64` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. Minimum shared measured pixels for overlap consistency. |
| `minimum_consistent_fraction` | `0.8` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum fraction of shared pixels within Maximum error. |
| `change_radius` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 63. Exclude this halo around detected overlap changes before filling. |
| `maximum_fill_distance` | `32.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 4096. Distance to current measured support; zero means unlimited. |
| `minimum_fill_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Required filled fraction of selected holes; no holes means 1. |
| `unfilled` | `"allow"` | `allow`, `fail` Allow partial review fills, or fail if any selected eligible hole remains. |
| `maximum_working_bytes` | `536870912` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2.14748e+09. Array estimate before preparation; excludes graph/native copies. |
| `maximum_graphics_points` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 4096. Bound fill/missing contour vertices and review annotation. |

## Debounce foreground components (`sequence.foreground_debounce`)

Sequence / Foreground masks

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reset` (data, optional), `stream` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Single-channel foreground image; numeric samples are thresholded explicitly.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `warmup` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Minimum eligible activations before primary data outputs are emitted. |
| `max_streams` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `128.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 4096. Stored array/metadata budget; intermediates and GUI copies use extra memory. |
| `overflow` | `"error"` | `error`, `evict_oldest` |
| `context_change` | `"reset"` | `reset`, `separate` Reset a source on recipe/grid/settings change, or cache separate contexts. |
| `max_gap_ms` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Reset after this ingress gap; zero keeps state across pauses. |
| `consecutive_hits` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Required consecutive eligible unambiguous observations. Missing or ambiguous matches restart confirmation. |
| `foreground_threshold` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number. Samples strictly above this raw-value threshold are foreground. Use 127 to exclude 127-valued shadow labels. |
| `connectivity` | `"8"` | `4`, `8` |
| `minimum_area` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. |
| `maximum_area` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 6.4e+07. Zero disables the upper area limit. Area is measured after selection. |
| `maximum_displacement` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 32768. Maximum centroid motion per activation in current-grid pixels. No prediction or gap bridging. |
| `minimum_area_ratio` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Smaller/larger area must meet this ratio; zero disables the gate. |
| `minimum_iou` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum bounding-box intersection over union. Zero disables overlap gating and allows disjoint moving masks. |
| `maximum_components` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 512. Eligible components per frame. Exceeding the bound fails and clears sequence memory. |

## GSoC background subtraction (`sequence.gsoc_background`)

Sequence / Foreground segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reset` (data, optional), `stream` (data, optional).

Input `image` — 8-bit or normalized Float32 grayscale or color frames.

Input `mask` — Restrict foreground outputs. Model updates still use the full frame.

Input `region` — Restrict foreground outputs. Model updates still use the full frame.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `warmup` | `20` | Minimum eligible activations before primary data outputs are emitted. |
| `max_streams` | `4` |  |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `256.0` | Estimated retained CPU model budget. Processing uses extra buffers. |
| `overflow` | `"error"` | `error`, `evict_oldest` |
| `context_change` | `"reset"` | `reset`, `separate` Reset a source on recipe/grid/settings change, or cache separate contexts. |
| `max_gap_ms` | `0.0` | Reset after this ingress gap; zero keeps state across pauses. |
| `camera_motion` | `"none"` | `none`, `lucas_kanade` Lucas–Kanade compensates between textured frames on a fixed-size grid. |
| `samples` | `20` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 1023. Native samples per pixel in each of two retained model buffers. |
| `noise_background` | `0.0004` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 0.499999. Remove small foreground components below this fraction of frame area. |
| `noise_foreground` | `0.0008` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 0.499999. Fill small background components below this fraction of frame area. |
| `replace_rate` | `0.003` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Chance of adding an unmatched color to the sample bank. |
| `propagation_rate` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Chance of copying a trusted sample to adjacent pixels. |
| `hits_threshold` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 2.14748e+09. Matches needed before a sample is eligible for propagation. |
| `alpha` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 100. Weight on the recent squared color error in the matching threshold. |
| `beta` | `0.0022` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 3. Additive matching threshold in normalized squared color units. |
| `blink_decay` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 0.999999. Decay factor for the foreground toggling estimate. |
| `blink_multiplier` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Strength of adaptation where pixels repeatedly toggle. |
| `color_learning_rate` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Blend rate for matched colors and error. Other updates continue. |

## KNN background subtraction (`sequence.knn_background`)

Sequence / Temporal processing

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reset` (data, optional), `stream` (data, optional), `known_foreground` (image, optional).

Input `known_foreground` — Current-grid uint8 object mask. With a restored reference, Guarded requires this mask and replaces marked samples with trusted background before learning.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `warmup` | `20` | Minimum eligible activations before primary data outputs are emitted. |
| `max_streams` | `4` |  |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `256.0` | Retained CPU model budget. Processing images require additional memory. |
| `overflow` | `"error"` | `error`, `evict_oldest` |
| `context_change` | `"reset"` | `reset`, `separate` Reset a source on recipe/grid/settings change, or cache separate contexts. |
| `max_gap_ms` | `0.0` | Reset after this ingress gap; zero keeps state across pauses. |
| `history` | `500` |  |
| `learning_rate` | `-1.0` | -1 selects OpenCV's automatic rate. Manual rates are in [1e-8, 1). |
| `detect_shadows` | `true` |  |
| `shadow_threshold` | `0.5` |  |
| `shadow_value` | `127` | Value in the raw mask for detected shadows. The shadows port is binary. |
| `include_shadows` | `false` | Include shadows in the main binary foreground mask. |
| `reference_path` | `""` | Optional sealed clean-background JSON. Restored through bounded reference replay. |
| `reference_sha256` | `""` | Optional exact file SHA256. Reset forces a fresh read even with unchanged file metadata. |
| `reference_camera_id` | `""` | Must match the saved reference's stable camera/setup ID. |
| `reference_calibration_id` | `""` | Must match the saved reference's calibration revision, including empty values. |
| `reference_replay_passes` | `32` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1024. Clean-reference updates before the first live image; native background probe must pass. |
| `reference_replay_rate` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-08 and 0.999999. Native learning rate used only during clean-reference replay, below one. |
| `maximum_reference_work` | `64000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 5.12e+08. Maximum reference pixels times (replay passes + one verification probe). |
| `reference_update` | `"frozen"` | `frozen`, `guarded` Frozen disables live learning; Guarded requires a known-foreground mask on every activation. |
| `squared_distance_threshold` | `400.0` | Squared distance used to decide if a sample matches. |
| `samples` | `7` |  |
| `neighbors` | `2` | Minimum matching samples required to classify a pixel as background. |

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reset` (data, optional), `stream` (data, optional).

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |

## LSBP background subtraction (`sequence.lsbp_background`)

Sequence / Foreground segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reset` (data, optional), `stream` (data, optional).

Input `image` — 8-bit or normalized Float32 grayscale or color frames.

Input `mask` — Restrict foreground outputs. Model updates still use the full frame.

Input `region` — Restrict foreground outputs. Model updates still use the full frame.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `warmup` | `20` | Minimum eligible activations before primary data outputs are emitted. |
| `max_streams` | `4` |  |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `256.0` | Estimated retained CPU model budget. Processing uses extra buffers. |
| `overflow` | `"error"` | `error`, `evict_oldest` |
| `context_change` | `"reset"` | `reset`, `separate` Reset a source on recipe/grid/settings change, or cache separate contexts. |
| `max_gap_ms` | `0.0` | Reset after this ingress gap; zero keeps state across pauses. |
| `camera_motion` | `"none"` | `none`, `lucas_kanade` Lucas–Kanade compensates between textured frames on a fixed-size grid. |
| `samples` | `20` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 1023. Native samples per pixel in each of two retained model buffers. |
| `noise_background` | `0.0004` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 0.499999. Remove small foreground components below this fraction of frame area. |
| `noise_foreground` | `0.0008` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 0.499999. Fill small background components below this fraction of frame area. |
| `descriptor_radius` | `16` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Pixel radius of the local texture samples. |
| `update_minimum` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Lower bound for the reciprocal update probability. |
| `update_maximum` | `32.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Upper bound for the reciprocal update probability. |
| `update_increase` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+06. Step toward slower updates on foreground pixels. |
| `update_decrease` | `0.05` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+06. Step toward faster updates on background pixels. |
| `distance_scale` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+06. Scale the mean sample error to target the adaptive color threshold. |
| `distance_adaptation` | `0.005` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Fractional adjustment of the adaptive color threshold. |
| `descriptor_threshold` | `8` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 33. Exclusive Hamming distance limit for the 32-bit texture codes. |
| `minimum_matches` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1023. Minimum matching samples required for a background pixel. |
