## Temporal pixel statistics (`sequence.pixel_moments`)

Sequence / Temporal statistics

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reset` (data, optional), `stream` (data, optional), `other` (image, optional), `learn` (boolean, optional).

Input `image` — Finite uint8/uint16/int16/float32/float64 gray/RGB(A); absolute samples ≤1e100.

Input `other` — Optional paired image with equal dimensions; connected inputs must emit.

Input `learn` — False freezes the model but allows outputs from existing eligible observations.

Input `mask` — Selected pixels learn and appear in maps; excluded pixels are not observations.

Input `region` — Rectangle/circle/polygon intersection with Mask in Image coordinates.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `warmup` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Minimum eligible activations before primary data outputs are emitted. |
| `max_streams` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `256.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 4096. Stored array/metadata budget; intermediates and GUI copies use extra memory. |
| `overflow` | `"error"` | `error`, `evict_oldest` |
| `context_change` | `"reset"` | `reset`, `separate` Reset a source on recipe/grid/settings change, or cache separate contexts. |
| `max_gap_ms` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 3.6e+06. Reset after this ingress gap; zero keeps state across pauses. |
| `method` | `"cumulative"` | `cumulative`, `exponential` Cumulative or exponentially weighted eligible observations per pixel. |
| `normalization` | `"sample"` | `population`, `sample` Population divides by sum of weights; sample uses W - sum(w²)/W. |
| `alpha` | `0.05` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1. Exponential observation weight; no decay while paused or outside selection. |
| `channel_x` | `"gray"` | `gray`, `blue`, `green`, `red`, `alpha` Measured channel of Image, in its original numerical sample units. |
| `channel_y` | `"gray"` | `gray`, `blue`, `green`, `red`, `alpha` Channel of Other when connected, otherwise another channel of Image. |
| `alignment` | `"same_frame"` | `same_frame`, `pixel_indices` Check declared grids, or explicitly assert registration by pixel indices. |
| `ticket_policy` | `"match_if_present"` | `match_if_present`, `require_match`, `ignore` Other-source ticket matching; not proof of exposure synchronization. |
| `minimum_samples` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 1e+09. Per-pixel accepted observations needed for a valid estimate. |
| `maximum_samples` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 1e+09. Freeze each pixel at this count; counts stay exactly representable. |
| `minimum_coverage` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1. Required valid fraction of currently selected pixels for readiness. |
| `maximum_pixels` | `4194304` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.67772e+07. |
| `maximum_workspace_bytes` | `1073741824` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2.14748e+09. Conservative working-map estimate, not a hard RSS bound; GUI/runtime copies are extra. |
| `preview_metric` | `"std_x"` | `std_x`, `variance_x`, `covariance`, `correlation`, `mean_x` Diagnostic heatmap only; graphics off/Lean skips it. |
| `preview_min` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+100 and 1e+100. Heatmap lower bound. Correlation always uses -1..1. |
| `preview_max` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+100 and 1e+100. Heatmap upper bound; does not alter numerical maps. |

## Replay motion trajectory (`sequence.replay_trajectory`)

Sequence / Recorded motion

Inputs: `frames` (any), `trajectory` (data).

Input `frames` — Complete recorded clip in original order; exact pixels/types/grids are checked.

Input `trajectory` — Versioned trajectory; replay does not fit motion or restore old tickets.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `interpolation` | `"recorded"` | `recorded`, `nearest`, `linear` Use saved interpolation or explicitly override sampling. |
| `min_coverage_fraction` | `0.65` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Minimum valid support per frame; zero coverage always fails. |
| `invalid_frames` | `"reject"` | `reject`, `fail_partial`, `skip` Reject all, emit failed partial, or skip invalid/low-coverage frames. |
| `preview_frame` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 63. Original ordinal to show as Image; omitted frames emit no preview. |

## Restore detail from neighbors (`sequence.restore_neighbors`)

Image processing / Video restoration

Inputs: `frames` (any), `coverage` (any, optional), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `frames` — Batch of 2-16 registered gray/RGB(A)/BGR(A) uint8/16 or float32/64 images.

Input `coverage` — Optional same-order uint8 support masks on each capture grid. Target support is never expanded.

Input `mask` — Target-grid output selection. Outside pixels are copied exactly; neighborhoods use full images.

Input `region` — Optional target-grid region intersected with mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `target_index` | `0` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 15. Zero-based capture whose pixels, support, ticket and coordinate grid are retained. |
| `temporal_radius` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 15. Use only neighbors within this many batch positions of the target. |
| `alignment` | `"same_frame"` | `same_frame`, `pixel_grid` Same frame requires every known matching root/grid; pixel grid explicitly asserts upstream registration. |
| `blur_model` | `"horizontal_box"` | `horizontal_box`, `vertical_box`, `gaussian` Expected target blur applied to a sharp neighbor for the scene-consistency check. |
| `blur_size` | `7` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 3 and 63. Odd box length or square Gaussian size in current pixels. |
| `blur_sigma` | `1.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 30. Gaussian only; zero derives sigma from the kernel size. |
| `maximum_model_error` | `0.02` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 2. Maximum absolute channel difference between blurred donor and target, divided by shared scale. |
| `change_radius` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 63. Exclude this halo around model mismatches, including changed or moving foreground. |
| `energy_window` | `5` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 1 and 63. Odd local averaging size for native 3x3 Sobel luminance energy. |
| `minimum_energy` | `1e-06` | Value must be a finite number at least 0. Minimum donor energy in squared shared-scale luminance units; positive energy required. |
| `minimum_sharpness_ratio` | `1.1` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 100. Donor energy must strictly exceed target energy times this factor. |
| `blend` | `"best"` | `best`, `weighted` Best copies the highest-energy donor; weighted mixes all accepted donors by energy. |
| `mix` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1. Convex mix of restored detail and original target; one uses the accepted donor result. |
| `minimum_restored_fraction` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Required restored fraction of selected, originally supported target pixels. |
| `no_neighbor` | `"pass_through"` | `pass_through`, `fail` Pass through unchanged when no pixels qualify, or report an inspection failure. |

## Stabilize recorded sequence (`sequence.stabilize_batch`)

Sequence / Temporal processing

Inputs: `frames` (any), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `frames` — Ordered batch of two to 64 same-grid frames from one recording.

Input `mask` — Optional static-background mask applied in each frame's raw grid.

Input `region` — Optional static-background region applied in each frame's raw grid.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `radius_frames` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 63. Maximum number of neighbors on each side of the current frame. |
| `sigma_frames` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 63. Standard deviation of the centered Gaussian weights, in frames. |
| `motion_model` | `"similarity"` | `translation`, `similarity` Translation shifts the view. Similarity also tracks rotation and scale. |
| `max_features` | `240` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 5000. |
| `feature_quality` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1. |
| `feature_spacing` | `8.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 512. |
| `tracking_window` | `21` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 3 and 255. |
| `max_pyramid_level` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 8. |
| `forward_backward_pixels` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 100. Maximum error when a tracked point is mapped back. |
| `max_patch_error` | `30.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 255. Maximum mean patch error on the 8-bit tracking image. |
| `inlier_pixels` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 100. Reprojection tolerance for a track to support the motion estimate. |
| `min_inliers` | `12` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 5000. |
| `min_inlier_ratio` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. |
| `max_step_pixels` | `40.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 16384. Largest accepted center displacement between consecutive frames. |
| `max_step_degrees` | `12.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. Largest accepted rotation between consecutive frames. |
| `max_step_scale_ratio` | `1.15` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 2. Allowed scale range is the reciprocal of this ratio to the ratio. |
| `min_coverage_fraction` | `0.65` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Withhold the batch if any frame falls below this coverage. |
| `interpolation` | `"linear"` | `nearest`, `linear` |

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `warmup` | `2` | Minimum eligible activations before primary data outputs are emitted. |
| `max_streams` | `4` |  |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `128.0` | Stored array/metadata budget; intermediates and GUI copies use extra memory. |
| `overflow` | `"error"` | `error`, `evict_oldest` |
| `context_change` | `"reset"` | `reset`, `separate` Reset a source on recipe/grid/settings change, or cache separate contexts. |
| `max_gap_ms` | `0.0` | Reset after this ingress gap; zero keeps state across pauses. |
| `motion_model` | `"similarity"` | `translation`, `similarity` Translation shifts the view. Similarity also tracks rotation and scale. |
| `follow` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Zero locks to the anchor. Intermediate values smooth the observed trajectory. |
| `max_features` | `240` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 5000. |
| `feature_quality` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1. |
| `feature_spacing` | `8.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 512. |
| `tracking_window` | `21` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 3 and 255. |
| `max_pyramid_level` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 8. |
| `forward_backward_pixels` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 100. Maximum error when a tracked point is mapped back. |
| `max_patch_error` | `30.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 255. Maximum mean patch error on the 8-bit tracking image. |
| `inlier_pixels` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 100. Reprojection tolerance for a track to support the motion estimate. |
| `min_inliers` | `12` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 5000. |
| `min_inlier_ratio` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. |
| `max_step_pixels` | `40.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 16384. Largest accepted center displacement between consecutive frames. |
| `max_step_degrees` | `12.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. Largest accepted rotation between consecutive frames. |
| `max_step_scale_ratio` | `1.15` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 2. Allowed scale range is the reciprocal of this ratio to the ratio. |
| `min_coverage_fraction` | `0.65` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Re-anchor when too little of the corrected canvas is covered. |
| `max_validation_features` | `120` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 5000. Maximum features seeded inside the check area at each anchor. |
| `min_validation_tracks` | `12` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 5000. Minimum surviving anchor tracks required in the check area. |
| `max_validation_error_pixels` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 16384. Allowed residual displacement in anchor pixels. |
| `min_validation_inlier_ratio` | `0.8` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1. Fraction of check tracks that must meet the error limit. |
| `interpolation` | `"linear"` | `nearest`, `linear` |

## Stabilize continuous stream (`sequence.stabilize_stream`)

Sequence / Temporal processing

Inputs: `image` (image, optional), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reset` (data, optional), `flush` (data, optional).

Input `image` — One ordered camera stream. Each output keeps its original ticket and timestamp.

Input `mask` — Optional static-background selection for tracking.

Input `region` — Optional static-background region in the raw image grid.

Input `reset` — Boolean pulse discarding buffered history before accepting this input.

Input `flush` — Boolean pulse emitting the tail. Finite playback flushes automatically.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `radius_frames` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 63. Look-ahead in frames. Retain at most 2 × radius + 1 source frames. |
| `sigma_frames` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 63. Standard deviation of the centered Gaussian weights, in frames. |
| `motion_model` | `"similarity"` | `translation`, `similarity` Translation shifts the view. Similarity also tracks rotation and scale. |
| `max_features` | `240` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 5000. |
| `feature_quality` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1. |
| `feature_spacing` | `8.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 512. |
| `tracking_window` | `21` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 3 and 255. |
| `max_pyramid_level` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 8. |
| `forward_backward_pixels` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 100. Maximum error when a tracked point is mapped back. |
| `max_patch_error` | `30.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 255. Maximum mean patch error on the 8-bit tracking image. |
| `inlier_pixels` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 100. Reprojection tolerance for a track to support the motion estimate. |
| `min_inliers` | `12` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 5000. |
| `min_inlier_ratio` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. |
| `max_step_pixels` | `40.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 16384. Largest accepted center displacement between consecutive frames. |
| `max_step_degrees` | `12.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. Largest accepted rotation between consecutive frames. |
| `max_step_scale_ratio` | `1.15` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 2. Allowed scale range is the reciprocal of this ratio to the ratio. |
| `min_coverage_fraction` | `0.65` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Reject the affected frame, retaining its ticket in Report. |
| `interpolation` | `"linear"` | `nearest`, `linear` |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `128.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 1024. State budget; native work buffers and GUI snapshots use extra memory. |
| `max_gap_ms` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 8.64e+07. Start a new segment after this arrival gap. Zero disables the gap limit. |

## Temporal median background (`sequence.temporal_median`)

Sequence / Temporal processing

Inputs: `frames` (any), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `frames` — Batch of two to 64 aligned frames of a stationary camera view.

Input `mask` — Write the median only inside this mask. Outside uses the earlier middle frame.

Input `region` — Write the median only inside this region. Outside uses the middle frame.

No configurable algorithm parameters.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |

## Track image detections (`sequence.track_image`)

Sequence / Object tracking

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reset` (data, optional), `stream` (data, optional), `detections` (data), `timestamp_ms` (number, optional).

Input `image` — Current image/grid/ticket; its pixels are not modified.

Input `detections` — An explicit empty list means no detections. Lists of center/optional size/angle records, measured collections or pattern matches.

Input `timestamp_ms` — Optional synchronized acquisition time in milliseconds; strict monotonicity within each stream.

Input `mask` — Select detection and output-prediction centers; intersected with region.

Input `region` — Current-grid selection. Excluded detections act as missed observations for existing tracks.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `warmup` | `1` | Minimum eligible activations before primary data outputs are emitted. |
| `max_streams` | `4` |  |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `128.0` | Stored array/metadata budget; intermediates and GUI copies use extra memory. |
| `overflow` | `"error"` | `error`, `evict_oldest` |
| `context_change` | `"reset"` | `reset`, `separate` Reset a source on recipe/grid/settings change, or cache separate contexts. |
| `max_gap_ms` | `0.0` | Reset after this ingress gap; zero keeps state across pauses. |
| `clock` | `"auto"` | `auto`, `explicit`, `media`, `nominal` Auto uses explicit timestamp, then video media time, then nominal intervals; switching clock type retires state. |
| `nominal_interval_ms` | `100.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+06. Deterministic per-activation interval when no acquisition/media clock is used. |
| `minimum_interval_ms` | `0.001` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1000. Reject duplicate/decreasing or smaller time intervals. |
| `maximum_interval_ms` | `1000.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+06. Larger gaps expire all old tracks before consuming current detections. |
| `angle_period` | `"360"` | `360`, `180` 360 for directed poses; 180 for unoriented principal axes. Output angles use [-period/2,period/2). |
| `maximum_tracks` | `64` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 128. Bounded active-track capacity; overflow detections are reported, not silently merged. |
| `minimum_observations` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1000. Cumulative matched detections needed to confirm an identity. |
| `emit_tentative` | `false` | Include unconfirmed tracks in Tracks/Poses. |
| `emit_predictions` | `true` | Include confirmed missing tracks; every item still identifies observed versus predicted. |
| `maximum_missed_frames` | `10` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 10000. Expire after more consecutive missing detections than this count. |
| `maximum_unobserved_ms` | `1000.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+09. Expire after this many media/nominal milliseconds since last observation. |
| `offscreen_margin` | `100.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 100000. Allowed predicted-center distance beyond image bounds before expiry. |
| `minimum_detection_score` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Ignore detections below this score; unscored detections use one. |
| `minimum_confidence` | `0.05` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Output floor for last score times confirmation and elapsed-time decay; not a probability. |
| `confidence_decay_ms` | `1000.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+09. Exponential prediction confidence time constant. |
| `label_policy` | `"if_both"` | `if_both`, `ignore`, `required` Require equal known labels/class IDs, ignore them, or require a label on every detection. |
| `appearance_policy` | `"off"` | `off`, `if_both`, `required` Off preserves motion-only behavior. If both uses available matching feature spaces; required rejects detector records missing appearance. Descriptors do not prove identity. |
| `appearance_weight` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Blend normalized cosine distance with motion cost after both hard gates; no effect when either descriptor is absent. Geometry gates always apply. |
| `maximum_appearance_distance` | `0.3` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 2. Cosine-distance gate [0,2]; zero permits only numerically identical descriptors. |
| `appearance_update_rate` | `0.2` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Normalized exponential update from matched detector evidence; zero freezes an established descriptor. |
| `maximum_appearance_dimensions` | `512` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Bound each external descriptor before normalization and sequence-state allocation. |
| `association_distance` | `50.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+06. Absolute center-distance gate in current pixels, independent of covariance growth. |
| `innovation_gate` | `18.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+06. Maximum squared Mahalanobis innovation over available measurement dimensions; not a probability. |
| `maximum_scale_ratio` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 1000. Per-axis measured/predicted size ratio gate, applied only when size is present. |
| `maximum_angle_difference` | `60.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. Wrapped angular association gate in degrees, applied when orientation is present. |
| `ambiguity_margin` | `0.05` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Normalized cost gap identifying near-tied associations; flagged and confidence halved. |
| `position_noise` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Measurement standard deviation in current pixels. |
| `scale_noise` | `0.05` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 10. Measurement standard deviation in log width/height. |
| `angle_noise` | `5.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Measurement standard deviation in degrees. |
| `position_acceleration` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+06. Process acceleration standard deviation in pixels/second squared. |
| `scale_acceleration` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10. Process acceleration standard deviation in log size/second squared. |
| `angular_acceleration` | `30.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+06. Process acceleration standard deviation in degrees/second squared. |
| `initial_position_sigma` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Initial center uncertainty in pixels. |
| `initial_velocity_sigma` | `100.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Initial center-velocity uncertainty in pixels/second. |
| `initial_scale_sigma` | `0.2` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 10. Initial log-size uncertainty. |
| `initial_scale_velocity_sigma` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 10. Initial log-size velocity uncertainty per second. |
| `initial_angle_sigma` | `20.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Initial angle uncertainty in degrees when measured. |
| `initial_angular_velocity_sigma` | `90.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Initial angular-velocity uncertainty in degrees/second. |
| `initial_width` | `20.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Fallback pixels for center-only measurements; size stays explicitly unavailable. |
| `initial_height` | `20.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Fallback pixels for center-only measurements; size stays explicitly unavailable. |
| `maximum_position_sigma` | `100.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Expire when either predicted center standard deviation exceeds this pixel bound. |
| `maximum_scale_sigma` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 10. Expire measured-size tracks above this log-size uncertainty. |
| `maximum_angle_sigma` | `90.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Expire measured-orientation tracks above this angular uncertainty in degrees. |

## Track object box (MIL) (`sequence.track_mil`)

Sequence / Object tracking

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reset` (data, optional), `stream` (data, optional), `seed` (data, optional).

Input `image` — uint8 gray/RGB/BGR/RGBA/BGRA, up to 2.1M pixels. MIL receives canonical grayscale.

Input `seed` — Optional [x,y,w,h] or {bounds:[...]}, in this image's coordinates, when no model exists.

Input `mask` — Seed support and optional accepted-center gate; not a native MIL training mask.

Input `region` — Rectangle/circle/polygon selection, intersected with Mask; same semantics.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `warmup` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Minimum eligible activations before primary data outputs are emitted. |
| `max_streams` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `128.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 4096. Conservative model/workspace estimate, not a native allocator or hard RSS limit. |
| `overflow` | `"error"` | `error`, `evict_oldest` |
| `context_change` | `"reset"` | `reset`, `separate` Reset a source on recipe/grid/settings change, or cache separate contexts. |
| `max_gap_ms` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 3.6e+06. Reset after this ingress gap; zero keeps state across pauses. |
| `initial_bounds` | `[]` | Initial [x,y,width,height], at least 8×8. Wired Seed takes precedence. |
| `max_misses` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1000. Consecutive missed updates before discarding the model and waiting for a seed. |
| `minimum_contrast` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 255. Minimum grayscale standard deviation inside the box. Zero disables. |
| `maximum_center_jump` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 65536. Maximum motion since the last accepted update. Zero disables. |
| `draw_overlay` | `true` | Enable drawable results and optional raster Overlay; Lean/off skips unrequested graphics. |
| `init_positive_radius` | `3.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 32. Native positive-sampling radius, in local pixels. |
| `init_negative_samples` | `65` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Native cap on negative initialization samples. |
| `search_radius` | `25.0` | Value must be a finite number between 2 and 128. Native local search window radius; larger windows increase work. |
| `track_positive_radius` | `4.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 32. Native tracking sampler control; SDK versions may use init sampling for updates. |
| `track_positive_samples` | `100000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 100000. Native tracking positive-sample cap; SDK policy determines actual use. |
| `track_negative_samples` | `65` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Native tracking negative-sample cap; SDK policy determines actual use. |
| `features` | `250` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1024. Native Haar feature count. MIL returns no probability/confidence through this API. |
| `selection_mode` | `"center"` | `center`, `seed_only` Mask/region bounds seed the model; Center also gates accepted centers. |
| `maximum_work` | `4000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 6.4e+07. Conservative feature/candidate product bound before native initialization. |

| `minimum_interval_ms` | `0.001` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1000. Reject duplicate/decreasing or smaller time intervals. |
| `maximum_interval_ms` | `1000.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+06. Larger gaps expire all old tracks before consuming current detections. |
| `angle_period` | `"360"` | `360`, `180` 360 for directed poses; 180 for unoriented principal axes. Output angles use [-period/2,period/2). |
| `maximum_tracks` | `64` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 128. Bounded active-track capacity; overflow detections are reported, not silently merged. |
| `minimum_observations` | `2` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1000. Cumulative matched detections needed to confirm an identity. |
| `emit_tentative` | `false` | Include unconfirmed tracks in Tracks/Poses. |
| `emit_predictions` | `true` | Include confirmed missing tracks; every item still identifies observed versus predicted. |
| `maximum_missed_frames` | `10` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 10000. Expire after more consecutive missing detections than this count. |
| `maximum_unobserved_ms` | `1000.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+09. Expire after this many media/nominal milliseconds since last observation. |
| `offscreen_margin` | `100.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 100000. Allowed predicted-center distance beyond image bounds before expiry. |
| `minimum_detection_score` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Ignore detections below this score; unscored detections use one. |
| `minimum_confidence` | `0.05` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Output floor for last score times confirmation and elapsed-time decay; not a probability. |
| `confidence_decay_ms` | `1000.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+09. Exponential prediction confidence time constant. |
| `label_policy` | `"if_both"` | `if_both`, `ignore`, `required` Require equal known labels/class IDs, ignore them, or require a label on every detection. |
| `appearance_policy` | `"off"` | `off`, `if_both`, `required` Off preserves motion-only behavior. If both uses available matching feature spaces; required rejects detector records missing appearance. Descriptors do not prove identity. |
| `appearance_weight` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Blend normalized cosine distance with motion cost after both hard gates; no effect when either descriptor is absent. Geometry gates always apply. |
| `maximum_appearance_distance` | `0.3` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 2. Cosine-distance gate [0,2]; zero permits only numerically identical descriptors. |
| `appearance_update_rate` | `0.2` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Normalized exponential update from matched detector evidence; zero freezes an established descriptor. |
| `maximum_appearance_dimensions` | `512` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 4096. Bound each external descriptor before normalization and sequence-state allocation. |
| `association_distance` | `50.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+06. Absolute center-distance gate in current pixels, independent of covariance growth. |
| `innovation_gate` | `18.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+06. Maximum squared Mahalanobis innovation over available measurement dimensions; not a probability. |
| `maximum_scale_ratio` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 1000. Per-axis measured/predicted size ratio gate, applied only when size is present. |
| `maximum_angle_difference` | `60.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. Wrapped angular association gate in degrees, applied when orientation is present. |
| `ambiguity_margin` | `0.05` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Normalized cost gap identifying near-tied associations; flagged and confidence halved. |
| `position_noise` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Measurement standard deviation in current pixels. |
| `scale_noise` | `0.05` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 10. Measurement standard deviation in log width/height. |
| `angle_noise` | `5.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Measurement standard deviation in degrees. |
| `position_acceleration` | `10.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+06. Process acceleration standard deviation in pixels/second squared. |
| `scale_acceleration` | `0.1` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 10. Process acceleration standard deviation in log size/second squared. |
| `angular_acceleration` | `30.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+06. Process acceleration standard deviation in degrees/second squared. |
| `initial_position_sigma` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Initial center uncertainty in pixels. |
| `initial_velocity_sigma` | `100.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Initial center-velocity uncertainty in pixels/second. |
| `initial_scale_sigma` | `0.2` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 10. Initial log-size uncertainty. |
| `initial_scale_velocity_sigma` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 10. Initial log-size velocity uncertainty per second. |
| `initial_angle_sigma` | `20.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Initial angle uncertainty in degrees when measured. |
| `initial_angular_velocity_sigma` | `90.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Initial angular-velocity uncertainty in degrees/second. |
| `initial_width` | `20.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Fallback pixels for center-only measurements; size stays explicitly unavailable. |
| `initial_height` | `20.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Fallback pixels for center-only measurements; size stays explicitly unavailable. |
| `maximum_position_sigma` | `100.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Expire when either predicted center standard deviation exceeds this pixel bound. |
| `maximum_scale_sigma` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 10. Expire measured-size tracks above this log-size uncertainty. |
| `maximum_angle_sigma` | `90.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1e+06. Expire measured-orientation tracks above this angular uncertainty in degrees. |
| `maximum_detector_age_frames` | `15` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Expire beyond this many acquisitions since a full detector match, even if local motion keeps succeeding. |
| `maximum_detector_age_ms` | `2000.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 1e+09. Expire beyond this acquisition/media age since a full detector match, regardless of local success. |
| `local_max_points` | `48` | Value must be a whole number between 4 and 256. Maximum ROI corners per track, at most 4096 across all tracks. |
| `local_quality` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1. goodFeaturesToTrack relative corner strength. |
| `local_min_distance` | `3.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 100. Minimum separation of ROI corners in pixels. |
| `local_block_size` | `5` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 3 and 63. Odd corner covariance neighborhood. |
| `local_gradient_size` | `"3"` | `3`, `5`, `7` Corner gradient aperture (dropdown). |
| `local_harris` | `false` | Use Harris corner scoring instead of minimum eigenvalues. |
| `local_harris_k` | `0.04` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Harris free parameter when enabled. |
| `local_window` | `21` | Value must be a positive odd integer between 3 and 63. Odd Lucas-Kanade patch width and height. |
| `local_levels` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 5. Maximum LK pyramid level; zero uses original resolution only. |
| `local_iterations` | `30` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 200. LK termination iteration ceiling. |
| `local_epsilon` | `0.01` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-06 and 1. LK termination displacement epsilon in pixels. |
| `local_min_eigenvalue` | `0.0001` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. LK minimum normalized corner eigenvalue. |
| `local_max_error` | `20.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 255. Maximum forward native patch error; used only in patch error mode. |
| `local_error_metric` | `"patch"` | `patch`, `min_eigenvalue` Native LK patch error or minimum eigenvalue; neither is confidence. |
| `fit_method` | `"ransac"` | `ransac`, `lmeds` Robust similarity estimation: RANSAC or LMedS (dropdown). |
| `local_fb_tolerance` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 100. Maximum round-trip pixel error; zero disables backward checking. |
| `local_min_points` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 4 and 256. Minimum supported points for local similarity fitting. |
| `local_min_inliers` | `0.6` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 1. Required retained-inlier fraction of the original ROI points. |
| `local_min_span` | `3.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 100. Minimum point span on both axes before and after robust fitting. |
| `local_max_shift` | `30.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.1 and 1000. Maximum local center displacement per acquisition in pixels. |
| `local_max_scale` | `1.2` | Value must be a finite number between 1 and 4. Maximum per-frame scale ratio (reciprocal sets the minimum). |
| `local_max_angle` | `20.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 180. Maximum per-frame local rotation in degrees. |
| `local_work_limit` | `32000000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1.28e+08. Conservative point times window area times level bound; not elapsed time or native RSS. |
| `fit_threshold` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 100. RANSAC reprojection and retained residual ceiling in pixels. |
| `fit_iterations` | `200` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Maximum RANSAC iterations. |
| `fit_confidence` | `0.99` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 0.999999. RANSAC confidence control, not track probability. |
| `fit_refine_iterations` | `10` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 100. Native similarity-fit refinement iterations. |

## Track object box (ViT) (`sequence.track_vit`)

Sequence / Object tracking

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional), `reset` (data, optional), `stream` (data, optional), `seed` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Original uint8 gray/RGB/BGR/alpha; native tracker receives canonical BGR.

Input `seed` — Current-grid [x,y,w,h] or {bounds:[...]}, used only when a new model is needed.

Input `mask` — Seed support and accepted-center gate; not a native template mask.

Input `region` — Current-grid rectangle/circle/polygon, intersected with Mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `warmup` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Minimum eligible activations before primary data outputs are emitted. |
| `max_streams` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `384.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.01 and 4096. Conservative native workspace/model allowance; not measured RSS. |
| `overflow` | `"error"` | `error`, `evict_oldest` |
| `context_change` | `"reset"` | `reset`, `separate` Reset a source on recipe/grid/settings change, or cache separate contexts. |
| `max_gap_ms` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 3.6e+06. Reset after this ingress gap; zero keeps state across pauses. |
| `initial_bounds` | `[]` | Initial [x,y,w,h], at least 8x8; wired Seed takes precedence. |
| `max_misses` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 1000. Consecutive missed updates before discarding the model and waiting for a seed. |
| `minimum_contrast` | `2.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 255. Minimum grayscale standard deviation inside the box. Zero disables. |
| `maximum_center_jump` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 65536. Maximum motion since the last accepted update. Zero disables. |
| `draw_overlay` | `true` | Current drawable status/box; raster Overlay only when exposed. |
| `contract_path` | `""` | Project-relative contract.json; uses its bundled, verified ONNX model. |
| `receipt_mode` | `"summary"` | `summary`, `off` Model/settings/import evidence or Off; native tensors are not exposed. |
| `fallback_policy` | `"error"` | `error`, `cpu_if_unavailable` Error by default; CPU fallback only for an unavailable requested target. |
| `model_path` | `""` | Local ViT ONNX, or use a sealed Contract path; no automatic download. |
| `mean` | `[0.485, 0.456, 0.406]` | Native normalized BGR means; native tracker owns template/search preparation. |
| `std` | `[0.229, 0.224, 0.225]` | Three positive native channel divisors; defaults mirror TrackerVit.Params. |
| `tracking_score_threshold` | `0.2` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Window-weighted response threshold; not a probability. |
| `engine` | `"classic"` | `classic`, `new` Explicit import engine; New is CPU-only here. No silent retry. |
| `backend` | `"opencv"` | `opencv`, `cuda`, `default` |
| `target` | `"cpu"` | `cpu`, `opencl`, `opencl_fp16`, `cuda`, `cuda_fp16` |
| `fusion` | `true` |  |
| `selection_mode` | `"center"` | `center`, `seed_only` Selections seed the rectangle; Center also gates accepted updates. |
| `maximum_source_pixels` | `2100000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 2.1e+06. |
| `maximum_patch_side` | `2048` | Value must be a whole number between 32 and 8192. Bound 4*sqrt(box area) search crop before native calls. |

## Video timing monitor (`sequence.video_timing`)

Sequence / Video diagnostics

Inputs: `image` (image), `info` (data, optional), `reference` (data, optional), `reset` (boolean, optional), `stream` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Current image with video_frame/network_frame metadata; pixels remain unchanged.

Input `info` — Optional matching source Info; otherwise reads the image's timing payload.

Input `reference` — Optional list of independent times, overriding Reference timestamps ms.

Input `reset` — Wire paced Cycle start here to keep loop boundaries out of the timing history.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `warmup` | `1` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Minimum eligible activations before primary data outputs are emitted. |
| `max_streams` | `4` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 64. |
| `max_bytes_mb` | `128.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0.001 and 4096. Stored array/metadata budget; intermediates and GUI copies use extra memory. |
| `overflow` | `"error"` | `error`, `evict_oldest` |
| `context_change` | `"reset"` | `reset`, `separate` Reset a source on recipe/grid/settings change, or cache separate contexts. |
| `max_gap_ms` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+12. Reset after this ingress gap; zero keeps state across pauses. |
| `history_size` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 3 and 4096. Bounded row/plot window. Session counters survive row eviction. |
| `timestamp_policy` | `"decoder_only"` | `decoder_only`, `allow_estimates` Require decoder properties, or explicitly allow labeled estimates. |
| `cadence_policy` | `"allow_variable"` | `allow_variable`, `require_constant`, `match_nominal_fps` Allow VFR, require constant cadence, or match the reported FPS. |
| `absolute_tolerance_ms` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+09. Cadence tolerance floor in media milliseconds per decoded frame. |
| `relative_tolerance_percent` | `5.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 100. Additional relative cadence tolerance; not a drop detector. |
| `maximum_interval_ms` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+12. Reject larger selected-frame media gaps. Zero disables this check. |
| `latch_failures` | `true` | Keep stream failures until Reset or a context reset. |
| `reference_timestamps_ms` | `[]` | Independent full-clip times indexed by source frame index. |
| `reference_tolerance_ms` | `1.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+12. Maximum absolute difference from the time reference. |

## Advanced global threshold (`skimage.global_threshold`)

Image processing / Segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Gray or RGB/BGR(A), uint8/uint16/int16/float32/float64. Raw units are retained; color uses weighted luminance without rescaling. Up to 2.1M pixels.

Input `mask` — Global/Multi-Otsu learn from selected pixels; local neighborhoods use the full image. All masks are zero outside selection; Multi-Otsu labels use -1 there.

Input `region` — Rectangle/circle/polygon selection, intersected with a wired mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `method` | `"yen"` | `otsu`, `yen`, `li`, `isodata`, `minimum`, `triangle`, `mean` Histogram or raw-sample global estimator. Algorithm failures are reported; there is no silent fallback to another method. |
| `polarity` | `"bright"` | `bright`, `dark` Bright selects image > threshold; dark selects image <= threshold. |
| `bins` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 8 and 1024. Bounded histogram bins for Otsu, Yen, Isodata, Minimum, Triangle. Exact integer levels when the range fits. Mean and Li use the selected raw samples. |
| `maximum_iterations` | `1000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Minimum smoothing iterations or Li threshold updates; reaching the limit raises a block error instead of publishing an unconverged result. |
| `li_tolerance` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1e+12. Li convergence tolerance in raw intensity units; 0 uses native automatic. |
| `li_initial` | `"mean"` | `mean`, `median`, `quantile`, `value` Li start: sample mean, median, quantile or an explicit intensity. |
| `li_initial_value` | `127.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+12 and 1e+12. Explicit Li starting intensity, strictly inside the sample range. |
| `li_quantile` | `0.5` | Value must be a finite number between 0 and 1. Quantile (0..1) for the Li starting estimate. |
| `isodata_threshold` | `"lowest"` | `lowest`, `middle`, `highest` Select lowest, middle or highest of all valid Isodata cutoffs. All candidates remain in statistics. |

## Advanced local threshold (`skimage.local_threshold`)

Image processing / Segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Gray or RGB/BGR(A), uint8/uint16/int16/float32/float64. Raw units are retained; color uses weighted luminance without rescaling. Up to 2.1M pixels.

Input `mask` — Global/Multi-Otsu learn from selected pixels; local neighborhoods use the full image. All masks are zero outside selection; Multi-Otsu labels use -1 there.

Input `region` — Rectangle/circle/polygon selection, intersected with a wired mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `method` | `"sauvola"` | `gaussian`, `mean`, `median`, `niblack`, `sauvola` Local Gaussian/mean/median, or native scikit-image Niblack/Sauvola. Generic Python neighborhood callables belong in user blocks. |
| `polarity` | `"bright"` | `bright`, `dark` Bright selects image > local threshold; dark uses <=. |
| `window_yx` | `[31, 31]` | Odd [height, width] from 1 to 255 pixels. Median has a bounded pixel-window work limit. Neighborhoods always use the full input image. |
| `offset` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+12 and 1e+12. Subtract from the computed threshold, in original intensity units. |
| `border` | `"reflect"` | `reflect`, `constant`, `nearest`, `mirror`, `wrap` Border mode for Gaussian/mean/median only; Niblack/Sauvola use native reflect. |
| `border_value` | `0.0` | Value must be a finite number between -1e+12 and 1e+12. Constant border intensity for Gaussian/mean/median. |
| `sigma_yx` | `[0.0, 0.0]` | Gaussian [row, column] sigmas, or [0, 0] for native window-derived values. |
| `k` | `0.2` | Value must be a finite number between -10 and 10. Niblack subtracts k times local deviation (OpenCV's sign convention differs). Sauvola uses mean * (1 + k * (std/range - 1)). |
| `sauvola_range` | `128.0` | Value must be a finite number between 1e-12 and 1e+12. Positive standard-deviation reference in original units; 128 for ordinary uint8, often 0.5 for normalized [0,1] images. No implicit rescaling. |

## Multi-Otsu threshold (`skimage.multi_otsu`)

Image processing / Segmentation

Inputs: `image` (image), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `image` — Gray or RGB/BGR(A), uint8/uint16/int16/float32/float64. Raw units are retained; color uses weighted luminance without rescaling. Up to 2.1M pixels.

Input `mask` — Global/Multi-Otsu learn from selected pixels; local neighborhoods use the full image. All masks are zero outside selection; Multi-Otsu labels use -1 there.

Input `region` — Rectangle/circle/polygon selection, intersected with a wired mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `classes` | `3` | Value must be a whole number between 2 and 8. Number of ordered intensity classes (0 is darkest). Work is limited to 2 million candidate bin partitions; reduce bins for larger class counts. |
| `bins` | `256` | Value must be a whole number between 8 and 1024. Histogram bins, including for uint16. Integer levels are exact if their range fits; otherwise cutoffs are bin centers in original units. More bins costs more work. |
| `selected_classes` | `[1, 2]` | Class IDs combined into the binary mask; an empty list selects none. Labels retain all classes, with -1 outside scope. Equality joins the upper class. |

## Region properties (scikit-image) (`skimage.region_properties`)

Inspection / Measurements

Inputs: `labels` (image), `intensity_image` (image, optional), `mask` (image, optional), `region` (data, optional).

Input `labels` — Integer label plane (up to 2.1M pixels); binary mode uses nonzero pixels.

Input `intensity_image` — Optional same-grid gray/RGB/BGR image, retained in raw sample units.

Input `mask` — Clip measured pixels. Reuse this scope when reconstructing masks from labels.

Input `region` — Optional rectangle, circle or polygon, intersected with a wired mask.

| Parameter | Default | Enum choices / help |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `input_mode` | `"labels"` | `labels`, `binary` Measure each label ID, or label connected nonzero binary pixels first. |
| `connectivity` | `8` | `4`, `8` 4 or 8 neighbors for binary labeling; existing label IDs are not split. |
| `ignored_labels` | `[-1, 0]` | IDs excluded after selection. Remove 0 to measure a class-zero region. |
| `property_set` | `"standard"` | `standard`, `comprehensive`, `custom` Standard measurements, all numeric/moment/tensor properties, or custom. |
| `properties` | `[]` | Custom names from the documented regionprops catalog. Extra Python callables belong in user blocks; this field does not execute code. |
| `include_coordinates` | `false` | Also return coords and coords_scaled; may be large. |
| `include_images` | `false` | Return cropped binary/convex/filled/intensity arrays as data. |
| `spacing_yx` | `[1.0, 1.0]` | Positive [row, column] spacing for native properties. Collection geometry and ordinary metrics stay in image pixels. Units are descriptive, not a calibration fit. |
| `offset_yx` | `[0, 0]` | Native regionprops [row, column] origin offset. Does not move GUI graphics. |
| `units` | `"pixels"` | Label for native property units; choose consistently with spacing. |
| `include_alpha` | `false` | Include alpha in intensity channels; default excludes it. |
| `intensity_alignment` | `"same_frame"` | `same_frame`, `pixel_grid` Same frame checks known grids and tickets; pixel grid explicitly accepts separately captured/exported aligned intensity data. |
| `cache` | `true` | Cache native intermediate properties during this invocation only. |
| `unavailable` | `"report"` | `report`, `error` Report null/reason for missing intensity, nonfinite or unsupported properties (e.g. anisotropic perimeters), or fail the block. |
| `maximum_regions` | `1000` | Value must be a whole number between 1 and 10000. Maximum distinct measured label IDs, even for sparse large IDs. |
| `maximum_values` | `1000000` | Value must be a whole number between 100 and 2e+06. Total property-value budget, including opted-in coordinates/images. |
| `preview_count` | `25` | Value must be a whole number between 0 and 250. Maximum boxes/centroids to draw. Zero disables these optional graphics. |
